## Long-term Knowledge

### Action

<!-- lore:019fb9ae-845b-729a-909d-ef2d0bbde2c5 -->
* **Deslop fix/verify compound hotkey CGEvent suppression**: Steps: 1. Apply minimal patch to macos-key-listener.swift: emit KEY\_DOWN:\<name> in tap callback before returning nil for suppressed hotkey events. 2. Rebuild native binary (swiftc) and TypeScript typecheck. 3. Verify full build passes (turbo build). 4. Test compound hotkey (Option+Space or similar) — dictation should activate. 5. Confirm no duplicate KEY\_DOWN events for normal typing. 6. Open PR #396 and link to issue. Gotchas: - Do NOT change KEY\_UP handling. - Globe/Fn and modifier-only hotkeys use different code path and are unaffected. - Ensure no other tap/monitor ordering assumptions break.

### Animation

<!-- lore:019fb971-5553-77e2-b874-45c7f1575d68 -->
* **Pill exit animation: shrink + fade without text**: After transcription completes, the pill animates via DOM ref (no React state change): 1. Capture \`pillRef.current\` 2. Animate \`opacity: 1 → 0\` over 200ms 3. Animate \`min-width: 32px → 0\`, \`padding: 0 10px → 0\`, \`gap: 3 → 0\` over 300ms 4. On \`onExited\`, call \`hidePill()\` to hide the Electron window 5. No "done" text, no "pasted" state, no 1200ms delay Key fixes: - Removed \`hidePill()\` from main-process \`paste:text\` handler so window stays open - Removed always-mounted orb \`width: 0\` hack; restored conditional rendering - Removed \`style.cssText = ""\` resets that wiped React inline styles - Removed unused \`Check\` icon, \`message\` state, \`done\` state, \`doneTimerRef\` - Animation uses \`requestAnimationFrame\` for smooth 60fps shrink/fade Files changed: \`apps/electron/src/renderer/src/pages/app.tsx\`

<!-- lore:019fb96f-4135-7d7d-bcb3-875a939dc166 -->
* **Pill initializing animation on first hotkey press**: When the hotkey is first pressed, the pill transitions to an 'initializing' state (pulsing glow) while waiting for mic access and streamer setup. This replaces the previous behavior where the pill would just show a static spinner or no feedback. The animation uses a CSS keyframe (\`pulse-glow\`) and is shown only on the first press of a session; subsequent rapid presses show the normal recording state. The initializing state is also reflected in the accessible label and the \`aria-busy\` attribute.

### Architecture

<!-- lore:019fafba-bbae-78aa-8679-b0f830f3c774 -->
* **Account disconnect: Better Auth unlinkAccount() chain**: Account disconnect feature uses Better Auth's built-in \`authClient.unlinkAccount({providerId})\` — prevents unlinking the last auth method by default (avoids lockout) unless server sets \`allowUnlinkingAll:true\`; no extra server-side wiring needed for the base capability. Desktop chain: packages/validations/src/auth.ts unlinkAccountSchema → apps/server/src/lib/freestyle-cloud.ts unlinkCloudAccount() (cloudJson POST /auth/unlink-account) → apps/server/src/routes/auth.ts POST /api/auth/unlink-account → use-profile.ts useUnlinkSocial() mutation (invalidates cloud-accounts query) → profile.tsx 'Disconnect' button, disabled+tooltip when it's the user's only sign-in method. Note: Better Auth's account table stores provider tokens encrypted at rest (decrypted on authClient.listAccounts()); linking a provider never overwrites the user's email/emailVerified, so linking cannot rebind account identity.

<!-- lore:019faf56-de93-7445-bf12-3e7fde8eb741 -->
* **Account unlink feature: better-auth unlinkAccount routed through local proxy**: Account-disconnect added across: packages/validations/src/auth.ts (\`unlinkAccountSchema\`), apps/server/src/lib/freestyle-cloud.ts (\`unlinkCloudAccount()\` → POST cloud \`/auth/unlink-account\` via \`cloudJson()\`), apps/server/src/routes/auth.ts (\`POST /unlink-account\`), apps/electron renderer use-profile.ts (\`useUnlinkSocial()\` mutation, invalidates accounts query on success), profile.tsx (Disconnect ghost button, disabled with tooltip when it's the user's only sign-in method). Better Auth's \`authClient.unlinkAccount({providerId})\` prevents unlinking the last linked account by default (\`allowUnlinkingAll\` opts out) — that guard is cloud-side, but the client still must disable the button locally so users don't hit a failed request with no explanation.

<!-- lore:019fba10-6454-7f13-af78-cf2c5d0518a6 -->
* **api.ts getClient() is primary local API path; apiFetch() reserved for non-typeable requests**: apps/electron/src/renderer/src/lib/api.ts centralizes local embedded server HTTP. getApiBase() resolves configured serverUrl (remote) or getLocalApiBase() (http://127.0.0.1:\<port>, default 4649). getClient() returns a typed Hono RPC client (hc\<AppType>) and is the primary path for all API calls — always prefer it. apiFetch(path, init) is a raw-fetch wrapper intentionally reserved only for requests the typed client can't express (binary WAV upload in app.tsx, fire-and-forget beacons in lib/report-error.ts and lib/analytics.ts) — it additively injects bearer auth headers without clobbering caller-set headers. refreshApiBase() re-reads serverUrl/token via IPC and re-verifies reachability (checkServerHealth/checkServerAuth) after the server:changed broadcast.

<!-- lore:019fb9e6-a617-7c7d-b9fb-04c6f07c442c -->
* **api.ts: getApiBase/apiFetch/getClient local server fetch layering**: apps/electron/src/renderer/src/lib/api.ts is the renderer's local-server fetch layer (no \`endpoint.ts\` exists anywhere in the repo). \`getApiBase()\` resolves configured serverUrl or \`getLocalApiBase()\` (http://127.0.0.1:4649). \`getClient()\` (typed Hono RPC via \`hc\<AppType>\`) is the primary path for nearly all calls. \`apiFetch()\` is intentionally narrow — only for requests the typed client can't express (binary WAV upload, fire-and-forget beacons like telemetry/client-error); it additively injects bearer auth headers without clobbering caller-set headers. \`refreshApiBase()\` re-reads serverUrl/token via IPC and re-verifies reachability; called after \`server:changed\` events.

<!-- lore:019fcbf5-37bc-724c-a261-be4063ee82fc -->

Both must be considered together: \`showCard = showErrorCard || showRemixCard\`, \`active\` includes \`showRemixCard\`, capsule element carries both \`pill-surface\` and \`pill-capsule\` classes. Enter animation uses remix's blur/rise/overshoot (\`.pill-surface\`); exit animation uses main's \`.pill-capsule\[data-exit]\` system (chosen per user: 'remix's summon, keep main's exit'). Any future pill work must account for both systems on the same DOM elements.

Separate \`dev:docs\` script: \`turbo dev --filter=docs\`. \`.github/workflows/build.yml\` bypass pattern includes \`apps/docs/\*\`. DISTINCT from root \`specs/\` folder (internal engineering specs/audits, renamed from \`docs/\` in commit \`b4b8d13\`). \`AGENTS.md\` documents this distinction; \`CLAUDE.md\` at repo root is a pointer/reference file to \`AGENTS.md\`.

<!-- lore:019fb9e7-97d1-7fd6-b624-df2ec25ba444 -->
* **apps/electron api.ts: apiFetch() vs getClient() for local server calls**: Local server fetch layer lives in apps/electron/src/renderer/src/lib/api.ts (no file named endpoint.ts exists anywhere in repo). getApiBase() resolves configured serverUrl or falls back to getLocalApiBase() (http://127.0.0.1:\<port>, default 4649). getClient() returns a typed Hono RPC client (hc\<AppType>) and is the PRIMARY path for local API calls. apiFetch(path, init) is a raw-fetch wrapper intentionally reserved only for requests the typed client can't express (binary WAV upload, fire-and-forget beacons like /api/client-error, /api/telemetry) — it adds headers additively, never clobbering caller-set headers. refreshApiBase() re-reads serverUrl/token via IPC after the \`server:changed\` broadcast and re-verifies reachability via checkServerHealth().

<!-- lore:019fb9fb-b564-7107-ae33-a2b2c1bed934 -->
* **apps/electron api.ts: local server fetch helper structure**: Local-server equivalent of cloud's freestyleCloudUrl()/cloudJson() lives in apps/electron/src/renderer/src/lib/api.ts (129 lines), not a file named endpoint.ts (none exists in repo). Key exports: getLocalApiBase() (http://127.0.0.1:\<port>, default 4649), getApiBase() (configured serverUrl || local), getServerToken(), isRemoteServer(), apiFetch(path, init) (raw fetch wrapper, additive headers, JSDoc says it's ONLY for requests the typed client can't express — binary WAV upload, fire-and-forget beacons), checkServerHealth()/checkServerAuth(), refreshApiBase() (re-reads serverUrl/token via IPC after \`server:changed\`, verifies reachability), and getClient() (typed Hono RPC client via \`hc\<AppType>\` — the primary path for everything else). apiFetch consumers: app.tsx (/api/transcribe), report-error.ts, analytics.ts. When adding new server calls from the renderer, prefer getClient() over apiFetch unless it's binary/fire-and-forget.

<!-- lore:019ef818-39a8-7346-bc15-eb09a03bbc2e -->
* **apps/electron env file loading: only .env.local is loaded, .env.example is onboarding template**: \`apps/electron/src/main/index.ts:31-38\` dev-only block calls \`proc.loadEnvFile?.('.env.local')\` — loads ONLY \`.env.local\`, never \`.env\` or \`.env.example\`. \`.gitignore\` rules (lines 60-64): \`.env\`, \`.env.local\`, \`.env.\*.local\` are ignored; \`!.env.example\` is explicitly whitelisted. \`.env.example\` is tracked in git, documented in \`CONTRIBUTING.md:105/115\` as the onboarding template (copy → \`.env.local\`). Do NOT remove \`.env.example\`. Any stray \`apps/electron/.env\` file is gitignored and never loaded — likely created by mistake instead of \`.env.local\`.

<!-- lore:019fba19-04ed-7671-95ca-e6da1e5835ad -->
* **apps/mobile cloud fetch lacks centralized wrapper (no cloudJson equivalent)**: apps/mobile/src/lib/cloud/\*.ts (config.ts, preferences.ts, usage.ts, cloud-config.ts, profile.ts) is a separate, parallel cloud-fetch layer from apps/server's freestyle-cloud.ts. Mobile has its own URL resolver \`cloudUrl()\` (config.ts:19, mirrors \`freestyleCloudUrl()\`, same DEFAULT\_CLOUD\_URL fallback) but NO equivalent to the server's private \`cloudJson()\` wrapper \[\[019fb9e6-a611-7c15-97d5-ec1bf4dbed15]] — every mobile cloud call is a raw \`fetch()\` with no shared retry/timeout/error-classification logic. If adding new mobile cloud calls, don't assume centralization exists; either follow the raw-fetch pattern already used or introduce a shared wrapper deliberately (not yet done as of this investigation).

<!-- lore:019fb9e7-9808-730c-9b36-8dfd41dd361e -->
* **apps/mobile cloud fetch layer is separate from server, has no cloudJson equivalent**: apps/mobile/src/lib/cloud/\* mirrors apps/server/src/lib/freestyle-cloud.ts conceptually — cloudUrl() (apps/mobile/src/lib/cloud/config.ts:19) is mobile's equivalent of freestyleCloudUrl(), same DEFAULT\_CLOUD\_URL fallback ('https://service.freestylevoice.com') — but there is NO shared fetch wrapper analogous to server's cloudJson() \[\[019fb9e6-a611-7c15-97d5-ec1bf4dbed15]]. Each mobile file (preferences.ts, usage.ts, cloud-config.ts, profile.ts) calls raw fetch() directly against \`${cloudUrl()}/...\`. This means cloudJson's retry-on-stale-socket and error classification (401→auth error, 429→usage error w/ resetsAt) are NOT applied on mobile. If adding/debugging mobile cloud calls, don't assume server-side hardening exists — it doesn't.

<!-- lore:019f2224-433f-78f6-bfca-64ea017a7d75 -->
* **apps/server HTTP API: 55 endpoints across 17 route files, 15 MCP tools**: Full API surface: 55 HTTP endpoints + WebSocket upgrade + MCP catch-all, 15 MCP tools, 17 route files in \`apps/server/src/routes/\`. Key domains: \`/api/settings\`, \`/api/keys\`, \`/api/auth\`, \`/api/models\`, \`/api/history\`, \`/api/transcribe\`, \`/api/formats\`, \`/api/dictionary\`, \`/api/vocabulary\`, \`/api/plugins\`, \`/api/health\` (always auth-exempt). \`GET /\` returns \`"Freestyle API"\`. \`POST /api/telemetry\` relays renderer events. \`POST /api/client-error\` logs renderer crashes. MCP mounted at \`/mcp\`, stream at \`/stream\`. Full inventory documented in \`apps/docs/api-reference.mdx\`.

<!-- lore:019f6a3c-4c57-7bb8-b8a5-fcd40f7edeb8 -->
* **apps/server index.ts: TIMEOUT\_PREFIXES, pluginMiddlewareDispatcher, and request pipeline**: \`REQUEST\_TIMEOUT\_MS = 30\_000\`. \`TIMEOUT\_PREFIXES\` = 8 prefixes: \`/api/settings\`, \`/api/keys\`, \`/api/dictionary\`, \`/api/vocabulary\`, \`/api/history\`, \`/api/models\`, \`/api/plugins\`, \`/api/usage\`. Transcription, post-process, model downloads, and auth device-flow poll are intentionally excluded. \`pluginMiddlewareDispatcher\` (line 67-78): reads live registry on every request via \`plugins().collectMiddleware()\` — runtime \`reloadServerPlugins()\` takes effect immediately without reconstructing the app. Middleware stack: \`trustedOriginMiddleware\` → \`pluginApiGuard\` → \`cors()\` → \`requestId()\` → \`logger\` → per-prefix \`timeout()\` → \`pluginMiddlewareDispatcher\` → routes.

<!-- lore:019ef818-39a6-7919-b8ee-1d465f3e7bb6 -->
* **apps/server uses process.env (not c.env) — it is a Node/Hono app, not a Cloudflare Worker**: \`apps/server\` is a Node-based Hono app embedded in Electron (port 4649). All config is via \`process.env\`. \`c.env\` (Hono's Cloudflare Workers bindings pattern) appears zero times — it belongs to the separate \`cloud\` repo Worker only. Trap: Hono is used in both places, so \`c.env\` looks applicable. Fix: never use \`c.env\` in \`apps/server\`; always use \`process.env\`. Entry point: \`apps/server/src/index.ts\`; default export is \`createApp\` factory (NOT an app instance). \`startServer()\` initializes plugins then calls \`createApp(pluginMiddleware)\`. Env vars: PORT, HOST, FREESTYLE\_AUTH\_TOKEN, FREESTYLE\_DB\_PATH, FREESTYLE\_ENV, FREESTYLE\_ANALYTICS\_DEV, DO\_NOT\_TRACK, FREESTYLE\_MLX\_ASR\_\*, HF\_HOME, FREESTYLE\_CLOUD\_URL.

<!-- lore:019f5b15-7bd7-76e7-a6d0-2d4e924772bf -->
* **ASR server shutdown: SIGTERM+SIGKILL fallback, process.once('exit') best-effort, both awaited async**: Whisper server (apps/server/src/lib/whisper/server.ts): stopServer() sends SIGTERM, then SIGKILL after 5000ms. process.once('exit') calls stopServerOnExit() (best-effort). scheduleUnload() skips if activeUses>0 or startPromise pending. MLX-ASR server (apps/server/src/lib/mlx-asr/server.ts): graceful shutdown (lines 551–563) writes JSON {type:'shutdown'} to proc.stdin, calls proc.stdin.end(), then proc.kill(SIGTERM). SIGKILL fallback at lines 309 and 539. process.once('exit') calls stopWorkerOnExit() (lines 60–73) — sends shutdown JSON + SIGTERM, both try/catch swallowed (best-effort only). Neither stop is synchronously awaited on quit — both are best-effort fire-and-forget from the exit handler.

<!-- lore:019fc2c4-89f9-7647-99f5-8e975015586f -->
* **Cloud member\_preferences is source of truth (payload ?? stored ?? default)**: Freestyle Cloud's v2 transcribe/post-process routes AND the streaming Durable Object (stt-session.ts) all read the stored \`member\_preferences\` row (per member, via resolveUsageOwnerId) before processing, then merge via \`resolvePreferences()\`: payload field wins when present, else stored value, else built-in default. Applies to intensity, customPrompt, tones, appAssignments, languages, vocabulary. \`systemFragments\`, \`appContext\`, \`skipPostProcess\` are NEVER stored — always payload-only, no stored fallback. This overturns older lore claiming 'v2 is payload-only, no DB read' — that was true only before the Jul 30 2026 cloud change making member\_preferences authoritative. Fails open (null prefs) to built-in defaults, not an error.

<!-- lore:019faf89-ebff-7924-9ae2-03cf0c2a4528 -->
* **Cloud OAuth token confined to apps/server SQLite sessions table**: Two distinct token systems: (1) Cloud Session Token (OAuth access\_token/refresh\_token) — stored in SQLite \`sessions\` table via apps/server/src/lib/sessions.ts, managed entirely by the embedded server process, NEVER serialized into any HTTP response or IPC channel to the renderer. GET /api/auth/status returns only \`{authenticated, user}\` (id/email/name/image) — getSessionUser() strips token fields. (2) Server bearer Token — stored in settings.json, deliberately exposed to renderer via \`server:token\` IPC (see \[\[019f6be5-ccf9-7839-94c0-4ba8ecc3693e]]). This asymmetry is intentional security-by-design: the cloud OAuth token would let a compromised plugin/XSS in the renderer exfiltrate the user's cloud identity, so all cloud calls (auth, usage, billing, org, transcription) are proxied server-side via cloudJson()/better-auth client with Authorization: Bearer injected from SQLite. No cookies are used anywhere — disableDefaultFetchPlugins:true on the better-auth client.

<!-- lore:019faf91-ce07-7e39-a8f5-788aaef1692b -->
* **Cloud OAuth token never exposed to renderer — server-side SQLite sessions**: Freestyle Cloud OAuth token (access\_token+refresh\_token) is stored server-side in apps/server's SQLite \`sessions\` table (single row, id=1) and NEVER included in any HTTP response to the renderer. Renderer only gets \`{authenticated, user:{id,email,name,image}}\` via GET /api/auth/status. All cloud calls (sign-out, accounts, link/unlink, usage, billing, org) happen server-side via cloudJson()/better-auth client with the token injected as a Bearer header — renderer never talks to Freestyle Cloud directly. This is deliberate security-by-design: exposing the token to renderer (like \`serverToken\` is) would let a compromised plugin or XSS exfiltrate it. Rejected alternative: expose cloud token via IPC like serverToken and call cloud directly from renderer — rejected for security downgrade, required CORS config on cloud side, and split token-refresh complexity across renderer+server. See also \[\[019f6be5-ccf9-7839-94c0-4ba8ecc3693e]].

<!-- lore:019fc2c7-f09b-775b-a268-04664b9796c3 -->
* **Cloud resolvePreferences merge: payload ?? stored ?? default**: In the Freestyle Cloud repo, \`member\_preferences\` (D1) is the source of truth for cleanup/language/vocabulary prefs. \`utils/preferences.ts\` resolvePreferences() merges per-field: payload value wins if present, else falls back to the stored row, else built-in default. Applies uniformly to /v2/transcribe, /v2/post-process, and the streaming Durable Object (stt-session.ts, read before the Soniox handshake). Fields NEVER stored/no fallback: systemFragments, appContext, skipPostProcess, translate — these must always be sent inline per-request. To explicitly CLEAR a stored field for one request, the client must send the built-in default value; omitting the field means 'use stored value', not 'clear it'. This overturns stale prior lore claiming v2 endpoints are payload-only with no DB read — verify cloud-repo behavior directly rather than trusting old lore.

<!-- lore:019fb9ae-e07d-7fad-8ee2-1c77c149a105 -->
* **Cloud-sync settings schema and conflict resolution**: Schema: allowlist of synced keys — dictionary\_entries, vocabulary\_terms, tone\_preferences (per-destination), cleanup\_tone\_settings, portable\_general\_settings (e.g. locale). Each entry carries a server\_version and client\_version vector-clock style timestamp (last\_modified). Conflict rule: server wins on mismatch, but merge dict/voc by key (add/update/remove) so union semantics are preserved for dictionary+vocabulary. Tombstones for deletes (tombstone record with deleted:true + timestamp) to avoid resurrecting deleted entries. Encryption: values stored as-is in cloud DB; no extra encryption at rest beyond cloud provider.

<!-- lore:019f2e7b-c8d7-796c-acd6-b994c31a9b9d -->
* **cloudJson retry loop: RETRIABLE\_CONNECTION\_CODES, 2 attempts, 150ms delay**: \`cloudJson\` in \`apps/server/src/lib/freestyle-cloud.ts\` retries up to \`CLOUD\_FETCH\_ATTEMPTS=2\` total attempts with \`CLOUD\_RETRY\_DELAY\_MS=150ms\` pause. Retries only on \`isRetriableConnectionError\` (walks \`.cause\` chain; matches codes in \`RETRIABLE\_CONNECTION\_CODES\`: ECONNRESET, ECONNREFUSED, ENOTFOUND, EAI\_AGAIN, EPIPE, UND\_ERR\_CONNECT\_TIMEOUT, UND\_ERR\_SOCKET). Excludes UND\_ERR\_HEADERS\_TIMEOUT, UND\_ERR\_BODY\_TIMEOUT, AbortError, TimeoutError — response-phase errors where the request may already be processing server-side. Stale-socket ECONNRESET is safe to retry for non-idempotent POSTs because the request never reached the server. Generates a fresh \`AbortSignal.timeout(CLOUD\_TRANSCRIBE\_TIMEOUT\_MS)\` per attempt when caller didn't supply a signal. \`network.ts\` keep-alive tuning was explicitly rejected — lowering \`keepAliveMaxTimeout\` adds constant TLS handshake latency to avoid a rare reset.

<!-- lore:019fb9e6-a611-7c15-97d5-ec1bf4dbed15 -->
* **cloudJson() is the private canonical cloud fetch wrapper**: \`cloudJson\<T>()\` (apps/server/src/lib/freestyle-cloud.ts:334-385, not exported) is the central fetch wrapper for Freestyle Cloud calls: builds URL via \`freestyleCloudUrl()\`, attaches bearer token, retries once on stale-socket errors, uses \`AbortSignal.timeout()\`, classifies errors (401→FreestyleCloudAuthError, 429→FreestyleCloudUsageError, other→FreestyleCloudRequestError). ~15 exported \`cloud\*\` functions wrap it. Documented raw-fetch exceptions (still via \`freestyleCloudUrl()\`): \`fetchCloudConfig()\` (unauthenticated /v2/config), \`signOutCloud()\` (best-effort), \`prewarmFreestyleCloudConnection()\` (fire-and-forget TLS prewarm while user speaks), \`plugins.ts\` GET /catalog (unauthenticated plugin registry), \`prompt-config.ts\` cached /v2/config refresh, and the Better Auth device-flow client. Renderer never calls cloud directly — path is renderer → local apps/server → Freestyle Cloud.

<!-- lore:019efae2-439a-75b0-b41c-7066fb1885bd -->
* **Craft independent release pipelines: per-package .craft.yml with tagPrefix**: Monorepo uses separate \`.craft.yml\` per releasable unit for independent versioning. App: \`/.craft.yml\` (no tagPrefix, no npm). SDK: \`packages/sdk/.craft.yml\` (tagPrefix \`freestyle-voice@\`, releaseBranchPrefix \`release-sdk\`). Audio plugin: \`plugins/audio-transcription/.craft.yml\` (tagPrefix \`plugin-audio-transcription@\`, releaseBranchPrefix \`release-plugin-audio-transcription\`). Craft's \`findConfigFile()\` walks CWD upward — running from a package subdirectory finds that package's config first. \`tagPrefix\` on the \`github\` target prevents git tag collisions across packages. All package pipelines use \`versioning: { policy: manual }\` (explicit semver per release). OIDC trusted publishing requires one-time manual npm publish to bootstrap each package before OIDC can be configured.

<!-- lore:019f5822-6465-750d-9f49-08219e748804 -->
* **dashboard.tsx i18n init: async lazy-load with always-mount guarantee**: PR \`10e2212\`: \`apps/electron/src/renderer/src/dashboard.tsx\` lazy-loads i18n locales (drops ~168 KB of always-loaded translations) before React mounts. Pattern: extract render into \`mount(): void\`, then \`void initI18n().catch((err) => { reportError(err, { scope: 'initI18n' }); }).finally(mount)\` — React always mounts even if i18n rejects. Trade-off: adds one sequential dynamic import on cold start (negligible on local disk, no network in Electron). Error reporting via \`reportError\` in \`apps/electron/src/renderer/src/lib/report-error.ts\` — POSTs to \`/api/client-error\` with \`{ message, stack, source: 'renderer', context }\`, keepalive: true, swallows all errors.

<!-- lore:019f240c-997b-7562-b034-67a79eb6e33b -->
* **deliverOutput() empty-text suppression: mode=None or empty text skips paste automatically**: \`deliverOutput()\` in \`apps/electron/src/main/index.ts:999\`: if \`out.mode === OutputMode.None || !out.text?.trim()\` → emits \`OutputDelivered\` with \`mode: OutputMode.None\` and returns immediately — no paste occurs. This means a plugin returning empty string from \`afterTranscribe\` (or a future suppress signal) automatically prevents output delivery with no cross-process coordination needed. \`beforeOutput\` hook runs first; paste errors call \`notifyPasteFailed()\` and emit \`PipelineError\` with \`stage: PipelineStage.Output\`.

<!-- lore:019f5b5e-925d-7589-8bf5-1baf23373385 -->
* **deliverOutput/drainQueue: paste flow, hidePill fires before paste keystrokes**: \`deliverOutput()\` (index.ts:1205) calls \`pasteIntoFocusedApp(text, async () => { hidePill(); await wait(0); })\` — \`hidePill()\` is the \`beforePaste\` callback, so \`hotkeyPressed\` is cleared BEFORE paste keystrokes are dispatched. Renderer \`drainQueue()\` (app.tsx:300–309): if \`\_outputMode === 'clipboard'\` → \`window.api.copyText()\`; else → \`window.api.pasteText()\`, then \`window.api.sendTranscriptionDone()\`. Analytics \`capture('dictation completed', {...})\` fires exactly once per dictation at the convergence of single-chunk and multi-chunk paths (lines 314–322). \`doPasteIntoFocusedApp\` (paste.ts:522): snapshots clipboard → writes text → calls \`beforePaste()\` → dispatches platform paste → restores clipboard.

<!-- lore:019fb997-fe22-7761-bb24-06f2c8723a5a -->
* **dev- prefix plugin folder for local plugin development**: Local plugin development uses a \`dev-\` prefix on the plugin folder name under \`\<userData>/plugins/\` to coexist with any prod-installed version. The link script creates a wrapper \`package.json\` with \`"name": "dev-\<original-npm-name>"\` and symlinks the source \`dist/\` into the dev folder. This keeps prod and dev versions side-by-side, avoids collisions, and lets the Electron \`discoverLocalDir\` scan find the dev copy as a separate \`DiscoveredPlugin\`. No source files in the plugin project are mutated. Confidence: 1.0

<!-- lore:019fceb0-f443-76e3-bee3-2b1e90603fb0 -->
* **Dictation vs remix: separate native key listeners, no unified mode enum**: apps/electron main process runs two independent NativeKeyListener instances: registerHotkey() (dictation, main/index.ts:4235, has globalShortcut fallback) and registerRemixHotkey() (remix, main/index.ts:3999, no fallback). Each has its own pressed flag (hotkeyPressed:379, remixPressed:391) and key handlers (handleNativeHotkeyDown/Up vs handleRemixHotkeyDown/Up). Both feed the same shared pill window (app.tsx) via separate IPC channels (hotkey:down/up vs remix:down/up/selection/supersede). The renderer has no single 'mode' enum: dictation state is state/stateRef+pillActiveRef (app.tsx:565), remix state is remix/remixRef.current (RemixSession|null, app.tsx:481), discriminated by \`remixRef.current !== null\`. On macOS, dictation (Fn) and remix (Fn+Control) share a home key, so one physical press can fire both listeners — the two lanes must coordinate manually, there's no OS-level mutual exclusion.

<!-- lore:019fcea4-c8a5-7399-a365-33e059d286e3 -->
* **Dual native key listeners: dictation vs remix hotkeys share macOS home key**: apps/electron main/index.ts runs two independent NativeKeyListener processes: registerHotkey() (dictation, has globalShortcut fallback) and registerRemixHotkey() (remix, no fallback, :3999). Each has its own pressed flag (hotkeyPressed vs remixPressed) and handlers (handleNativeHotkeyDown/Up vs handleRemixHotkeyDown/Up). On macOS both share a home key (Fn=dictation, Fn+Control=remix) but the two OS-level listener processes 'cannot see each other' (code comment near :3888). IPC to the shared pill window uses separate channels: hotkey:down/up (dictation) vs remix:down/up/selection (remix). Renderer (app.tsx) has NO single 'mode' enum — dictation state lives in state/stateRef + pillActiveRef; remix state lives in remix/remixRef.current (RemixSession|null with phase: capturing|listening|running|chat|error). Any dictation/remix bug must be traced through both lanes independently since neither state machine is aware of the other except via explicit supersede/self-heal hooks.

<!-- lore:019faf56-de7f-7e32-ae49-551dcf49a084 -->
* **Dual-token auth: Cloud Session Token (SQLite) vs Server Token (settings.json)**: Two separate token systems exist. (1) Cloud Session Token — OAuth access/refresh token authenticating with Freestyle Cloud; stored server-side only in SQLite \`sessions\` table (apps/server/src/lib/sessions.ts), managed by the embedded Hono server; NEVER exposed to renderer or serialized into any HTTP response — deliberate security boundary against exfiltration via a compromised plugin or renderer XSS. (2) Server Token — bearer token for the configured Freestyle server (local or remote); stored in settings.json (Electron main process), deliberately exposed to renderer via \`server:token\`/\`server:set-token\` IPC because renderer needs it to call the local API. See \[\[019f6be5-ccf9]] for the SETTINGS\_KEYS storage-key distinction. Plugin views get the server token injected via a session header filter scoped to server origin only (view-manager.ts) so third-party APIs never see it.

<!-- lore:019ee7ed-0eb7-76e6-8a8e-feb96e665987 -->
* **Electron diagnostics: crash handlers, renderer error forwarding, IPC channels**: Main-process crash handlers use \`process.on('uncaughtExceptionMonitor')\` + \`process.on('unhandledRejection')\` (not \`uncaughtException\` — monitor variant avoids changing Node crash behavior). Renderer errors captured via \`window.addEventListener('error'/'unhandledrejection')\` and forwarded through preload IPC (\`logs:renderer-error\`) so main logs them into the same session file. IPC channels: \`logs:renderer-error\`, \`logs:reveal\`, \`logs:export\`, \`logs:get-path\`. Preload exposes \`logRendererError\`, \`revealLogs\`, \`exportLogs\`. Settings → Data panel gets a Diagnostics row with Reveal/Export buttons. \`captureException\` lives in \`apps/server/src/lib/posthog.ts\` and is NOT exported from the package — Electron main cannot import it directly; PostHog reporting from main must go through local HTTP once server is available.

<!-- lore:019f2e7b-c8dc-7c84-b008-b2c2d4051f0c -->
* **electron-updater: feed configured at build time via electron-builder.yml, no runtime auth token**: \`autoUpdater\` (from \`electron-updater\`) is configured at \`apps/electron/src/main/index.ts:1970-1974\`, gated by \`!is.dev\`. Feed URL is NOT set at runtime — it is baked into \`app-update.yml\` at build time via \`apps/electron/electron-builder.yml:72-75\` (\`provider: github\`, \`owner: freestyle-voice\`, \`repo: freestyle\`). No \`setFeedURL\`, \`requestHeaders\`, \`GithubOptions\`, \`allowPrerelease\`, \`channel\`, or auth token is set anywhere in the codebase. electron-updater treats the repo as public and sends no credentials. \`dev-app-update.yml\` is never used — all updater code is gated behind \`!is.dev\`. \`UPDATE\_CHECK\_INTERVAL\_MS = 5 \* 60 \* 1000\`. \`autoUpdater.checkForUpdatesAndNotify()\` at line 2060 had no \`.catch()\` handler (identified as a bug).

<!-- lore:019f2e7c-b33e-7741-b325-58de33fb5d82 -->
* **electron-updater: GitHub provider config, no auth headers, all defaults**: All auto-updater (electron-updater) logic lives in \`apps/electron/src/main/index.ts\` — no dedicated updater file. Feed configured at build time via \`apps/electron/electron-builder.yml:72-75\` (provider: github, owner: freestyle-voice, repo: freestyle). No \`private: true\`, no token, no channel — electron-updater treats repo as public and sends no credentials. Runtime config block at index.ts:1970-1974 (guarded by \`!is.dev\`): sets \`autoDownload\`, \`autoInstallOnAppQuit\`, and logger only. NO \`setFeedURL\`, NO \`requestHeaders\`, NO \`GH\_TOKEN\`. Dev override: \`dev-app-update.yml\` uses generic provider pointing to example.com (never executed — updater gated behind \`!is.dev\`). electron-updater@^6.3.9, electron-builder@^26.0.12.

<!-- lore:019f2e7c-b347-7709-8be6-8e66c580e8e5 -->
* **electron-updater: update check triggers and event handlers**: Update check triggers in \`apps/electron/src/main/index.ts\`: (1) Startup: \`autoUpdater.checkForUpdatesAndNotify()\` at line 2060 (only when not read-only location). (2) Periodic: \`startUpdateCheckInterval()\` every 5 min (\`UPDATE\_CHECK\_INTERVAL\_MS = 5\*60\*1000\`, line 1953); cleared on \`update-downloaded\`. (3) Menu/Tray: \`checkForUpdatesFromMenu()\` at line 1392-1445. (4) IPC renderer: \`ipcMain.handle('updater:check')\` at line 2073-2085. (5) IPC download: \`ipcMain.on('updater:download')\` at line 2065. (6) IPC install: \`ipcMain.on('updater:install')\` at line 2069. \`update-available\` handler (1976-1999): sends \`updater:available\` IPC, sets download state. \`update-downloaded\` handler (2001-2032): sends \`updater:downloaded\` IPC, clears interval, calls \`prefetchManagedMlxRuntimeForAppRel\`.

<!-- lore:019fb972-64b2-7a7e-9616-e381391d78d2 -->
* **electron-vite externalizeDeps false + rollupOptions external**: Disables electron-vite's automatic externalizeDeps (which reads all \`dependencies\` from package.json and leaves them as runtime \`require()\` calls). Instead, manually externalizes only \`electron\` and \`node-global-key-listener\` via \`rollupOptions.external\`. Everything else — \`@sentry/electron\`, \`electron-updater\`, \`ws\`, \`hono\`, OpenTelemetry, \`debug\`, \`ms\`, etc. — is bundled into the main process JS. This prevents pnpm's isolated \`node\_modules\` from causing missing transitive dependency errors in the packaged \`.asar\`.

<!-- lore:019f6b7b-1b9a-7802-b56d-6aad1f3b4d7c -->
* **Empty-transcript protection: two layers in batch and streaming paths**: Desktop has two layers of empty-transcript protection: - \*\*Layer 1 (route-level):\*\* \`transcribe.ts:455\` — \`if (!rawText.trim() || api.control.state !== 'running') return suppressedResponse()\` before \`postProcess()\`. \`stream.ts:496\` — \`if (api.control.state !== 'running' || !rawText?.trim()) { ws.send(final:''); return; }\` before \`postProcess()\`. - \*\*Layer 2 (post-process.ts:240-252):\*\* strips filler words (um, uh, i mean, etc.) and punctuation; \`if (!stripped) return { cleaned: '', llmProvider: null, ... }\` — filler-only transcripts skip LLM call. Caveat: combined Freestyle Cloud mode (cloud voice + cloud cleanup) is a single remote call — desktop cannot pre-skip remote cleanup, but does guard local \`applyFinalRewrites\`, history row, and paste on empty output.

<!-- lore:019fcea2-002a-7170-97af-a9b3509858f3 -->
* **endRemix vs failRemix: only endRemix clears remixRef, hide:false skips main hidePill cleanup**: In app.tsx, endRemix() (line 1920) is the only function that clears remix state (setRemix(null), line 1933); it's called with {hide:false} from several paths (dictation self-heal at 2399, RemixChat onClose at 3653) which skips window.api.hidePill() — meaning main's hidePill() reset logic (remixPressed, route keys, setFocusable(false)) doesn't run. failRemix() (line 1945) deliberately does NOT clear remixRef — it sets phase:'error' and leaves the session object so an error card can render; only the REMIX\_WARNING\_MS (4500ms) timeout or explicit dismiss later calls endRemix(). When debugging remix/dictation state issues, check which exit path was used — {hide:false} exits are the ones that leave main-process cleanup (focusable/remixPressed) potentially stale. See \[\[\<gotcha-entry-remix-supersede>]] for the suspected downstream bug this causes.

<!-- lore:019f2c4b-0843-7c92-9fb2-4e13f0893e75 -->
* **Enterprise proxy/CA support: network.ts global undici dispatcher, configureNetwork() precedence**: PR #388 adds \`apps/server/src/lib/network.ts\`: \`resolveNetworkConfig()\` reads \`network\_proxy\_url\` and \`network\_ca\_cert\_path\` settings; \`configureNetwork()\` installs a global undici dispatcher. Precedence: explicit proxy URL → \`ProxyAgent\`; env proxy vars → \`EnvHttpProxyAgent\`; custom CA → \`Agent{connect:{ca}}\`. \`startServer()\` calls \`configureNetwork()\` before any fetch. Settings route re-runs \`configureNetwork()\` on save. \`apps/server/src/lib/download-guard.ts\` adds \`assertNotProxyPage()\`, \`ProxyInterceptionError\`, and \`isLikelyProxyOrTlsFailure()\` for detecting proxy interception during model downloads. Validations: \`proxyUrlSettingSchema\` (http/https/socks or empty), \`caCertPathSettingSchema\`. Frontend: new Network section in settings.tsx with save-on-blur inputs. \`undici@7.28.0\` added as server dep.

<!-- lore:019fbc93-bbaa-7c02-976d-c6681a0d799c -->
* **Freestyle Cloud cleanup/post-process never throws — falls back to raw text**: In Freestyle Cloud's transcribe/post-process pipeline (\`routes/v2/transcribe.ts\`, \`post-process.ts\`), the LLM cleanup step is designed to NEVER throw — on any failure it returns the raw (uncleaned) transcript instead of erroring the request. This is a deliberate reliability guarantee so a cleanup provider outage never blocks dictation. When modifying cleanup/post-process code, preserve this fallback-on-error behavior; do not let cleanup exceptions propagate to the client response.

<!-- lore:019fbc93-bbb9-7212-a95d-bc2d26c27aea -->
* **Freestyle Cloud industry-defaults seeding never touches personalTone or languages**: \`seedMemberPreferencesForIndustry()\` (\`apps/server/src/lib/seed-preferences.ts\`) fires when a member's industry is set/changed. It ONLY overwrites \`workTone\`/\`emailTone\`/\`overallTone\` and merges \`vocabulary\` terms (drop old industry terms, add new, case-insensitive dedupe, preserve \`vocabulary.text\` and user-added terms). It NEVER touches \`personalTone\` or \`languages\` — industry seeding has no opinion on either. The preferences row is always written on industry change regardless of vocab length (no length-based skip).

<!-- lore:019fbc93-bb96-7340-99ce-f99be7b58a44 -->
* **Freestyle Cloud STT: Soniox uses full languages\[] hints, Whisper batch uses languages\[0] only**: Freestyle Cloud's two STT paths consume \`languages\` differently. Streaming (Soniox, \`streaming/soniox.ts\`) passes the FULL array as \`language\_hints\` with \`language\_hints\_strict=true\`, falling back to \`enable\_language\_identification=true\` when empty — Soniox can bias toward multiple languages at once. Batch (Whisper via OpenRouter, \`routes/v2/transcribe.ts:149\`) only accepts a single decode-language hint, so only \`languages\[0]\` is passed to Whisper; the full array still drives the cleanup prompt's language constraint (\`streaming/prompt.ts buildLanguageBlock\`) on both paths. Soniox one-way translation only activates when exactly one language resolves (\`languages.length === 1\`) — never for auto-detect or multi-language selections.

<!-- lore:019fbc93-bbb2-7cb4-8d8f-52aeb5ac6ad0 -->
* **Freestyle Cloud usage-gate: fails open except NoOrganizationError**: In \`streaming/stt-session.ts\`, the usage-check gate before opening a Soniox STT session: Pro plan skips the check entirely (unlimited). Free tier checks remaining word balance — if exhausted, tears down upstream and sends \`{type:"error", code:"usage\_exceeded"}\`. On check failure: if the error is \`NoOrganizationError\`, hard-block (tear down + \`{code:"no\_organization"}\`); for any OTHER error, fail OPEN (never block transcription), just \`console.error\`. This asymmetry is deliberate — org-membership issues are unrecoverable for billing, but transient usage-check failures shouldn't block dictation.

<!-- lore:019fbc93-bb5c-783f-b64f-cebc8897a2a0 -->
* **Freestyle Cloud: member\_preferences.languages\[] canonical, singular language deprecated**: In Freestyle Cloud (\`/Users/am/dev/freestyle-voice/cloud\`), \`member\_preferences.languages\` (json string\[], migration 0014 renamed from singular \`language\` column added in 0011) is canonical everywhere: DB schema, validations, transcription, streaming, config. The old singular \`language\` field survives only as \`@deprecated\` legacy input, coalesced into \`languages\` at every entry point (\`routes/v2/transcribe.ts\`, \`routes/v2/post-process.ts\`, \`streaming/stt-session.ts\`) via \`languages ?? (language ? \[language] : undefined)\`, each tagged \`TODO(legacy): drop once all clients send languages\` with a ~1 month removal timeline. Always read/write \`languages\`; treat \`language\` as read-compat only.

<!-- lore:019fb1fd-8e08-7e24-8b9d-c14ba6311a49 -->
* **freestyleCloudUrl() is the canonical resolver for FREESTYLE\_CLOUD\_URL**: \`freestyleCloudUrl()\` (\`apps/server/src/lib/freestyle-cloud.ts:236-241\`) reads \`process.env.FREESTYLE\_CLOUD\_URL\`, falls back to \`DEFAULT\_CLOUD\_URL\` ("https://service.freestylevoice.com"), and strips trailing slashes. This is the single source of truth for resolving the cloud host — used by \`sessions.ts\` (host-scoped session keys), \`llm/registry.ts\`, \`prompt-config.ts\`, \`freestyleCloudStreamWsUrl()\`, and others. Always import and call this function; never read \`process.env.FREESTYLE\_CLOUD\_URL\` directly or hardcode the default string elsewhere, since the fallback and slash-stripping logic must stay in one place.

<!-- lore:019f418b-e39f-789c-8a78-19f3ee96e01a -->
* **GET /api/settings deduplication: pill window has no QueryClientProvider**: The pill window (\`pill.tsx\` → \`app.tsx\`) has NO \`QueryClientProvider\` wrapper — only \`dashboard.tsx\` wraps one. All API calls in the pill window are plain fetches and cannot participate in React Query deduplication. The 6× \`GET /api/settings\` at startup come from (a) separate Electron windows/processes with independent QueryClient instances and (b) plain fetch calls bypassing React Query. Within the dashboard window, all settings consumers share \`SETTINGS\_QUERY\_KEY = \["settings-all"] as const\` (defined in \`apps/electron/src/renderer/src/lib/query.ts\`) and deduplicate correctly. \`settingsQueryOptions()\` uses \`staleTime: ONE\_HOUR\` (3600000ms), \`refetchOnWindowFocus: false\`, \`retry: 1\`.

<!-- lore:019f40a9-8d0d-7b24-b797-143037deb58c -->
* **history.tsx: global diff/AI-edit toggles lifted to FilterPanel, FeedItem memoized**: As of July 2026 refactor: \`showDiff\` and \`showAiEdit\` state lifted from per-card local state to page-level props. \`FeedItem\` wrapped in \`React.memo\`, accepts \`diffMode\` and \`showAiEdits\` as props. Per-card \`FileDiff\`, \`Undo2\`, \`Redo2\` buttons removed — only Copy and Delete remain on individual cards. \`FilterPanel\` is a memoized subcomponent containing global diff/AI-edit toggles (Switch components), preset buttons (2×2 grid: Today/Last7/Last30/All Time), and DayPicker. \`useQuery\` uses \`placeholderData: keepPreviousData\` to prevent full-page flash on filter changes. Callbacks (\`applyPreset\`, \`selectDateRange\`, \`resetFilters\`, \`closeFilter\`) stabilized with \`useCallback\`.

<!-- lore:019f5b5e-925a-72de-84e3-454d2b6303ba -->
* **Hotkey state machine: hotkeyPressed flag, toggle vs hold modes, hidePill reset**: Key state: \`hotkeyPressed\` (boolean, \`index.ts:271\`). In hold mode: \`handleNativeHotkeyDown\` sets true only if \`!hotkeyPressed\`; \`handleNativeHotkeyUp\` clears it and calls \`sendHotkeyUp()\`. In toggle mode: \`handleNativeHotkeyDown\` flips the flag each press; \`handleNativeHotkeyUp\` returns immediately (no key-up events from globalShortcut fallback). \`hidePill()\` (line 1187) resets \`hotkeyPressed = false\` — this is the canonical post-session reset, firing BEFORE paste keystrokes via \`beforePaste\` callback in \`doPasteIntoFocusedApp\`. \`registerHotkey()\` also resets on re-registration (line 2603). \`ipcMain.on('hotkey:set-mode')\` resets on mode change (line 2326). Fallback path (\`registerGlobalShortcutToggle\`) always uses toggle semantics — no key-up events available from Electron globalShortcut.

<!-- lore:019f7e80-c7ba-7695-8e69-c904c9fe3b60 -->
* **iOS keyboard Expo plugin: withKeyboardExtension.js structure and known CocoaPods bug**: \`apps/mobile/plugins/withKeyboardExtension.js\` (244 lines). Constants: \`EXT\_NAME='FreestyleKeyboard'\`, \`APP\_GROUP='group.com.freestylevoice.app'\`, \`SOURCE\_FILES=\['KeyboardViewController.swift','SharedStore.swift']\`, \`DEPLOYMENT\_TARGET='16.0'\`. Pipeline: \`withMainAppEntitlements\` → \`withMainAppInfoPlist\` → \`withKeyboardXcodeProject\`. Known bug: \`node-xcode addTarget\` creates orphaned 'Products' group when Expo project's products group lacks 'Products' comment → breaks CocoaPods with consistency error. Fix: \`ensureProductInProductsGroup()\` looks up real products group via \`PBXProject.productRefGroup\`, falls back to \`pbxGroupByName('Products')\`, creates one if absent. Plugin deliberately does NOT add a second \`PBXCopyFilesBuildPhase\` — \`addTarget('app\_extension')\` already creates the embed phase on the host target.

<!-- lore:019f7bf9-db2e-7e69-9257-9ff9ffae3117 -->
* **iOS keyboard extension: KeyboardViewController layout — hStack fillEqually trap and row width constants**: \`KeyboardViewController.swift\` (510 lines) in \`apps/mobile/ios-keyboard/\`. Layout: \`hStack(equal:Bool=true)\` — rows mixing fixed-width special keys (shift/delete/123) with letter groups must use \`hStack(equal: false)\` (\`.fill\` distribution); letter group wrapped in inner \`hStack()\` (equal) so only letters/space stretch. Affected rows: \`insetRow\`, \`shiftRow\`, \`symbolRow\`, \`bottomRow\`. Trap: \`.fillEqually\` on mixed rows collapses the letter group. Bottom row widths: layerKey=92pt, globe=44pt (conditional on \`needsInputModeSwitchKey\`), space=flexible, return=100pt. Total keyboard height = toolbarHeight(44) + rowSpacing(9) + rowHeight(46)×4 + rowSpacing(9)×3 + rowSpacing(9).

<!-- lore:019f7e80-c7b2-7907-b08e-6f1ae9cde910 -->
* **iOS keyboard extension: KeyboardViewController.swift structure and key metrics**: KeyboardViewController at \`apps/mobile/ios-keyboard/KeyboardViewController.swift\` (587 lines). Key metrics: rowHeight=46, rowSpacing=9, keySpacing=6, toolbarHeight=44. State: \`layer\` (.letters/.numbers/.symbols), \`shift\` (ShiftState: .off/.on/.capsLock, starts .on for sentence start). Toolbar: brandStack (brandLabel + statusLabel) on left, MicLink SwiftUI view (34×34, \`freestyle://dictate\` deep link) on right. Character keys use \`UIButton(type: .system)\` with \`.touchUpInside\` — root cause of dropped keystrokes during fast typing. Delete uses \`.touchDown\` with 0.4s initial delay → 0.1s char repeat → 0.5s word repeat. \`applyAutoCap()\` triggers on viewWillAppear, textDidChange, handleDeleteUp, return key. \`insertPendingTranscriptIfAny()\` reads from SharedStore, shows 'Inserted ✓' in statusLabel for 1.6s.

<!-- lore:019fafc5-aa9b-7e9e-b8ae-bb352d773a0c -->
* **iOS keyboard-enabled detection via App Group handshake timestamp**: iOS provides no API to programmatically detect if a custom keyboard is enabled or has Full Access. Detection uses a handshake: the keyboard extension writes a timestamp to shared App Group UserDefaults via bridge.markKeyboardActive() (KeyboardViewController.swift:126-135) — this write only succeeds if the extension has Full Access. The host app reads it back via keyboardLastActive() (FreestyleSharedStoreModule Expo native module); a non-zero recent timestamp proves the keyboard is both enabled and has Full Access. use-keyboard-status.ts hook re-reads on app foreground plus a 1.5s poll so status flips instantly when the user returns from Settings. Returns "unsupported" | "not-enabled" | "ready". Same App Group bridge (DictationBridge.swift, two verbatim copies) used for mic access constraints. See \[\[019f7e74-2ac6-7ab3-b0fc-3531b5d7bcc1]].

<!-- lore:019f7c13-7d9a-74da-8e61-03fd1b3da9fd -->
* **iOS: no public API to return to previous app after dictation — Wispr pattern is manual**: iOS provides no public API for a keyboard extension to programmatically return to the previous app after dictation. The "Wispr Flow" pattern is manual: after \`onFinal\`, show a "Return to your app" card (dictate.tsx lines 210–220) that the user taps. No auto-return logic exists anywhere in the codebase. Related: \`hasDictationKey = true\` must be set in \`init\`, \`viewDidLoad\`, AND \`viewWillAppear\` — setting only in \`viewDidLoad\` is insufficient on iOS 18+. See also: https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/727983

<!-- lore:019faff6-172c-724c-b139-6ef1301ff009 -->
* **Keyboard status detection via App Group handshake (markKeyboardActive/keyboardLastActive)**: iOS provides no API to programmatically enable a custom keyboard extension or grant Full Access (same constraint as Wispr Flow) — only sanctioned path is deep-linking to Settings + detecting the result. Detection mechanism: the keyboard extension can only write to shared App Group UserDefaults if it has Full Access, so it stamps a handshake timestamp via bridge.markKeyboardActive() on load (KeyboardViewController.swift). App reads it back via keyboardLastActive() (native module FreestyleSharedStoreModule.swift + platform-safe dictation-bridge.ts facade, returns null on Android/Expo Go). useKeyboardStatus hook (apps/mobile/src/lib/keyboard/use-keyboard-status.ts) maps this to "unsupported"|"not-enabled"|"ready", re-reading on app foreground + a 1.5s poll so status flips instantly after returning from Settings. Used in keyboard-setup.tsx and onboarding.tsx to show live status + guided CTA instead of a blind "did you enable it?" prompt.

<!-- lore:019fd03f-b217-7824-8f85-4d65b2c3312b -->
* **knip.jsonc: templates/\*\* and apps/docs/\*\* are non-workspace, always ignored**: knip.jsonc explicitly ignores templates/\*\* (standalone starter projects downloaded by create-freestyle-plugin, never imported/built in the monorepo) and apps/docs/\*\* (standalone Mintlify project, not a pnpm workspace member) — don't flag files there as dead code or try to wire them into the workspace. Also ignores \`expo-updates\` in apps/mobile (falsely inferred by knip's Expo plugin) and \`@sentry/cli\` (invoked via Xcode build phase, not TS imports). knip rules for exports/types/enumMembers/namespaceMembers are all "off" — export cleanup is deferred backlog; current CI gate only covers unused files/deps/unresolved imports/binaries.

<!-- lore:019fbc97-18ce-7014-a5d3-1fe363d264e3 -->
* **Language handling is singular \`language\` end-to-end (no plural array)**: Confirmed via exhaustive grep across apps/electron, apps/server, and packages/validations: transcription language is stored/sent as a single \`language: string\` everywhere — settings-keys.ts, SQLite settings table, \`/v2/transcribe\`, \`/v2/post-process\`, \`/v2/stream\` WS start message, all BYOK/local providers (Soniox, ElevenLabs, Deepgram, whisper.cpp, MLX), and prompt-building. No plural \`languages\` field exists anywhere in code; the only plural name is \`suggestedLanguages\` (cloud config's ~66-language Soniox catalog for populating pickers) and doc-comment prose. This is the key gap/starting point for any refactor from single-language select to multi-language pill selector — every call site (transcribe.ts, stream.ts, freestyle-cloud.ts, streaming providers, preferences-sync.ts) assumes exactly one language string.

<!-- lore:019f1407-8c67-7621-bf04-568e694d4555 -->
* **Language pipeline: auto-detect flows as undefined through ASR and LLM cleanup stages**: \`normalizeLanguageSetting()\` returns \`undefined\` for \`null\`, \`undefined\`, or \`'auto'\`. \`getLanguageSetting()\` reads the \`settings\` table and returns \`undefined\` for auto. In \`transcribe.ts\` line 222, language is omitted from the ASR call when \`undefined\`. In \`post-process.ts\`, \`undefined\` is passed directly to \`buildRewritePrompt()\` → \`buildLanguageBlock()\`. Whisper-local sends \`opts.language ?? 'auto'\` as the wire value. ElevenLabs uses key \`languageCode\`; all other providers use \`language\`. \`aiSdkProviderOptions()\` skips the language key entirely when falsy or \`'auto'\`. The LLM cleanup stage (not ASR) is where translation bugs manifest when the language constraint is absent.

<!-- lore:019fbc9a-9e58-78fb-b28b-d45ba467b85f -->
* **Language setting is singular \`language\` end-to-end, no plural \`languages\` field exists**: Exhaustive grep across apps/electron, apps/server, and packages/validations (Aug 2026 mapping session) confirmed transcription language is a single string everywhere: settings-keys.ts \`language\`, SQLite \`settings\` table, \`getLanguageSetting()\`/\`normalizeLanguageSetting()\` (apps/server/src/lib/language.ts), \`LanguageCombobox\`/\`LanguagePickerDialog\`/\`useLanguageOptions\` (single-value, radio-style), Freestyle Cloud wire fields (\`/v2/transcribe\`, \`/v2/stream\`, \`/v2/post-process\` all send \`language\` singular), all BYOK/local streaming providers (Soniox, Deepgram, ElevenLabs, whisper-local, MLX). The only plural is \`suggestedLanguages\` (cloud's ~66-language catalog array used for populating options, not for selection). Any multi-language pill-selector refactor must introduce a new plural field/schema across all these layers — there is no existing plumbing to repurpose.

<!-- lore:019f0ebe-4535-7651-a2fc-7aa5c6c169f9 -->
* **loadServerPlugins() in apps/server: reads settings, filters disabled, calls SDK loadPlugins()**: \`apps/server/src/lib/plugins/loader.ts\` (54 lines): \`loadServerPlugins(builtin: Plugin\[] = \[])\` reads \`disabled\_plugins\` via \`parseDisabledPlugins(readSetting('disabled\_plugins'))\`, reads \`plugins\` via \`parsePluginsSetting(readSetting('plugins'))\`, filters out disabled specifiers, passes \`defaultLocalPluginsDir()\` as \`localDir\`, then delegates to SDK \`loadPlugins()\`. Hook failures reported via \`reportHookFailure\` → \`log.error\` + \`captureException\`. Repo's \`plugins/\` directory contains \`audio-transcription/\` and \`example/\` — these are NOT auto-loaded; they must be built and placed in \`\<userData>/plugins/\` or added to the \`plugins\` setting.

<!-- lore:019fb9e6-a5fa-7ae3-bdf2-5fd4a50ece29 -->
* **Local embedded server runs continuously, independent of remote URL config**: The Electron app's embedded local server (default port 4649) always keeps running regardless of the configured remote serverUrl — there is no app restart for server URL/token changes. Config changes propagate via \`server:changed\` IPC broadcast → \`refreshApiBase()\` (apps/electron/src/renderer/src/lib/api.ts:105) → React Query cache invalidation. This is a firm architectural requirement, not an optimization; any proposal requiring an app restart to apply server config changes is wrong.

<!-- lore:019f433a-5962-7104-900a-03327758f604 -->
* **local\_llm\_api\_key and local\_llm\_url: dedicated settings entries, not api\_keys table**: \`local\_llm\_api\_key\` and \`local\_llm\_url\` are stored as dedicated \`settings\` table entries, NOT in the \`api\_keys\` table (which holds cloud provider keys only). Server reads both in \`apps/server/src/routes/models.ts:52,66-67\`; sends \`Authorization: Bearer \<key>\` to \`\<baseUrl>/v1/models\`. Renderer writes via \`PUT /api/settings/local\_llm\_api\_key\` in \`use-models.ts:596-602\`; deletes via \`DELETE\` if key is empty. \`LocalLlmConnect\` component (\`model-list.tsx:709-777\`) provides dedicated UI: URL field (default \`http://localhost:11434\`), API key input (line 757), test-connection button. Rendered when \`showLocalLlmForm\` is true — only for LLM in local-only mode. \`useModels.localLlm\` exposes: \`localUrl\`, \`localApiKey\`, \`localTesting\`, \`localConnected\`, \`localError\`, \`localModels\`, \`testLocalLlm\`, \`clearLocalStatus\` (lines 652-663).

<!-- lore:019fb9b5-15ad-799b-96f7-8309b5051aff -->
* **lucide barrel import replaced with curated icon map in plugins/helpers.ts**: Replaced full \`lucide-react\` barrel import in \`renderer/src/plugins/helpers.ts\` with a curated map of icons actually used by plugins (Puzzle, Mic, Stop, Settings, Check). Preserves kebab/Pascal resolution in \`resolvePluginIcon\`. Reduces bundle size and avoids pulling unused icon set. No runtime behavior change for existing plugins.

<!-- lore:019f5b7f-fff7-70bb-8780-76df38c1b16e -->
* **macOS Fn-paste desync bug: macOS-specific, not reproducible on Windows or Linux**: The stuck-modifier / paste-desync bug (synthetic CGEvents not filtered by key listener) is macOS-specific. Requires 3 conditions absent elsewhere: (1) solo-modifier hotkey (Fn) that is edge-detected, (2) suppression gate blocking activation when non-Fn modifiers are present, (3) macOS CGEvent flag mask treated as persistent global state with no OS resync. Fix: stamp synthetic \`keyDown\`/\`keyUp\` CGEvents with \`.eventSourceUserData = freestyleSyntheticMarker\` (0x4653\_5459 'FSTY') in \`macos-fast-paste.swift\`; key listener early-returns on matching marker in both \`NSEvent.addGlobalMonitorForEvents\` and \`CGEvent.tapCreate\` callbacks. Windows: \`GetAsyncKeyState()\` polling in \`SyncModifierState()\` self-heals stale state. Linux: compound chord hotkey; \`modifiers\_satisfied()\` only checks required modifiers present, never unwanted ones absent.

<!-- lore:019f7af5-5db1-7629-b492-f13ad79283c6 -->
* **macOS: Fn/Globe hotkey is a solo modifier — uniquely vulnerable to modifier-state desync**: The default Fn/Globe hotkey is a solo modifier. macOS key-listener has no OS-level resync (unlike Windows' GetAsyncKeyState or Linux's uinput balance), and the listener derives modifier state from per-event flag masks with no resync. If the app's own injected paste doesn't release the Command flag, the flag accumulates and blocks the hotkey. Fix: tag synthetic events with an \`eventSourceUserData\` marker and ignore them in the listener. This is macOS-specific; Windows and Linux are unaffected.

<!-- lore:019fbca6-2b36-70c4-9e83-8d9f783c5dac -->
* **Mobile app language field is singular string (apps/mobile)**: apps/mobile models language as a singular string everywhere: AsyncStorage key "language" (settings.tsx), CLOUD\_FIELD\_MAP language↔cloud field, member-preferences schema \`language: z.string().max(10).nullish()\` (packages/validations/src/member-preferences.ts), cloud-config \`/v2/config\` suggestedLanguages (options catalog only, not selection), and the stream \`start\` message. No \`languages\` array exists anywhere in mobile or shared validations. LanguageSheet picker (components/language-sheet.tsx) and onboarding StepLanguage pills (app/(app)/onboarding.tsx) are single-select only (checkmark/highlight, one active code, no removable pills). A multi-language refactor requires migrating \`language: string\` → \`languages: string\[]\` across settings.tsx, member-preferences.ts (shared, affects cloud), and stream.ts — no existing plumbing for multi-select. Mirrors the same singular-language finding already confirmed for desktop/electron \[\[019fbc9a-9e58-78fb-b28b-d45ba467b85f]].

<!-- lore:019f7af5-5dae-782c-8b73-551345775a22 -->
* **Mobile app: backend URL config and auth — EXPO\_PUBLIC\_CLOUD\_URL, better-auth/expo, SecureStore**: \`apps/mobile/src/lib/cloud/config.ts\` resolution order: (1) \`EXPO\_PUBLIC\_CLOUD\_URL\` from \`apps/mobile/.env.local\` (gitignored), (2) \`Constants.expoConfig?.extra.freestyleCloudUrl\`, (3) production default \`https://service.freestylevoice.com\`. Physical device requires LAN IP (not \`localhost\`) — find via \`ipconfig getifaddr en0\`. Auth via \`@better-auth/expo\` \`expoClient\` plugin, scheme \`"freestyle"\`, session in \`expo-secure-store\`. \`authClient.getCookie()\` available for authenticated requests. Streaming STT over WebSocket at \`/v2/stream\`. User prefers streaming to Freestyle Cloud production rather than local \`apps/server\`.

<!-- lore:019f7af5-5da8-71a5-a401-6a65098c5e7b -->
* **Mobile app: Expo SDK 57, expo-dev-client, no EAS, local prebuild only**: Located at \`apps/mobile\`. Expo SDK 57, expo-router (file-based, \`src/app\`), \`"main": "expo-router/entry"\`. Requires \`expo-dev-client ~57.0.5\` — NOT plain Expo Go. \`ios/\` and \`android/\` are gitignored, always produced by \`expo prebuild\`. No \`eas.json\` — all native builds are local via \`expo run:ios\` / \`expo run:android\`. Root convenience scripts: \`dev:ios\` / \`dev:android\` via Turbo. Two native components requiring prebuild: (1) iOS Keyboard Extension (\`plugins/withKeyboardExtension.js\` + \`ios-keyboard/\`); (2) local Expo native module at \`modules/freestyle-shared-store/\`. Bundle ID: \`com.freestylevoice.app\`; URL scheme: \`freestyle://\`; App Group: \`group.com.freestylevoice.app\`. Icon library: \`lucide-react-native\` + \`react-native-svg\` (SDK 57-pinned). \`expo-linking\` is listed as a dep but never imported or used for URL handling.

<!-- lore:019f7af5-5db9-765a-b255-d671fb6db238 -->
* **Mobile audio pipeline: toCloudFrame produces 16kHz PCM16LE, always plain non-shared ArrayBuffer**: \`apps/mobile/src/lib/audio/resample.ts\`: \`TARGET\_SAMPLE\_RATE = 16\_000\`. \`toCloudFrame(data, sampleRate, channels)\` pipeline: \`Int16Array(data)\` → \`downmixToMono\` (averages interleaved channels) → \`resampleMono\` (linear interpolation) → copy into fresh \`Int16Array\` → return \`.buffer\`. Returns \`null\` if \`data.byteLength < 2\`. Result is always a plain (non-shared) ArrayBuffer — copy step ensures tightly-sized output, never padded. \`CloudStreamSession\` sends binary PCM16LE 16kHz mono frames; server→client JSON messages: \`config\`, \`session.ready\`, \`partial\`, \`final\`, \`error\`.

<!-- lore:019fba0c-4eb6-798a-bd4e-0ef72f91bf55 -->
* **Mobile authHeaders() uses session Cookie, not Bearer token like desktop**: apps/mobile/src/lib/cloud/session.ts:30-33 \`authHeaders()\` returns \`{Cookie: authClient.getCookie()}\` from the @better-auth/expo client (session persisted via expo-secure-store) — contrast with desktop's OAuth device-flow Bearer token in freestyle-cloud.ts:347 (see cloudJson() \[\[019fb9e6-a611-7c15-97d5-ec1bf4dbed15]]). Used by mobile usage.ts, preferences.ts, profile.ts, and stream.ts. React Native's global WebSocket uniquely accepts a 3rd constructor arg for custom headers (stream.ts:97-100) to pass the Cookie — desktop/browser WebSocket has no such option. Any shared cloud-fetch helper across mobile/desktop must accept an injectable auth-header provider rather than assuming Bearer-only auth.

<!-- lore:019fba12-ee70-7b52-a4c1-96556dc744f1 -->
* **Mobile cloud layer (apps/mobile/src/lib/cloud/) uses Cookie auth, not Bearer**: apps/mobile/src/lib/cloud/ mirrors desktop's freestyle-cloud.ts but authenticates differently: better-auth Expo client stores a session cookie via expo-secure-store, and session.ts:30 \`authHeaders()\` returns \`{Cookie: authClient.getCookie()}\` — vs desktop's OAuth device-flow Bearer token (freestyle-cloud.ts:347) \[\[019fb9e6-a611-7c15-97d5-ec1bf4dbed15]]. Base URL resolved via config.ts \`cloudUrl()\` (EXPO\_PUBLIC\_CLOUD\_URL → expo-constants extra.freestyleCloudUrl → DEFAULT\_CLOUD\_URL) vs desktop's \`freestyleCloudUrl()\` reading FREESTYLE\_CLOUD\_URL. Auth/org/subscription actions mostly go through \`authClient.\*\` (better-auth: unlinkAccount, linkSocial, organization.list, subscription.upgrade) rather than raw fetch; only usage/preferences/profile use raw fetch, each duplicating the same auth-guard + org-slug-resolution + AbortSignal.timeout + error-classification boilerplate (5x repeated — candidate for a shared helper). Both apps import shared types from \`@freestyle-voice/validations\` workspace package.

<!-- lore:019fba00-e951-793c-a914-8b39976bc3bb -->
* **Mobile cloud layer lacks a cloudJson() equivalent — 5x duplicated fetch boilerplate**: Unlike desktop's centralized \`cloudJson\<T>()\` wrapper \[\[019fb9e6-a611-7c15-97d5-ec1bf4dbed15]], apps/mobile/src/lib/cloud/ has no shared fetch helper. The same boilerplate is hand-copied across 5 authenticated calls (usage.ts, preferences.ts x2, profile.ts x2): authHeaders() null-guard → CloudAuthError, org-slug resolution + early return, \`credentials:"omit"\`, \`AbortSignal.timeout()\`, 401→CloudAuthError, \`!res.ok\`→throw Error, JSON parse/stringify with content-type header. A shared runtime-agnostic \`cloudJson()\` (e.g. in a new \`@freestyle-voice/cloud-client\` package, or extending \`@freestyle-voice/validations\`) is feasible since both mobile (RN fetch) and desktop (Node fetch) support \`fetch\` + \`AbortSignal.timeout()\` with no axios/ky/undici dependency. Each app should keep a thin adapter for baseUrl/auth-header injection rather than sharing those directly.

<!-- lore:019fb9f9-a330-7297-86fd-b8b79cf60ead -->
* **Mobile cloud layer: Cookie auth via authHeaders() vs desktop Bearer token**: apps/mobile/src/lib/cloud/ mirrors desktop's Freestyle Cloud layer but with different auth: mobile uses a session Cookie (via @better-auth/expo + expo-secure-store), obtained through \`authHeaders()\` in session.ts:30-33 (\`{Cookie: authClient.getCookie()}\` or null). Desktop uses an OAuth device-flow Bearer token in cloudJson() \[\[019fb9e6-a611-7c15-97d5-ec1bf4dbed15]]. Both sides share type contracts via the \`@freestyle-voice/validations\` workspace package (CloudMemberPreferences, CloudProfile, ProfileInput, CloudConfigResponse, tone types) — confirmed imported in both apps/mobile/src/lib/cloud/\*.ts and apps/server/src/lib/freestyle-cloud.ts. Mobile has 6 fetch() calls, all confined to cloud/, each duplicating: authHeaders() guard → CloudAuthError, org-slug resolution + early return via resolveActiveOrgSlug(), credentials:"omit", AbortSignal.timeout, 401→CloudAuthError, !res.ok→throw. This boilerplate is the basis for a proposed shared cloudJson-style helper.

<!-- lore:019fba02-ea3e-77fb-9033-8eaef1ffe819 -->
* **Mobile cloud layer: Cookie auth via better-auth/expo, no shared fetch wrapper**: apps/mobile/src/lib/cloud/\*.ts has 6 raw fetch() calls (cloud-config.ts, usage.ts, preferences.ts x2, profile.ts x2), each duplicating: authHeaders() guard, credentials:"omit", AbortSignal.timeout, 401→CloudAuthError, !res.ok→throw. Mobile auth is a session Cookie header — authHeaders() at session.ts:30-33 returns \`{Cookie: authClient.getCookie()}\`, backed by @better-auth/expo + expo-secure-store — NOT desktop's Bearer-token pattern used by cloudJson() \[\[019fb9e6-a611-7c15-97d5-ec1bf4dbed15]]. This auth-mechanism difference is the key blocker to naive helper sharing: any unified wrapper needs an injectable auth-header provider, not a hardcoded Authorization header.

<!-- lore:019fb9ff-1cfc-7d0e-b66c-74542a80d1c5 -->
* **Mobile cloud/ layer: 5 fetches duplicate auth+error boilerplate**: apps/mobile/src/lib/cloud/ (usage.ts, preferences.ts×2, profile.ts×2) has 5 authenticated fetch() calls that each repeat: authHeaders() guard → CloudAuthError if null, org-slug resolution + early-return if none, credentials:"omit", AbortSignal.timeout(), 401→CloudAuthError, !res.ok→throw Error, JSON stringify/parse with content-type header. No shared wrapper exists on mobile (unlike desktop's cloudJson() \[\[019fb9e6-a611-7c15-97d5-ec1bf4dbed15]]). Mobile also has only 2 error types (CloudAuthError + generic Error) vs desktop's richer FreestyleCloudAuthError/UsageError/RequestError hierarchy with retry-on-stale-socket. A shared runtime-agnostic cloudJson\<T>() helper (candidate package @freestyle-voice/cloud-client) is feasible since both apps use bare fetch + AbortSignal.timeout with no axios/ky/node:\* deps — but must accept injectable baseUrl/authHeaders/org-slug resolvers rather than hardcoding either app's mechanism.

<!-- lore:019fc2d6-cd1f-7f8f-b2d3-07aee02b41f6 -->
* **Mobile cloud/streaming code is independent from apps/server — no shared module**: apps/mobile has its own \`CloudStreamSession\` (apps/mobile/src/lib/cloud/stream.ts), independent \`StreamCleanupPreferences\` type, \`CLOUD\_FIELD\_MAP\` in settings.tsx, and \`pushCloudPreferences\`/\`fetchCloudPreferences\` helpers — none of it imports from or shares code with apps/server/src/lib/streaming or preferences-sync.ts, despite both talking to the same Freestyle Cloud /v2/stream and member\_preferences endpoints. Confirmed during the Aug 2026 'stop resending synced cleanup prefs' refactor, which required a second, fully separate pass over apps/mobile/src/lib/cloud/stream.ts, use-dictation.ts, use-resident-dictation.ts, and settings.tsx after finishing the desktop server changes. Any future change to cloud payload/sync behavior must be applied to both codebases by hand — there's no shared package to update once.

<!-- lore:019f7af5-5dbc-737c-ab82-51a69b9734d0 -->
* **Mobile dictate screen: auto-starts recording, MIN\_RECORDING\_MS=350, setPendingTranscript on final**: On iOS 18+, \`UIApplication.shared.open(\_:options:completionHandler:)\` and \`perform(sel\_registerName("openURL:"))\` both fail silently when called from a keyboard extension. The only Apple-sanctioned mechanism is a SwiftUI \`Link(destination: url)\` hosted via \`UIHostingController\`. This also applies to \`MicLink\` used to launch dictation — use \`Link\` instead of gesture-based \`openURL\` calls.

<!-- lore:019f7c13-7d96-7b26-a66e-14e457edc08d -->
* **Mobile dictate.tsx: state machine, auto-start, and transcript handoff flow**: \`apps/mobile/src/app/(app)/dictate.tsx\` (268 lines). MicState: \`"idle" | "recording" | "finalizing"\`. MIN\_RECORDING\_MS=350. Auto-starts via \`useEffect\` guarded by \`autoStarted\` ref + \`signedIn\`. Tap-to-toggle: \`onPressIn=toggle\`, \`onPressOut\` is no-op. \`onFinal\`: sets idle, calls \`setPendingTranscript(text)\` → \`keyboard-bridge.ts\` → \`FreestyleSharedStoreModule\` (native) → writes \`pendingTranscript\`+\`pendingTranscriptAt\` to App Group \`group.com.freestylevoice.app\` UserDefaults. Keyboard reads on \`viewWillAppear\` via \`insertPendingTranscriptIfAny()\` (guards on timestamp > lastInsertedAt). "Done" button does \`router.replace("/(app)")\` — in-app nav only, no app-switch. \`expo-linking\` is listed as a dep but never imported or used for URL handling.

<!-- lore:019fbca6-2b59-7e7d-99ab-258bf95e4ada -->
* **Mobile settings.tsx: cloud-authority optimistic-write pattern**: apps/mobile/src/lib/settings.tsx (SettingsProvider/useSettings) is the single source of truth for dictation settings. Pattern: local AsyncStorage (instant/offline) + TanStack Query key \["cloud-preferences"] (5min staleTime), where cloud always wins when present (\`cloud ? applyCloudPreferences(local, cloud) : local\`). Writes use a plain \`persist(storageKey, cloudField, value)\` callback (no useMutation): optimistically updates local state + AsyncStorage + query cache, then fire-and-forget PUTs to cloud (\`.catch(() => {})\` — cloud sync errors never affect the local write). \`CLOUD\_FIELD\_MAP\`/\`CLOUD\_FIELD\_STORAGE\_KEY\` map each synced setting to its cloud field and AsyncStorage key; \`cleanup\` toggle is intentionally excluded (device-only, never synced). Simpler cousin of desktop's cloud-sync architecture \[\[019fbc9a-9e58-78fb-b28b-d45ba467b85f]] — no vector-clock/tombstones, just last-write cloud-wins per field.

<!-- lore:019fafc5-aaa5-7a03-9dc5-6d0e8759dbe5 -->
* **Mobile streaming cleanup payload matches desktop exactly**: apps/mobile hits the same WSS /v2/stream endpoint as desktop (cloud/config.ts:36-38) and sends the identical cleanup payload — skipPostProcess, intensity, customPrompt, all four tones (stream.ts:114-136) — with field names matching desktop's freestyle-cloud.ts:102-122 exactly. Consumes the cloud-cleaned final in use-dictation.ts:160-172, then applies local dictionary replacements. Mobile lacks desktop's appAssignments/app-context tone routing and batch fallback, but this does not disable core cleanup. If cleanup "feels off" on mobile, first check the settings.cleanup toggle (sends skipPostProcess:true when off) or the deployed Freestyle Cloud service (separate repo) — not a mobile client bug.

<!-- lore:019faff6-173b-7f44-9c42-a4a5d3b2d1ee -->
* **Native IAP trust model — server verification before finishing transaction**: apps/mobile IAP flow only finishes a StoreKit/Play transaction after server-side receipt verification succeeds — unverified purchases are redelivered (not lost) on next launch/restore, never silently dropped. iap-verify.ts implements the client for POST /v1/iap/verify and documents the full intended backend contract (Apple App Store Server API / Google Play Developer API validation + store server notifications). That endpoint lives in the separate service.freestylevoice.com repo (Freestyle Cloud) — until deployed, the client shows "not available yet" and preserves the receipt for restore. Product IDs are defined in cloud/config.ts, overridable via extra.iap or env vars. App Store Connect / Play Console product setup (subscriptions, Paid Apps agreement, sandbox testers) must be done separately in each console before this can go live.

<!-- lore:019fcec5-f537-7619-b8cb-19062fee3a36 -->
* **No unified dictation/remix mode enum — pillActiveRef vs remixRef track independently**: apps/electron/src/renderer/src/pages/app.tsx's pill state machine has no single 'mode' enum. Dictation state = state/stateRef (PillState: idle|initializing|recording|transcribing|error) plus pillActiveRef, which is set true ONLY in startRecording(). Remix state = remix/remixRef.current (RemixSession|null) with phase capturing|listening|running|chat|error, set via setRemix()/patchRemix() in beginRemix(), openRemixChat(), and the onRemixOpenChat handler. These two flags are independent and NOT mutually exclusive — remixRef can be non-null while pillActiveRef is false (e.g. during remix chat). Any dictation/remix bugfix must check both flags explicitly rather than assuming one implies the other is false.

<!-- lore:019f3166-f49a-7845-a7c5-25fce44d2dd2 -->
* **Onboarding auth flow: GitHub OAuth device flow, CloudStep component, auth-context.tsx**: Sign-in UI lives in \`apps/electron/src/renderer/src/onboarding.tsx\` — \`CloudStep\` component (lines 1104–1205). \`GitHubMark\` SVG defined at lines 1091–1102. Button (lines 1161–1178): dark zinc-900, \`onClick={onSignIn}\`, shows spinner while \`signingIn\`. Auth logic in \`apps/electron/src/renderer/src/lib/auth-context.tsx\` (168 lines): \`signIn()\` calls \`api.auth.device.code.$post()\`, opens browser via \`window.api.openExternal(verification\_uri\_complete)\`, polls \`api.auth.device.token.$post()\`. 202=pending, 429=slow\_down (+5000ms). \`cloud-signin-modal.tsx\` shows device \`userCode\` in monospace \`tracking-\[0.3em]\`. Server route: \`apps/server/src/routes/auth.ts\` — \`POST /device/token\` calls \`fetchCloudUser\` + \`setSession\` + \`applyFreestyleCloudDefaults\` + \`identifyCloudUser\`. Only GitHub OAuth — no Google login. \`CLIENT\_ID='freestyle-desktop'\`, \`DEFAULT\_CLOUD\_URL='https://service.freestylevoice.com'\`.

<!-- lore:019f18ae-ce53-7a82-82f5-299365738007 -->
* **Onboarding VoiceItem/model state: chosen, chosenReady, recommended, RECOMMENDED\_LOCAL\_IDS**: Onboarding VoiceItem/model state: \`chosen = allVoiceItems.find((v) => v.selected) ?? recommended\`. \`chosenReady\`: cloud → \`!!chosen.hasKey\`; local → \`chosen.status === 'ready'\`. \`recommended\` prefers MLX Qwen3 when \`mlxStatus?.canRun === true\`, else Whisper small-q5\_1. Constants: \`RECOMMENDED\_MLX\_DEF = 'qwen3-0.6b-8bit'\`, \`RECOMMENDED\_WHISPER\_DEF = 'small-q5\_1'\`. \`RECOMMENDED\_LOCAL\_IDS = new Set(\['local-mlx/qwen3-0.6b-8bit', 'local-whisper/small-q5\_1'])\` (models.ts lines 247-250). \`VoiceItem.status\`: \`'not\_downloaded'|'downloading'|'verifying'|'ready'|'error'\`. Auto-setup \`useEffect\` guarded by \`autoPicked.current\`; calls \`selectLocalModel\` + optionally \`downloadLocalModel\` + \`commitFreestyleCloudDefault\` for cloud users. PR #357: replaced silent auto-download with explicit \`ModelSetupPanel\` (Download button + progress). Cloud users should not see download prompt — gate on \`chosen.kind !== 'cloud'\`.

<!-- lore:019faf9d-1cfc-77aa-a56b-7aff2f801278 -->
* **Org endpoints (list/active/set-active) mirror existing cloud proxy pattern**: apps/server/src/routes/org.ts: GET /api/org/list, GET /api/org/active, POST /api/org/set-active follow the same server-side proxy pattern as auth/usage/billing — read session token from SQLite via getSessionToken(), inject Bearer header, forward to cloud (/auth/organization/list, /get-full-organization, /set-active) via cloudJson() (retry + 401/429/5xx classification). Confirmed: every signed-in user has exactly one default org auto-created by the cloud; if useActiveOrganization returns null the UI just hides org display — no fallback-to-first-org logic was added. New route prefixes added to apiRouter must ALSO be added to TIMEOUT\_PREFIXES in index.ts or they skip the 30s timeout guard (missed initially for /api/org, fixed in review). See \[\[019faf91-ce07-7e39-a8f5-788aaef1692b]] for the underlying token-never-to-renderer boundary this pattern preserves.

<!-- lore:019fafcb-7600-78e4-9b92-efaf2220226e -->
* **Org feature: GET /api/org/list, /active, POST /set-active**: apps/server/src/routes/org.ts proxies 3 endpoints to Freestyle Cloud via cloudJson(): GET /list → cloud GET /auth/organization/list; GET /active (optional ?organizationId=) → cloud GET /auth/organization/get-full-organization; POST /set-active → cloud POST /auth/organization/set-active (validated by SetActiveOrgInput in packages/validations/src/auth.ts). Confirmed assumption: every signed-in user has exactly one org created by default, and Better Auth sets it as the session's activeOrganizationId automatically on session creation — no client-side fallback (e.g. picking first org when active is null) is needed. Renderer hooks in use-profile.ts: useActiveOrganization, useListOrganizations, useSetActiveOrganization (React Query). Org switcher UI in cloud-profile.tsx is a dropdown submenu, hidden entirely when user has only 1 org.

<!-- lore:019fbc45-72ed-727e-856b-9ab729be87ad -->
* **packages/utils/cloud-client.ts: shared runtime-agnostic Freestyle Cloud HTTP client**: PR #520 introduced packages/utils/src/cloud-client.ts, a shared HTTP client for Freestyle Cloud used by both apps/server (Node 22) and apps/mobile (Expo/RN) — runtime-agnostic, only uses global fetch + AbortSignal.timeout (no node:\*/undici/expo-\* imports). Callers inject getBaseUrl() (desktop: process.env; mobile: expo-constants) and getAuthHeaders() (desktop: Bearer token; mobile: session cookie). Retry logic only retries connection-level faults (request never reached server) — safe even for non-idempotent POSTs; deliberately excludes response-phase timeouts (UND\_ERR\_HEADERS\_TIMEOUT/UND\_ERR\_BODY\_TIMEOUT) and generic aborts since retrying could double-charge (e.g. double transcribe). UND\_ERR\_\* codes are undici-specific and never occur on React Native. This closes the earlier gap where apps/mobile's cloud calls had no centralized wrapper equivalent to server's cloudJson() \[\[019fb9e6-a611-7c15-97d5-ec1bf4dbed15]].

<!-- lore:019f4680-6d73-7d3f-bca8-7d4719085dda -->
* **packages/validations cleanup-tones.ts: 4 tone schemas, app assignments, all private**: \`packages/validations/src/cleanup-tones.ts\` (135 lines): exports \`cleanupPersonalToneSchema\` (polished/casual/very\_casual/off), \`cleanupWorkToneSchema\` (direct/friendly/formal/off), \`cleanupEmailToneSchema\` (casual/warm/formal/off), \`cleanupOverallToneSchema\` (casual/neutral/professional/off); 4 DEFAULT consts (all "off"); \`areAllCleanupTonesOff()\`; \`cleanupAppAssignmentSchema\` (match: lowercased max 120, label max 120, kind: app|site, destination); \`cleanupAppAssignmentsSchema\` (array max 200). Uses \`zod/v3\`. Package \`@freestyle-voice/validations\` is \`private:true\` — cannot be a dep of any publishable shared package.

<!-- lore:019f5b5e-9263-741a-bbd6-9722e2421246 -->
* **paste.ts settle times: native 300ms, legacy 500–600ms, env override available**: \`PASTE\_SETTLE\_MS\` (native): darwin/win32/linux = 300ms. \`PASTE\_SETTLE\_LEGACY\_MS\`: darwin=500ms, win32=600ms, linux=500ms. \`pasteSettleMs()\` checks \`FREESTYLE\_PASTE\_SETTLE\_MS\` env override first, then selects native vs legacy constant, fallback 500ms. \`pasteMac()\`: tries \`macos-fast-paste\` binary; exit code 2 → falls back to osascript \`keystroke "v" using {command down}\`; exit code ≠ 0 → throws. Paste operations are serialized via \`pasteChain\` promise chain in \`pasteIntoFocusedApp()\`.

<!-- lore:019fb9c8-445f-7f36-9bae-b6892e895f47 -->
