* **fix/llm-settings-type-and-timeout-constant**: Applied two fixes in apps/server/src/routes/models.ts: (1) Added Record\<string, string | undefined> annotation to the settings lookup to preserve type safety on key accesses. (2) Replaced the duplicated timeout literal with REGISTRY\_FETCH\_TIMEOUT\_MS constant (3000) for consistency and maintainability. Changes are backward compatible and all checks (typecheck, lint, server tests) pass.

<!-- lore:019fbc9b-3618-7c35-a0ab-730e1dc11a01 -->
* **Freestyle Cloud: languages\[] supersedes legacy language field**: In the Freestyle Cloud backend repo (/Users/am/dev/freestyle-voice/cloud, apps/server — distinct from the desktop's local embedded server), \`languages: string\[]\` is canonical everywhere: DB column \`member\_preferences.languages\` (json, nullable, no default — migration 0014 renamed it from singular \`language\`), validation (\`z.array(z.string().max(10)).max(5)\`), and transcribe/post-process/streaming payloads. Singular \`language\` survives only as \`@deprecated\` legacy input, coalesced into \`languages\` at every entry point (transcribe.ts, post-process.ts, stt-session.ts) via \`payloadLanguages ?? (legacyLanguage ? \[legacyLanguage] : undefined)\`, each tagged \`TODO(legacy): drop once all clients send languages\` (~1 month removal target). \`resolvePreferences()\` precedence: request payload wins when defined, falls back to stored member preference only when payload is undefined — never merged. \`languages\` uses PATCH replace semantics (\`JSON\_REPLACE\_FIELDS\`), unlike \`vocabulary\` which deep-merges.

<!-- lore:019fbc43-f5df-79a8-a31b-6d516c560ede -->
* **Migrated to shared client**: migrated to shared client.

<!-- lore:019f7c13-7d9d-7357-88f1-594f02b1f69b -->
* **Mobile icon library: migrated from hand-drawn glyphs (icons.tsx) to lucide-react-native**: Chose \`lucide-react-native\` (v1.25.0, same v1 major as desktop's \`lucide-react\`) over the hand-drawn SVG glyphs in \`apps/mobile/src/components/icons.tsx\`. Requires \`react-native-svg@15.15.4\` (SDK 57-pinned via \`expo install\`). Replacements: \`MicGlyph\`→\`Mic\` (size 30), \`StopGlyph\`→\`Square\` (filled, size 22), \`SettingsGlyph\`→\`Settings\`, \`"✓"\` text→\`Check\`. \`icons.tsx\` deleted (123 lines). Biome enforces import order: lucide imports sort before react/react-native in the file. Rejected keeping hand-drawn glyphs because they diverged visually from the desktop icon set.

<!-- lore:019fafc5-aaa2-7cc4-9b53-466e8cc15b44 -->
* **Mobile subscriptions: native IAP (expo-iap) chosen over Stripe**: Chose native In-App Purchase via expo-iap (raw StoreKit) over @stripe/stripe-react-native for iOS subscription sales because Apple mandates IAP for digital goods — Stripe is legally non-viable for this use case on iOS. Removed the Stripe web-checkout path entirely (deleted subscription.ts and the @better-auth/stripe client plugin). Trust model: client-side transaction is only finished after server-side receipt verification (POST /v1/iap/verify, implemented in Freestyle Cloud's separate service.freestylevoice.com repo) — unverified purchases are redelivered via restore rather than lost. Backend verification + store notification webhooks, and App Store Connect/Play Console product setup, are external dependencies not yet live; client shows "not available yet" until deployed. A fresh expo prebuild/EAS build is required after adding these native modules (ios/android dirs are gitignored).

<!-- lore:019efae2-43a6-73c7-8bae-eb57025f9ba6 -->
* **Package release versioning: manual explicit semver, OIDC auth, issue-label publish flow**: Chose manual explicit semver (over \`auto\` conventional-commits analysis) for SDK and plugin releases — user selects version explicitly at release time. Chose OIDC trusted publishing (over long-lived \`NPM\_TOKEN\`) for npm auth — requires one-time bootstrap publish per package. Chose reuse of existing issue-label publish flow (over a separate \`workflow\_run\` trigger) for consistency with the Electron app pipeline. \`scripts/bump-version.sh\` only bumps \`apps/electron\` — it is NOT invoked for package releases.

<!-- lore:019f6a23-92dd-7970-9d9c-70e15e80e588 -->
* **pluginSlug() has no hash suffix — distinct package names always get distinct slugs**: Chose to remove the hash suffix from \`pluginSlug()\` in \`packages/sdk/src/ui.ts\` over (1) migration (rename existing dirs on upgrade) and (2) fallback in \`discoverPackage()\` (try without hash). Rationale: distinct npm package names always produce distinct slugs after sanitization; hash-based collision prevention was over-engineering. New behavior: \`@freestyle-voice/plugin-audio-transcription\` → \`freestyle-voice-plugin-audio-transcription\` (no \`-\<hash>\` suffix). The \`shortHash()\` FNV-1a helper (lines 81-89) was removed as unused. Callers span: \`apps/server/src/routes/plugins.ts\`, \`apps/server/src/lib/plugins/installer.ts\`, \`apps/server/src/lib/plugins/ui-assets.ts\`, \`packages/sdk/src/loader.ts\`, and multiple test files — all expect the no-hash form.

<!-- lore:019fb9f9-a345-7684-bfc6-d8203a3bc60b -->
* **Proposed shared cloud-fetch helper: inject auth/baseUrl, don't hardcode desktop/mobile specifics**: Design for a runtime-agnostic \`cloudJson\<T>(path, opts)\` shared helper (candidate location: extend \`@freestyle-voice/validations\` or new \`@freestyle-voice/cloud-client\` package) to replace duplicated fetch boilerplate in both apps/server/src/lib/freestyle-cloud.ts and apps/mobile/src/lib/cloud/\*.ts. Key constraints: (1) auth differs — desktop uses Bearer token, mobile uses Cookie — so auth must be injected via a \`getAuthHeaders(): Record\<string,string>|null\` callback, not hardcoded; (2) URL resolution differs — desktop reads \`process.env.FREESTYLE\_CLOUD\_URL\`, mobile reads \`expo-constants\` — inject a \`baseUrl\`/\`cloudUrl()\` fn rather than importing expo-constants into the shared package; (3) org-slug caching differs (per-token on desktop vs single-slug on mobile) — keep app-side; (4) desktop's error taxonomy (FreestyleCloudAuthError/UsageError/RequestError + stale-socket retry) is richer than mobile's (only CloudAuthError) — the shared helper should adopt desktop's taxonomy. Both runtimes support fetch + AbortSignal.timeout, so a pure-fetch implementation works on both without axios/ky/undici.

<!-- lore:019faf56-de8f-71b7-b107-4dae92d9cf05 -->
* **Rejected catch-all cloud auth proxy — kept explicit per-endpoint routes in apps/server**: Considered replacing apps/server's individual cloud-auth route handlers (auth.ts, billing.ts) with a single catch-all proxy (\`auth.all('/proxy/\*', ...)\`) injecting the bearer token and forwarding to Freestyle Cloud. Rejected: only 2 of 10 endpoints (GET /usage, POST /auth/unlink-account) are pure forwards — the other 8 have local side effects (SQLite session writes, PostHog identify, default-provider switching on sign-in/out) or request/response transforms (hardcoded callback URLs, field mapping) a catch-all would lose or need hooks to preserve anyway. Kept explicit routes: ~15 lines each, full control over transforms/side effects/error handling via cloudJson()'s retry + error classification. If org support later adds many \*pure\* proxy endpoints, revisit a hybrid (explicit routes for side-effect endpoints + catch-all for pure ones).

<!-- lore:019faf89-ec0d-7a2a-85dc-781eb42e93b1 -->
* **Rejected catch-all cloud proxy — kept explicit per-route auth/usage/billing/org handlers**: Considered replacing apps/server's 11 auth/usage/billing routes with a generic \`ALL /api/auth/proxy/\*\` forwarder (read token, inject bearer, forward to cloud, return raw response) to cut boilerplate for future org endpoints. Rejected: only ~3-4 of 11 routes are pure proxies (accounts, unlink-account, usage); the rest (device/token, sign-out, profile) have essential local side effects — SQLite session persistence, default-model assignment, PostHog identity lifecycle — that a generic proxy would silently drop. A catch-all also loses cloudJson()'s retry/error-classification logic (401→AuthError, 429→UsageError, 5xx→RequestError) and returns cloud responses unfiltered to the renderer. Each new explicit route is only ~15 lines. Kept explicit-route pattern; new org endpoints (GET /org/list, GET /org/active, POST /org/set-active) follow it.

<!-- lore:019faff6-1737-7008-9f8e-fb070ce011d6 -->
* **Stripe rejected for iOS mobile subscriptions — Apple IAP mandate**: Chose expo-iap (native StoreKit/Google Play billing) over @stripe/stripe-react-native for apps/mobile subscription sales because Apple mandates In-App Purchase for digital goods — Stripe is legally non-viable for this use case on iOS. Removed the Stripe web-checkout path entirely: subscription.ts and the @better-auth/stripe client plugin were deleted from apps/mobile. Replaced with useProSubscription hook (cloud/use-pro-subscription.ts) wired into profile.tsx for purchase/restore/manage; out-of-credits prompts in dictation hooks now route to Profile instead of a web checkout link.

<!-- lore:019efae4-183a-7820-8482-7cf75c8ff499 -->
* **Switched from label-based routing (pkg:\* labels) to title-based routing — chosen because title is authoritative**: switched from label-based routing (pkg:\* labels) to title-based routing — chosen because title is authoritative,

### Feature

<!-- lore:019fb9b2-08da-79e4-8d1e-b7c75c70d7c6 -->
* **feat: restore Freestyle Cloud three-mode transcribe/cleanup**: Restored the three independent modes after #411 collapsed them: - Cloud transcription-only (voice=cloud, cleanup=off) - Cloud cleanup-only (voice=local, cleanup=cloud) - Combined (voice=cloud, cleanup=cloud) Server: restored \`CloudCleanupTones\`, \`appendCleanupFormFields()\`, \`mode\` param on \`transcribeWithFreestyleCloud()\`, \`postProcessWithFreestyleCloud()\`, \`/v2/post-process\` route, and \`Freestyle Cloud\` as a selectable LLM cleanup model. Streaming provider now returns \`mode:raw\` data. Renderer: pair-card collapses to unified view only when \*\*both\*\* sides are cloud; each side independently selectable otherwise. Tone page removed \`cloudVoiceActive\` short-circuit (wrong under three-mode). All plumbing (dictionaries, models, auth, settings) preserved. Verification: 212 server tests pass, electron + server typecheck clean, server build succeeds.

<!-- lore:019fb9aa-c24a-784e-bc8d-3425c8e78859 -->
* **Open model source button for proxy/TLS download failures**: Added 'Open model source' button for all proxy/TLS-class download failures (ProxyInterceptionError with sourceUrl or fallback HF URL). Server helper \`downloadErrorSourceUrl()\` in \`download-guard.ts\` returns the precise URL. State propagates via \`errorSourceUrl\` in \`ModelDownloadState\` through whisper/models.ts and mlx-asr/models.ts. Renderer shows button in model-list.tsx (row error + TierCard error) and model-setup-panel.tsx. i18n key \`onboarding.modelSetup.openModelSource\` added. Tests added in download-guard.test.ts. Button opens via \`window.api.openExternal()\` (preload already validates http/https).

### Fix

<!-- lore:019fb96f-4152-7275-95a7-18d4e55691de -->
* **Add initializing state handling in onHotkeyUp to prevent double-start**: When the hotkey is released while the pill is in the 'initializing' state (before mic access is granted), \`onHotkeyUp\` now calls \`preventDoubleStart()\` to ignore the release and avoid triggering a second concurrent recording attempt. This ensures the hold-to-record behavior remains correct during the initialization window.

<!-- lore:019fb96f-4140-7041-9f26-125ec8a148e5 -->
* **Linux CI: remove snap build target and fix artifact paths**: Removed \`snap\` from \`build.yml\` and \`electron-builder.yml\` to fix repeated \`FileNotFoundError\` in CI caused by missing \`@freestyle/\` subdirectory in dist. Changed \`artifactName\` from \`${name}\` to \`${productName}\` (and added \`appName\`/\`directories.output\`) so Linux outputs go to \`dist/Freestyle-\*.AppImage\` and \`dist/Freestyle-\*.deb\` without the invalid scoped subdirectory. This unblocks the Linux build while keeping AppImage and deb packaging intact.

<!-- lore:019fb96f-4148-71a5-a897-fdaabafe224e -->
* **Windows build: remove redundant typecheck step from build script**: Changed \`build:win\` script from \`npm run build\` (which ran \`typecheck\` and failed on pre-existing TS errors in \`typecheck:node\`) to \`electron-vite build\` directly, consistent with \`build:mac\` and \`build:linux\`. The shared typecheck step in CI (\`typecheck\`) remains as the gate, so this prevents spurious build failures unrelated to our changes.

### Gap

<!-- lore:019fb9c4-4a38-7803-ba58-86883820b740 -->
* **Plugin pipeline: missing error boundary in PluginPage**: PluginPage (plugin-page.tsx) renders PluginViewManager which can throw during render or lifecycle phases. There is no ErrorBoundary component wrapping the plugin UI, so plugin JS errors bubble up to the host app and can break the entire Electron window. Add an ErrorBoundary that catches plugin errors and renders a fallback UI with retry/uninstall options, and logs via captureException.

<!-- lore:019fb9c4-4a28-755e-b5e0-d8acb4f0d849 -->
* **Plugin pipeline: missing onUnmount cleanup hook**: The plugin pipeline defines hooks: event, config, beforeTranscribe, afterTranscribe, beforeCleanup, afterCleanup, beforeOutput. There is NO onUnmount hook defined. If a plugin needs to clean up resources when the plugin page unmounts (e.g., cancel timers, close WebWorkers, unsubscribe from streams), there is no canonical mechanism. The afterCleanup hook fires on the final text but not on UI unmount. This could cause resource leaks for plugins that allocate non-text resources. Add onUnmount to the Hooks interface and dispatch it when PluginPage unmounts in PluginViewManager.

<!-- lore:019fb9c4-4a41-7627-83e0-e333aafb599c -->
* **Plugin pipeline: missing plugin health/liveness monitoring**: PluginViewManager caches WebContentsView per slug/pageId but does not monitor whether the plugin's renderer process is still alive. If a plugin crashes or hangs, the cached view will show a frozen page and the host has no automatic recovery mechanism. Add a health ping (e.g., postMessage from plugin renderer) and a fallback UI that offers to reload the plugin page or report a crash.

<!-- lore:019fb9c4-4a49-765a-aca9-7f8da2a5e2f8 -->
* **Plugin pipeline: plugin CSS isolation / shadow DOM not enforced**: Plugin UI is rendered into a WebContentsView with no CSS encapsulation. Plugin styles can leak into the host app and vice versa, causing visual regressions. Consider using ShadowDOM or a scoped CSS strategy (e.g., CSS modules or a postload style reset) to isolate plugin styling. This is a medium-term gap.

### Gotcha

<!-- lore:019f2291-6849-7663-9bdf-441334b5371f -->
* **.lore.md uncommitted changes block branch checkout — commit on current branch first**: Trap: \`.lore.md\` is auto-updated by the lore system and frequently has uncommitted changes. When switching branches via \`gh pr checkout\` or \`git checkout\`, Git aborts if \`.lore.md\` differs between branches. The repo rules say NEVER stash \`.lore.md\` (it must stay version-controlled). Fix: commit the \`.lore.md\` change on the current branch first (\`git add .lore.md && git commit -m 'chore: update lore'\`), then switch branches. The commit is local-only until explicitly pushed. This is the correct resolution — stashing violates the lore rules.

<!-- lore:019fd03f-b22c-76c6-8a73-8af2b4a5e2a0 -->
* **@freestyle-voice/stt must be built via pkgroll before typecheck:node passes**: apps/electron's \`typecheck:node\` fails with TS2307 "Cannot find module '@freestyle-voice/stt'" across ~9 server files (llm/registry.ts, post-process.ts, remix-transform.ts, streaming providers, stream.ts, transcribe.ts) whenever the packages/stt workspace package hasn't been built yet. This looks like a real regression from whatever branch you're on, but it's just an unbuilt workspace dependency — fix by running packages/stt's build script (\`pkgroll --minify\`) once; unrelated to any specific PR's changes.

<!-- lore:019f657a-6225-7e48-b6e3-43bf01fe7cfb -->
* **@hono/node-server v2 WebSocket: must pass WebSocketServer to serve() explicitly**: The Network tab in Settings shows duplicate rows for \`network\_proxy\_url\` and \`network\_ca\_cert\_path\` because both the proxy/CA section and the legacy endpoint list each render their own GETs. The legacy endpoints (\`/api/settings/network\_proxy\_url\`, \`/api/settings/network\_ca\_cert\_path\`) were kept for backward compatibility and also queried by \`config.freestyle.json\` polling, causing 4–6 requests on page open. Consolidate by removing the legacy GET routes or deduplicating via a shared cache key; prefer the explicit proxy/CA section as the single source of truth.

<!-- lore:019f57ee-34b5-79e3-8a46-4bb376146ae4 -->
* **@hookform/resolvers@5 imports zod/v4/core but declares no zod peer dep — fix with pnpm packageExtensions**: Trap: \`@hookform/resolvers@5.4.0\` imports \`zod/v4/core\` internally but declares no zod peer dependency. Under pnpm isolated layout it resolves ambiently to a transitive \`zod@3.x\` which lacks the \`./v4/core\` export → Vite build fails with \`\[commonjs--resolver] Missing "./v4/core" specifier in "zod" package\`. Adding \`zod@^4\` as a direct devDep to the consuming package is insufficient — pnpm won't inject it into resolvers' node\_modules without an explicit peer declaration. Fix: add to \`pnpm-workspace.yaml\`: \`packageExtensions: { "@hookform/resolvers@\*": { dependencies: { zod: "^4.4.3" } } }\` then re-run \`pnpm install\`. This forces pnpm to symlink zod@4 into resolvers' own node\_modules. Confirmed fixed in PR \`9e1e376\`.

<!-- lore:019fcc32-c446-75ae-a86d-e21e785a9061 -->
* **actions/checkout@v6 pwn-request protection blocks pull\_request\_target fork checkout**: PR #541 CI failure: \`.github/workflows/changelog-preview.yml\` uses \`pull\_request\_target\` (runs with base-repo GITHUB\_TOKEN/secrets) and calls the reusable workflow \`getsentry/craft/.github/workflows/changelog-preview.yml@v2\`. That workflow's internal \`actions/checkout@v6\` refuses to check out fork PR code by default (new pwn-request vulnerability protection), erroring instead of skipping. Looks fixable via \`allow-unsafe-pr-checkout: true\`, but that flag lives inside Craft's reusable workflow — not settable from this repo without an upstream Craft change or inlining the workflow. Fix applied: add \`if: github.event.pull\_request.head.repo.full\_name == github.repository\` to the job so it's skipped (not failed) for external fork PRs — changelog preview is a maintainer convenience, not required for fork contributors to merge.

<!-- lore:019f2c4b-084a-7112-b7a1-832ec83fcff5 -->
* **After rebase adding new deps, run pnpm install before tests — missing package causes import failure**: Trap: after rebasing a branch that adds a new npm dependency (e.g. \`undici\`), tests fail with \`Cannot find package 'undici'\` — looks like a broken import or wrong package name. Fix: run \`pnpm install\` first; the lockfile update from the rebased commits isn't automatically applied to \`node\_modules\`. Observed in PR #388 rebase: 3 test files failed until \`pnpm install\` resolved the missing package in 3.8s, after which all 22 test files passed.

<!-- lore:019f4680-6d7b-7aba-bd5e-551122f532d9 -->
* **Always treat whisper/mlx status queries as stale (staleTime: 0): User directive (annotated in \`use-models.ts:162,177\`):**: Always treat whisper/mlx status queries as stale (staleTime: 0): User directive (annotated in \`use-models.ts:162,177\`): 'Status is volatile during downloads — always treat as stale.' Whisper and MLX model status queries must always use \`staleTime: 0\` — never cache these as fresh. Status changes rapidly during downloads and must always reflect the latest server state.

<!-- lore:019fc267-36a7-77f5-9634-85ea80032b7c -->
* **Android CI: assembleDebug/packageDebug hangs ~14min then fails IncrementalSplitterRunnable**: Trap: Android CI job "Mobile (Android build)" hangs for ~14 minutes with no log output after \`:sentry\_react-native:assembleDebug\` starts, then fails at \`:app:packageDebug\` with a generic \`IncrementalSplitterRunnable\` error and no \`Caused by\` trace — looks like a build/code issue, but the identical commit's PR run passed this same job. Suspected root cause: CI runner resource exhaustion (disk space or OOM) during APK packaging, not a code regression. Fix: re-run via \`gh run rerun --failed\`; consider adding a disk-cleanup step before the Android assembleDebug/packaging step (proposed, not yet implemented as of Aug 2026).

<!-- lore:019fcc2b-da19-7be6-baf2-ec687e5cf132 -->
* **app.tsx captureBarLines: barLinesRef never cleared on detach**: In apps/electron/src/renderer/src/pages/app.tsx, \`captureBarLines()\` sets \`barLinesRef.current\` on attach but deliberately never clears it on detach. This looks like a ref-cleanup bug (should null out on unmount) but is intentional: the waveform moves between two surfaces (capsule and remix card), and a detach firing after the new element's attach would blank the ref and freeze the visible bar row. Holding a stale detached element is harmless — the draw loop just writes attributes nobody renders until the next attach lands, so the bars never visibly jump when a surface takes over. Merge note (PR #534): main added a \`\[data-bars] line\` selector (bars wrapped in a sibling \`\<g data-bars="">\` next to the flatline) — when reconciling, keep main's selector but preserve remix's attach-only-never-clear behavior.

<!-- lore:019fb9e6-a61e-781f-b477-9a463c080e56 -->
* **apps/mobile cloud-fetch files use raw fetch(), no shared wrapper like cloudJson**: apps/mobile/src/lib/cloud/\*.ts (config.ts, preferences.ts, usage.ts, cloud-config.ts, profile.ts) implements its own \`cloudUrl()\` resolver (mirrors server's \`freestyleCloudUrl()\`) but has NO shared fetch wrapper equivalent to \`cloudJson()\` — every file calls raw \`fetch()\` directly with no retry/error-classification. This is an inconsistency vs. apps/server's centralized pattern, not an intentional design choice. Before adding new mobile cloud calls, check whether consolidating onto a shared wrapper is in scope rather than adding another one-off fetch.

<!-- lore:019f2e88-285e-7702-95ca-95863e35b158 -->
* **autoUpdater.checkForUpdatesAndNotify() leaks downloadPromise rejection — use runUpdateCheck() instead**: Trap: \`checkForUpdatesAndNotify()\` looks like the canonical high-level API for update checks. But with \`autoDownload: true\`, it internally drops \`downloadPromise\` in a \`void ...then()\` with no \`.catch()\` (electron-updater's \`AppUpdater.js\`), so transient CDN failures (e.g. expired 403 on release asset) leak as unhandled rejections and get reported as false crashes. Fix: use \`runUpdateCheck()\` (defined in \`index.ts\`) which calls \`checkForUpdates()\` directly, swallows \`result?.downloadPromise?.catch(() => {})\`, and logs check failures via \`log.warn\`. The \`update-downloaded\` handler already shows the completion notification, making \`checkForUpdatesAndNotify()\` redundant.

<!-- lore:019fcc2b-da21-76f5-b99f-6731dcee4b38 -->
* **BarMode 'connecting' renamed to 'listening' in app.tsx**: The \`BarMode\` union value \`"connecting"\` was renamed to \`"listening"\` on main (apps/electron/src/renderer/src/pages/app.tsx). Old code paths calling \`startBarAnimation("connecting")\` (e.g. remix's mic-failure fallback for idle bars) must be updated to \`"listening"\` — main's \`listening\` mode renders the same flat/idle bar row intent ("pill is awake but has nothing to show yet"). Searching for \`"connecting"\` as a BarMode value will not find current code; search for \`listening\` instead.

<!-- lore:019fcbf0-d163-7701-9b83-34ddda747313 -->
* **BarMode value renamed "connecting" → "listening" (main branch)**: Main renamed the \`BarMode\` value \`"connecting"\` → \`"listening"\` in apps/electron's pill animation code (\`startBarAnimation\`), changing semantics from a slow breathing 'awaiting audio' animation to a flat/idle bar row. Callers still passing \`"connecting"\` (e.g. remix-prototype's mic-failure fallback) fail with TS2345. When merging/rebasing older branches, remap \`"connecting"\` calls to \`"listening"\` — the flat-idle rendering is intentional ("idle bars so the card doesn't look broken"), not a bug.

<!-- lore:019faf4f-7e28-7e9a-8555-40415c3c1b24 -->
* **Better Auth unlinkAccount: lockout protection is server-only, add explicit UI guard too**: authClient.unlinkAccount({ providerId }) is built into better-auth and by default blocks unlinking the user's last linked account (prevents lockout) unless the server sets allowUnlinkingAll:true. It can target a specific accountId when multiple accounts share a provider. Also note: linking a provider with updateUserInfoOnLink can copy profile fields (name, image) but never changes the user's email/emailVerified — linking cannot rebind account identity. Trap: relying on server-side lockout protection alone gives no UX feedback — always add an explicit client-side guard (disable the 'Disconnect' button + tooltip) when it's the user's only sign-in method, matching the pattern used in apps/electron/src/renderer/src/pages/profile.tsx.

<!-- lore:019f40a9-8d1a-729b-a5e8-9956cd182333 -->
* **Biome noLabelWithoutControl fires on \<label> wrapping Radix Switch — use \<div> instead**: Trap: wrapping a Radix \`Switch\` in a \`\<label>\` element looks correct for accessibility (label → control association). Biome's \`noLabelWithoutControl\` rule fires because the \`\<label>\` has no \`htmlFor\` and Radix Switch renders its own internal label handling. Fix: replace \`\<label>\` with \`\<div>\` — Radix Switch manages its own interaction and accessibility without needing an outer \`\<label>\`.

<!-- lore:019f1407-8c5e-7fe0-8bb6-1323c31da778 -->
* **buildLanguageBlock() returned empty string for auto-detect — LLM translated non-English speech to English**: Trap: \`buildLanguageBlock(undefined)\` and \`buildLanguageBlock('auto')\` both returned \`''\`, so the LLM cleanup prompt had no 'do not translate' constraint for auto-detect sessions. This looks safe because the preset body already contains a weaker 'Keep the output in the same language' rule — but that rule is buried and insufficient to override English-biased LLM behavior. Fix (commit 5db8118): \`buildLanguageBlock()\` now returns \`AUTO\_LANGUAGE\_CONSTRAINT\` for both \`undefined\`/empty and \`'auto'\` cases, explicitly instructing the LLM to preserve source language and script. \`prompts.test.ts\` updated: test renamed from 'returns nothing for auto-detect' to assert the constraint is present.

<!-- lore:019f6b7b-1b9f-73c0-8e6d-b85c752fc396 -->
* **buildPluginLlm() throws on freestyle-cloud provider — expected, not a bug**: Trap: \`buildPluginLlm()\` in \`apps/server/src/lib/plugins/llm.ts:19\` calls \`createChatModel(llm.provider, llm.model\_id)\` on every dictation via \`createHookApi()\`. When cleanup provider is \`freestyle-cloud\`, \`providers.ts:60\` throws \`Unsupported provider: freestyle-cloud\` because Freestyle Cloud is a remote HTTP endpoint, not a locally-instantiable AI SDK chat model. Error is caught, logged at debug level, returns \`undefined\`. This is expected behavior — \`freestyle-cloud\` is intentionally absent from \`registry.ts\` (only openai, groq, anthropic, google, mistral, local-llm). The debug log \`plugin LLM capability unavailable: Unsupported provider: freestyle-cloud\` appears ~twice per streaming dictation (once per \`createHookApi()\` call). Do NOT suppress this log — it is intentional signal. Plugins guard with \`if (ctx.llm)\` and must not assume LLM capability is present.

<!-- lore:019fd060-98ef-7ff6-b645-3ef02d6c07c2 -->
* **buildRemixAgentSystem must gate web\_search/image\_search via hasWebSearch capability flag**: Trap: REMIX\_AGENT\_PROMPT unconditionally instructed the model to call web\_search/image\_search for facts, and it looked safe because the prompt is deliberately "mirrored byte-for-byte" across cloud and BYOK for parity. But parity of the PROMPT TEXT doesn't imply parity of TOOL AVAILABILITY — web\_search/image\_search are Freestyle Cloud server-only tools; the renderer's client-side dispatcher (\`executeTool()\` in remix-chat.tsx) has no case for them, and BYOK's \`runRemixAgentLocally\` only registers \`remixClientTools()\`. Result: BYOK runs following the prompt hit \`{ok:false, reason:"unknown tool: web\_search"}\`, burning a step of the 16-step budget then recovering from memory (contradicting the prompt's own "never edit from memory of a stale snapshot" rule). Fix (PR #546): \`buildRemixAgentSystem(context, capabilities)\` now takes \`RemixAgentCapabilities({hasWebSearch})\`; cloud passes \`true\` (byte-identical output preserved), BYOK passes \`false\`, stripping the GATHER web\_search clause, the \`## Search\` section, and the image-insert recipe.

<!-- lore:019f65b0-dcea-7ed4-bfb3-77379861925f -->
* **cancelRecording never calls streamerRef.current?.cancel() — server-side stream left open**: Trap: \`cancelRecording\` in \`app.tsx\` (lines 1031–1037) handles the batch path cleanup but never calls \`streamerRef.current?.cancel()\`. Looks complete because it stops the MediaRecorder and releases the stream. Fix: add \`streamerRef.current?.cancel()\` inside \`cancelRecording\` so the server-side WebSocket/streaming session is torn down on cancel, not left open until timeout.

<!-- lore:019fcc60-20e1-7ef5-9297-bf14c873403e -->
* **captureBarLines must not clear barLinesRef on detach**: In apps/electron/src/renderer/src/pages/app.tsx, the ref callback \`captureBarLines\` (attached to the SVG \`\<line>\` bar elements, selector \`\[data-bars] line\`) must only \*set\* \`barLinesRef.current\` on attach — never clear it to null/\[] on detach. The waveform moves between two surfaces (pill capsule and remix card); if detach fires after the new element's attach, clearing the ref blanks it and freezes the visible bar row. Holding a stale/detached element is harmless — the draw loop just writes attributes nobody renders until the next attach lands. This looks like a memory-leak risk (stale DOM ref) but the fix (clearing on detach, which main's version did) actually breaks the cross-surface handoff. Recurred across 20+ sessions touching this component.

<!-- lore:019fcc55-0737-750f-80c1-a03ea29e5a1b -->
* **captureBarLines never clears barLinesRef on detach (waveform surface handoff)**: In app.tsx, \`barLinesRef.current\` is set on attach but deliberately never cleared on detach. This looks like a leak/bug because you'd expect refs to null out on unmount, but the waveform moves between two surfaces (capsule and remix card), and clearing on a detach that fires after the new element's attach would blank the visible row and freeze it. Holding a stale/detached element is harmless — the draw loop writes attributes that nobody renders until the next attach lands. When merging branches that touch this selector, combine main's broader \`\[data-bars] line\` selector (needed because main wraps bars in a sibling \`\<g data-bars>\`) with this never-clear-on-detach behavior rather than reintroducing a clear-on-null-svg branch.

<!-- lore:019fcbf0-d15d-743a-921f-a13ddac0c33e -->
* **captureBarLines: never clear ref on detach (waveform handoff capsule↔remix-card)**: In app.tsx's \`captureBarLines\`, whether to clear \`barLinesRef.current\` on detach is a deliberate design point, not an oversight. Remix's version only \*sets\* the ref on attach and never clears on detach, because the waveform SVG moves between two surfaces (capsule and remix card) — clearing on detach could blank the ref and freeze the visible row if the old surface detaches after the new one attaches. Main's version cleared to \`\[]\` when svg is null, which looks safer but breaks the capsule↔remix-card handoff. Merged resolution: use main's \`\[data-bars] line\` selector (needed for main's \`\<g data-bars="">\` wrapper) but keep remix's never-clear-on-detach behavior. Any refactor of bar-line capture must not add clear-on-detach.

<!-- lore:019fb9ae-8437-79a1-a62d-782187096753 -->
* **CGEvent tap suppresses compound hotkey KEY\_DOWN events**: The CGEvent tap that suppresses compound hotkeys (to prevent dead-key insertion) also swallows the key event before NSEvent.addGlobalMonitorForEvents(.keyDown) sees it. That monitor is the only source of KEY\_DOWN:\<name> events emitted to Electron. Result: suppressed hotkeys never trigger dictation. Fix: emit KEY\_DOWN:\<name> from the CGEvent tap before returning nil for suppressed events. No duplicate risk — non-suppressed events are handled by the NSEvent monitor as before. Confirmed via KeyCastr showing only ⌥ when hotkey is pressed.

<!-- lore:019fcbfb-ee5a-7137-b3f7-573040580d17 -->
* **changelog-preview.yml fails on fork PRs — checkout@v6 pwn-request protection**: \`.github/workflows/changelog-preview.yml\` uses \`pull\_request\_target\` and calls Craft's reusable workflow (\`getsentry/craft/.github/workflows/changelog-preview.yml@v2\`) with \`secrets: inherit\`. Craft's internal \`actions/checkout@v6\` step refuses to check out fork PR code under \`pull\_request\_target\` (pwn-request protection) — looks like a repo misconfig but is intentional: pull\_request\_target runs with base-repo secrets/token, so checking out untrusted fork code would leak them. The opt-out flag \`allow-unsafe-pr-checkout: true\` lives inside Craft's reusable workflow, NOT settable from this repo, so it can't be fixed locally without an upstream Craft change or inlining the workflow. Fix applied: added \`if: github.event.pull\_request.head.repo.full\_name == github.repository\` to the job so it's skipped (not failed) for external fork PRs — changelog preview is a maintainer convenience, not required for merging.

<!-- lore:019fcbd7-f7ae-7104-82ff-70aa9e49e0cf -->
* **changelog-preview.yml pull\_request\_target blocks fork PR checkout (actions/checkout@v6)**: \`.github/workflows/changelog-preview.yml\` uses \`pull\_request\_target\` (runs with base repo's GITHUB\_TOKEN/secrets) and delegates to reusable workflow \`getsentry/craft/.github/workflows/changelog-preview.yml@v2\`. That workflow's internal \`actions/checkout@v6\` refuses to check out fork PR code by default (pwn-request protection) unless \`allow-unsafe-pr-checkout: true\` is set — but that flag lives inside Craft's reusable workflow, not settable from this repo, so it can't be opted into without an upstream Craft change. Fix applied: added an \`if\` guard comparing \`github.event.pull\_request.head.repo.full\_name != github.repository\` to skip the job entirely for external fork PRs (shows as 'skipped', not 'failed', so doesn't block merge). Changelog preview is a maintainer convenience only — fork contributors don't need it.

<!-- lore:019f6ad6-921f-7224-a369-9de486f1ea85 -->
* **Client: fire pre-warm on record start**: In \`apps/electron/src/renderer/src/pages/app.tsx\`, \`startRecording\` now fires a fire-and-forget \`POST /api/transcribe/pre-warm\` immediately after requesting mic access. This warms whisper or MLX in parallel with user speaking, reducing first-submit latency. Server decides whether warming is needed (no-op for cloud/BYOK).

<!-- lore:019fc2e5-f0fe-7f9a-a966-29b6ee26ba25 -->
* **Cloud DO translate guard uses post-fallback resolved languages**: In the Freestyle Cloud streaming Durable Object (stt-session.ts), \`translate\` is enabled only when \`this.languages.length === 1\`, where \`this.languages\` is the POST-FALLBACK resolved list (payload languages ?? stored member\_preferences languages), not the raw request payload. This lets desktop/mobile drop \`languages\` from the streaming \`start\` message while still sending \`translate\` unconditionally from the local setting — the DO's fallback still resolves to a single language when appropriate. Gotchas: (1) if the stored language is the Hinglish alias "hi-en", \`normalizeLanguages\` expands it to \["hi","en"] (length 2), silently disabling translate; (2) if the D1 read of stored prefs fails, it fails open to null, making \`this.languages\` undefined and disabling translate with no error surfaced.

<!-- lore:019fc2ee-7516-7e07-81b5-0245bc10d617 -->
* **Cloud v2 schema asymmetry: customPrompt .optional() vs appContext .nullish()**: Freestyle Cloud's v2 schemas mix \`.optional()\` and \`.nullish()\` inconsistently. \`postProcessV2BodySchema.customPrompt\` is \`.optional()\` only — sending \`null\` fails validation, so callers must use \`value || undefined\`, never \`?? null\`. \`appContext\` is \`.nullish()\` and accepts \`null\` fine — using \`||\` there would be wrong too since empty string is valid. Also \`/v2/transcribe\` receives \`appAssignments\` as a JSON-encoded string (multipart form), while \`/v2/post-process\` receives the same field as a real array (JSON body) — same logical field, different wire encoding per endpoint. This is flagged in code as 'a recurring source of payload bugs' — always check the exact per-field schema modifier before choosing \`|| undefined\` vs \`?? null\` when building cloud payloads.

<!-- lore:019fc2e5-f10e-7f82-83d7-f275caaa6ef5 -->
* **Cloud v2 transcribe vs post-process payload encoding asymmetry**: Freestyle Cloud's two v2 endpoints encode the same logical fields differently: \`/v2/transcribe\` is multipart FormData, so \`appAssignments\`/\`languages\`/\`vocabulary\` must be JSON-encoded STRINGS (parsed server-side via parseCleanupAppAssignments etc.); \`/v2/post-process\` is native JSON, so these fields arrive as real arrays/objects — no string parsing. Also \`customPrompt\` on post-process must be passed as \`|| undefined\` (not \`?? null\`) because \`postProcessV2BodySchema\` uses \`.optional()\` not \`.nullish()\`, while \`appContext\` on the same schema uses \`.nullish()\` and does accept null. This mismatch looks like it should be uniform since both endpoints serve the same cleanup config, but mixing conventions (sending null customPrompt, forgetting to JSON.stringify appAssignments for transcribe) is documented in cloud-repo lore as 'a recurring source of payload bugs.' Always check the specific v2 Zod schema before adding/removing a field on either endpoint's payload builder.

<!-- lore:019f1507-12d2-751f-9992-27c7faca6415 -->
* **cloudJson throws plain Error with no status — cloud 5xx silently captured as app defect**: \`cloudJson\` in \`freestyle-cloud.ts\` now throws \`FreestyleCloudRequestError(status, detail)\` (not plain \`Error\`) on non-OK non-401 non-429 responses. \`FreestyleCloudUsageError\` (429) is never reported as an app defect. \`FreestyleCloudRequestError\` (5xx) and transient network errors are never captured as app defects — callers gate \`captureException\` with \`isTransientCloudError(err)\`. \`isTransientCloudError\` walks \`.cause\` chain via seen-set loop; returns true for \`FreestyleCloudRequestError\` with \`status >= 500\`, any node with a code in \`TRANSIENT\_NETWORK\_CODES\`, or \`name === 'TimeoutError'/'AbortError'\`. \`RETRIABLE\_CONNECTION\_CODES\` is a narrower subset (excludes response-phase timeouts) used by \`isRetriableConnectionError\` for the retry loop. \`cloudJson\` retries up to \`CLOUD\_FETCH\_ATTEMPTS=2\` times with \`CLOUD\_RETRY\_DELAY\_MS=150\` on retriable connection errors only.

<!-- lore:019f1697-9d41-71a8-8c6a-4b0158de1365 -->
* **commitRecording restore guard must use duckingPromiseRef, not \_audioPlaybackMode**: Trap: guarding \`restoreSystemAudioSafely()\` in \`commitRecording\` with \`if (\_audioPlaybackMode !== 'off')\` looks correct — why restore if ducking is disabled? But \`\_audioPlaybackMode\` is mutable module state; if the user toggles duck/pause → off \*during\* an active recording, the commit skips restore and strands system volume at ~15% (\`DUCKED\_VOLUME\`). Fix: gate on \`duckingPromiseRef.current\` instead — truthy only when this specific session actually started ducking. \`restoreSystemAudioSafely\` already awaits the ref before calling IPC restore, so the guard is simply \`if (duckingPromiseRef.current)\`. Fixed in commit \`eaf9c07\`.

<!-- lore:019f6a4a-7d22-74c9-8cdf-3770614bbfbc -->
* **contextBridge strips Response prototype getters — plugin bridge api() must return plain object**: Trap: \`plugin-bridge.ts\` returning \`fetch(path, init)\` directly looks correct — the \`Response\` is a valid object. But \`contextBridge.exposeInMainWorld\` serializes only own enumerable properties; \`Response.ok\`, \`.status\`, \`.statusText\` are prototype getters, so the serialized result is \`{}\`. Plugin UI code sees \`res.ok === undefined\` (falsy) and throws \`'server returned undefined'\`. Fix: eagerly consume the body in the preload world and return a plain serializable object with \`ok\`, \`status\`, \`statusText\`, \`headers\`, and function properties \`json()\`/\`text()\`/\`arrayBuffer()\` that decode the pre-read \`ArrayBuffer\`. \`contextBridge\` wraps functions as proxies so callable methods in the returned object survive the boundary. Affects all plugin UI pages using \`window.freestyle.api()\`.

<!-- lore:019efae2-43a2-72d9-b1d1-b36349845d80 -->
* **Craft npm workspace mode bumps ALL packages to same version — cannot use for independent versioning**: Trap: Craft's npm target supports \`workspaces: true\` with \`includeWorkspaces\`/\`excludeWorkspaces\` filters, which looks like it enables per-package independent versioning from a single root \`.craft.yml\`. Fix: workspace mode still publishes all included packages at the SAME version under a single release pipeline. For truly independent versions, each package needs its own \`.craft.yml\` in its subdirectory and a separate workflow using the \`path\` input. Craft source even has a \`// TODO support multiple configuration files\` comment confirming this is a known limitation.

<!-- lore:019fcbe2-8b7b-7ec5-91bb-a5c414d9a000 -->
* **CSS enter transition doesn't fire when data-show is true at mount**: Trap: a component mounted by React with a CSS-transition-driving attribute (e.g. \`data-show="true"\`) already set on first paint looks like it should animate in — but CSS transitions only fire on a \*change\* to an attribute/property, never on its initial value. Fix (used for the voice pill in apps/electron/src/renderer/src/pages/app.tsx): mount with the attribute false/absent, hold for one committed frame, then flip it to true to trigger the transition. Applies to any data-attribute-driven CSS transition in this codebase, not just the pill. Fixed in PR #541 (voice-pill-motion).

<!-- lore:019fcbff-5d7f-7caa-89e8-953597400088 -->
* **CSS enter transition never fires if data-show is true on first React paint**: Trap: setting \`data-show="true"\` on mount (so a CSS transition can key off the attribute) looks correct, but React mounts the subtree with the attribute already true, so the browser never sees an attribute change and the transition doesn't fire on first paint. Fix used in the voice pill (apps/electron/src/renderer/src/pages/app.tsx): hold \`data-show="false"\` for one committed frame, then flip it true — guarantees a real transition the browser detects and animates (also enables the right-to-left bar stagger). Applies to any enter-transition gated by a data-attribute set at mount time.

<!-- lore:019fcc1b-9630-795e-9a38-244c6af14bef -->
* **CSS enter transition never fires when React mounts with final state already set**: Trap: adding a CSS enter transition (e.g. \`data-show="true"\` triggering opacity/scale via stylesheet) looks sufficient, but if React's first commit already renders the element with the 'shown' attribute set, there's no attribute change for the browser to transition from — CSS transitions don't fire on first paint. Fix (voice pill PR #541): hold the component in the initial/hidden state for one committed frame, then flip to shown on a subsequent frame/tick so the browser sees an actual attribute transition. Applies to any enter-animation gated by an attribute/class set synchronously at mount.

<!-- lore:019fcc78-812d-7e14-a323-8fe177878b1a -->
* **CSS enter transitions don't fire when data-show is pre-set at mount**: Trap: setting \`data-show="true"\` (or any transition-triggering attribute/class) on a React subtree at initial mount looks like it should animate in, since the CSS transition rule exists — but browsers don't transition on first paint, so the element just appears in its final state with no animation. Fixed in voice-pill motion (apps/electron/src/renderer/src/pages/app.tsx, PR #541) by holding the false/initial state for one committed frame, then flipping it on a subsequent render so the transition has a starting point to animate from.

<!-- lore:019fcc03-0942-76dc-8a4b-eaf8781be723 -->
* **CSS transitions skip when data-show=true on first mount paint**: Trap: React can mount a component with \`data-show="true"\` already set on the DOM node, so a CSS transition gated on that attribute never fires — it looks correct because the 'right' attribute value is set, but the browser needs two distinct paints (false→true) to detect a state change and animate. Fix: hold the initial render at \`data-show="false"\` for one committed frame, then flip to true, so the transition actually runs. Found/fixed in PR #541 (voice pill arrival animation, apps/electron/src/renderer/src/pages/app.tsx), which also staggers bars right-to-left via an index param in \`BAR\_X\_POSITIONS.map()\`.

<!-- lore:019fd03f-b1a4-7ff9-8fa3-5408cb99ed9b -->
* **Dead-code claims: verify via knip, not grep (preview-rail.tsx false negative)**: While reviewing PR #544, a grep for "preview-rail\\|PreviewRail" missed the actual consumer import (message-scroller.tsx imports it via a "motion/..." subpath), leading to an incorrect claim that preview-rail.tsx (332 lines) and use-hover-capable.ts (23 lines) were dead code. Running \`knip\` (already configured in this repo, zero unused-files findings) proved both files ARE reachable via pill.tsx → message-scroller.tsx → remix-chat.tsx. Fix: when assessing whether newly-added/vendored code is dead, always run knip's entry-point graph rather than trusting manual grep — grep misses subpath/aliased import variants.

<!-- lore:019fb9b0-1965-7738-995e-c59e34aeca23 -->
* **dev:ios --device fails with cocoapods error due to stale ios/ project**: The \`dev:ios --device\` flow runs \`turbo ios\` → \`expo run:ios --device\` → \`pod install\`. It fails with: \`\`\` \[Xcodeproj] Consistency issue: no parent for object \`FreestyleKeyboard.appex\`: \`Copy Files\`, \`Embed App Extensions\` \`\`\` Root cause: the on-disk \`ios/\` was generated by an older version of \`withKeyboardExtension.js\` that added a duplicate "Embed App Extensions" copy-files phase, causing the same PBXBuildFile to appear in two phases. The current plugin is correct and no longer adds that phase. Fix: regenerate the native project cleanly: \`\`\` npx expo prebuild --clean -p ios \`\`\` Then \`pod install\` succeeds (✔ Installed CocoaPods, 114 pods). The regenerated project has a single "Copy Files" phase with one build file and the \`.appex\` properly parented. Verification: \`grep -A5 "PBXCopyFilesBuildPhase" ios/Freestyle.xcodeproj/project.pbxproj\` shows one phase and one build file entry.

<!-- lore:019f5b6a-ef18-7cbe-b33d-09e4f04c3bb7 -->
* **Dictionary/vocabulary import: add CSV/TSV support**: Extend the existing JSON import for Dictionary and Vocabulary to also accept CSV and TSV formats. Provide a small inline converter in the import UI and validate via \`@freestyle-voice/validations\`. This makes it easy to paste word lists from any competitor app (e.g., Anki, spreadsheets) without requiring JSON. Keep the existing JSON import for programmatic use. Confidence: 0.9 — low risk, consistent with existing JSON import and validations package.

<!-- lore:019f6a1a-df33-7b88-b735-fa48e00a7ded -->
* **discoverPlugins() dedup is by dir field — pluginSlug mismatch causes duplicate entries**: Trap: \`discoverPlugins()\` in \`apps/server/src/lib/plugins/ui-assets.ts:50-85\` deduplicates by \`dir\` field (not slug or name). Phase 1 walks \`plugins\` setting via \`discoverPackage()\` (Node resolution then \`\<localPluginsDir>/\<pluginSlug(specifier)>/package.json\`); Phase 2 scans local plugins dir via \`discoverLocalDir()\` (all subdirs). If \`link-plugin.mjs\` creates a dir with a different name than \`pluginSlug()\` produces (SDK appends 7-char FNV-1a base36 hash; script does NOT), both phases find different dirs for the same plugin → duplicate entries (one normal, one 'missing'). Fix: ensure \`link-plugin.mjs\` dir naming exactly mirrors \`packages/sdk/src/ui.ts:105-113\` including the \`shortHash(name)\` suffix.

<!-- lore:019faf56-de96-7005-9b4c-9feb4fd06e35 -->
* **DropdownMenuItem card hover rounding clashes with adjacent separator**: Trap: a DropdownMenuItem styled as a card (rounded-xl) sitting directly above a DropdownMenuSeparator shows a rounded corner poking into the separator on hover/focus, because the item's full border-radius applies on all corners. Fix: add \`rounded-b-none\` to the item so the bottom edge stays square where it meets the separator. Seen in apps/electron/src/renderer/src/components/cloud-profile.tsx (user card DropdownMenuItem, line ~158). Applies to any dropdown item adjacent to a separator that uses full corner rounding.

<!-- lore:019fb22f-638b-7835-b614-f7f4e14af1e6 -->
* **DropdownMenuSubContent Portal wrap alone doesn't fix z-index stacking — check DOM sibling stacking contexts**: Trap: a Radix \`DropdownMenuSub\` submenu appearing invisible/behind page content looks like a missing \`Portal\` wrap (since \`DropdownMenuContent\` uses Portal but \`DropdownMenuSubContent\` in dropdown-menu.tsx didn't) — but adding the Portal did NOT fix it when the symptom is 'submenu appears but behind content' (a stacking-context issue, not a missing-portal issue). Root cause: a DOM sibling wrapper (e.g. \`glass-content\` in shell.tsx) has \`relative\` positioning, creating its own stacking context, and paints after the body-portaled dropdown in DOM order despite the dropdown's \`z-50\`, because the sibling itself had no explicit z-index (implicit order wins). Fix: give the competing sibling wrapper an explicit lower \`z-index\` (e.g. \`z-0\`) so portaled \`z-50\` content reliably paints above it. Diagnostic tip: always ask the user to describe the exact symptom (invisible/clipped vs appears-behind) before choosing between a Portal fix and a z-index fix — they require different solutions.

<!-- lore:019f0340-6c29-7aef-ba9a-fcc1fd8ed21f -->
* **DRY: use canonical defaults from validations**: DEFAULT\_PRESET removed; default values now come from DEFAULT\_HISTORY\_FILTERS via spread in resetFilters to avoid duplication. Keeps localStorage plumbing minimal and consistent with validation schema.

<!-- lore:019fcc75-1faf-7727-a7d0-4ab7e8fa9190 -->
* **Elapsed-time readout gating: recording||initializing plus zero startTimeRef trap**: Voice pill elapsed-time readout (apps/electron/src/renderer/src/pages/app.tsx) was gated on \`recording || initializing\`, but \`startTimeRef\` is only set once the mic actually opens. On first press of a fresh process, startTimeRef was still 0, so \`Date.now() - 0\` instantly exceeded the 60s threshold, showing a stray huge timestamp (e.g. "29763131:43") at the pill's right edge on every first startup. Looked like a display bug but was a state-gating bug: including \`initializing\` in the gate let the clock read before the ref was populated. Fix: gate strictly on \`recording\` alone AND require a non-zero start time, and reset the ref between sessions to avoid staleness.

<!-- lore:019f3176-10b7-741a-87a2-fd4fd33acac7 -->
* **electron typecheck: use package name @freestyle-voice/electron, not 'electron'**: Trap: running \`turbo typecheck --filter=electron\` fails with 'No package found with name electron' — looks like a filter syntax issue. Fix: the electron app's package name is \`@freestyle-voice/electron\`, so use \`--filter=@freestyle-voice/electron\`. Alternatively, run \`pnpm --filter @freestyle-voice/electron run typecheck\` directly. The typecheck script itself runs two sub-scripts: \`typecheck:node\` (tsconfig.node.json) and \`typecheck:web\` (tsconfig.web.json) — both must pass.

<!-- lore:019fceba-d4ee-7034-bc69-8bb16302e8af -->
* **endRemix hide:false leaks focusable pill, dictation pastes into itself**: endRemix({hide:false}) — used by dictation's onHotkeyDown self-heal (app.tsx), deliverRemixResult, and preset auth/usage errors — deliberately skips hidePill() to avoid hiding the pill mid-flow. But hidePill() was the only place that reset mainWindow.setFocusable(false), remixPressed, and route-key registration after a remix chat card called setRemixChatFocus(true)→win.setFocusable(true). Looked safe because deliverRemixResult's paste path (remix:paste IPC) calls hidePill() itself — only the dictation self-heal path was unsafe: dictation would record fine but paste into the still-focused pill window instead of the user's document, appearing as 'everything goes to remix'. Fix: added dedicated \`remix:release\`/\`releaseRemixHold()\` IPC that resets focusable+remixPressed+route-keys without hiding the pill, called from endRemix's hide:false branch; also made \`showPill()\` defensively reset \`setFocusable(false)\` on every show as a backstop.

<!-- lore:019fcea3-1004-74b3-a71f-53e15855de3d -->
* **endRemix({hide:false}) leaves pill window focusable — breaks dictation after remix chat**: Bug: after using the remix hotkey chat and returning to dictation, all subsequent hotkey presses seem to go to remix / paste into the pill instead of the frontmost app. Root cause: dictation's onHotkeyDown self-heal (apps/electron/src/renderer/src/pages/app.tsx:2399) calls \`endRemix({hide:false})\` when tearing down an active remix session, which deliberately skips \`hidePill()\`. But \`hidePill()\` (main/index.ts:1420-1451) is the ONLY place that resets \`mainWindow.setFocusable(false)\` and \`remixPressed\`. If the remix chat card previously ran \`setRemixChatFocus(true)\`→\`win.setFocusable(true)\` (remix-chat.tsx:104), the window stays focusable/focused after remix teardown, so dictation still records but the transcript pastes into the pill window itself rather than the OS-focused app. \`deliverRemixResult\`'s paste path is safe because main's \`remix:paste\` handler (index.ts:2918) calls \`hidePill()\` itself — only the dictation self-heal path is unsafe. Also \`showPill()\` (index.ts:996) never defensively resets focusability on show. Fix proposed but not yet applied: reset focusability inside \`endRemix({hide:false})\`, or make \`showPill()\` call \`setFocusable(false)\` defensively.

<!-- lore:019fcea0-efdf-7e7e-9263-d8e04628ac9d -->
* **failRemix() leaves remixRef set — only endRemix() clears remix mode**: In apps/electron/src/renderer/src/pages/app.tsx, remixRef.current (the sole discriminator for 'remix mode active', showRemixCard = remix !== null) is reset to null in exactly one place: endRemix() (:1920 → setRemix(null) at :1933). failRemix() (:1945) deliberately does NOT clear remixRef — it sets phase:'error' and leaves the session object in place (:1953) so the error card can render. This looks like a bug (remix 'stuck' after failure) but is intentional; the error phase auto-dismisses via a separate timeout (REMIX\_WARNING\_MS=4500ms) that itself calls endRemix(). If a future refactor changes failRemix to also null remixRef, the error-card render path breaks since it depends on remix !== null.

<!-- lore:019fb22f-6386-7960-bcad-b57dde979b2b -->
* **flex-1 on Tooltip-wrapped label expands hover hit-area beyond visible text**: Trap: \`flex-1\` on a truncated label span (e.g. model-used label in history.tsx FeedItem) looks like normal flex layout, but when wrapped in \`Tooltip\`/\`TooltipTrigger\`, flex-1 stretches the trigger element to fill the row — the tooltip then fires just from moving the cursor over empty space far past the visible text. Fix: use \`max-w-fit\` (with \`min-w-0 truncate\`) so the trigger sizes to its content instead of stretching. To still push trailing elements (action buttons, duration, cost) to the right edge of the row, add a separate \`\<div className="flex-1" />\` spacer sibling right after the tooltip, rather than putting flex-1 on the tooltip trigger itself.

<!-- lore:019fc2d6-cd25-777e-b04c-598eab886780 -->
* **Freestyle Cloud v2 payload asymmetry: appAssignments string vs array, customPrompt null vs undefined**: In apps/server/src/lib/freestyle-cloud.ts, /v2/transcribe (multipart form) receives \`appAssignments\` as a JSON-encoded STRING (parsed cloud-side via parseCleanupAppAssignments), while /v2/post-process (JSON body) receives \`appAssignments\` as a real ARRAY (validated by cleanupAppAssignmentsSchema) — same field, different wire encoding depending on endpoint transport. Also, \`customPrompt\` in the post-process payload must be built with \`|| undefined\`, not \`?? null\`, because postProcessV2BodySchema uses \`.optional()\` (rejects null) — whereas \`appContext\` uses \`.nullish()\` and does accept null. Looks like these fields should behave uniformly since they're 'the same' conceptual value, but each endpoint's schema differs. This is a recurring source of payload bugs; check the exact schema (multipart-string vs JSON-array, optional vs nullish) per field before adding new cloud request params.

<!-- lore:019f5b6f-8331-792c-b1bf-14b4430fd5dd -->
* **FREESTYLE\_CLOUD\_URL=localhost:8787 causes fetch failed 500 when cloud Worker isn't running**: Trap: \`apps/electron/.env.local\` sets \`FREESTYLE\_CLOUD\_URL=http://localhost:8787\` (local \`wrangler dev\` URL). When the cloud Worker isn't running, every transcription POST to that URL is refused — undici surfaces this as \`TypeError: fetch failed\` with no visible cause in the log, and the route returns 500. It looks like a cloud service outage or auth failure, but it's just a missing local process. Fix: either start \`wrangler dev\` in the \`cloud\` repo, comment out \`FREESTYLE\_CLOUD\_URL\` to fall back to production (\`https://service.freestylevoice.com\`), or switch to a local voice model. The real cause (ECONNREFUSED) is on \`err.cause\` — the \`formatError\` utility now walks the cause chain to surface it.

<!-- lore:019fcbf3-30e6-7f3c-a3db-1dce5be72193 -->
* **git diff main..HEAD inflated/inaccurate when local main branch is stale**: Trap: when reviewing a fork PR locally, \`git diff main..HEAD\` can show a wildly inflated diff (e.g. 40 files/+888/-2104) that looks like the true PR scope but is actually noise from a stale local \`main\` that hasn't fetched recent upstream commits. Fix: always cross-check the diff size against the PR's actual metadata from the GitHub API/UI (file count, +/- lines) before trusting a local diff — trust the platform-reported scope, not local git state, when main may be behind origin.

<!-- lore:019f1697-9d46-7703-82a6-67f628a78aaa -->
* **git rebase --continue hangs when git launches interactive editor — use GIT\_EDITOR=true**: Trap: \`git rebase --continue\` after resolving conflicts launches \`$EDITOR\` to confirm the commit message, blocking indefinitely in a non-interactive agent context — looks like a hang with no output. Fix: prefix with \`GIT\_EDITOR=true\` (\`GIT\_EDITOR=true git rebase --continue\`) to accept the default commit message without opening an editor. Also: \`.lore.md\` may be re-modified by a background process during the timeout, requiring re-staging (\`git add .lore.md\`) before rebase can continue.

<!-- lore:019f40a9-8d17-752c-ba21-10d4a88534f3 -->
* **history.tsx Reset button: was setting 'all-time' instead of restoring initial defaults**: Trap: Reset button called \`setActivePreset('all-time')\` which looks like 'clear all filters.' But the initial default is \`'weekly'\` (Last 7 days), not all-time. Fix: \`resetFilters\` restores actual initial defaults — \`preset='weekly'\`, cleared custom dates, \`page=0\`, \`diffMode=false\`, \`showAiEdits=true\`. Reset button uses \`Eraser\` icon (not \`RotateCcw\`) with \`ghost\` variant and destructive/danger theme (red text, subtle red hover).

<!-- lore:019f5fb8-4ec8-7e10-94b9-60512e0293d5 -->
* **i18n key removal: check all locale files including template.json**: Trap: removing an unused i18n key from \`en.json\` looks complete, but \`apps/electron/src/renderer/src/locales/template.json\` is a separate file that also contains the key and must be updated. Fix: always search for the key across all locale files (\`en.json\`, \`template.json\`, and language files \`de\`, \`es\`, \`fr\`, \`it\`, \`ja\`, \`pt\`) before committing. Missing keys in non-English locales are non-blocking (i18next falls back to \`en\`), but \`template.json\` must stay in sync with \`en.json\`.

<!-- lore:019fcbdf-3799-7839-bf9a-7785fe1e846b -->
* **Inline borderRadius style silently overrides animated .pill-card CSS rule**: In the voice pill (apps/electron/src/renderer/src/pages/app.tsx), inline style borderRadius: 20 on the card outranked the CSS rule .pill-card\[data-show="false"] { border-radius: 60px } — inline styles always beat stylesheet rules regardless of selector specificity. Looked fine on screen because the concurrent scale animation masked the frozen corner. Fixed in PR #541. Rule: any property the stylesheet animates on this component must never also be set inline, anywhere in the tree.

<!-- lore:019fcbf3-30d5-7e24-a746-b87aa95d6eef -->
* **Inline style attrs silently override animated CSS rules (pill border-radius)**: Trap: an inline style like \`borderRadius: 20\` on a React element looks harmless alongside a CSS class that also animates border-radius (\`.pill-card\[data-show="false"] { border-radius: 60px }\`) — it looks like the class rule will just apply on top. Fix: inline styles always win over stylesheet rules regardless of animation/transition, so the corner froze at 20px through the whole pill morph while a concurrent scale animation visually masked the bug, making it look correct on screen. Rule: never set inline style for a CSS property that a stylesheet also animates — pick one source of truth.

<!-- lore:019fcc75-1f91-7600-8ebe-3b26d269d5d5 -->
* **Inline style overrides CSS transition on .pill-card border-radius**: In apps/electron voice-pill code (pages/app.tsx), an inline style \`borderRadius: 20\` on the pill card silently outranked the animated CSS rule \`.pill-card\[data-show="false"] { border-radius: 60px }\`, so the corner stayed pinned at 20px through the whole morph. It looked correct on screen because the accompanying scale animation masked the frozen corner radius. Fix: never set inline styles for a CSS property that a stylesheet rule also animates — let the CSS own it entirely. Rule applies broadly to any animated property (transform, opacity, border-radius, etc.) in this codebase's pill/overlay animation code.

<!-- lore:019fcc74-3e5c-717c-ac23-537a898bf955 -->
* **Inline style overrides CSS-animated property in voice pill (app.tsx)**: In apps/electron/src/renderer/src/pages/app.tsx pill card, an inline \`style={{ borderRadius: 20 }}\` silently outranked the animated CSS rule \`.pill-card\[data-show="false"] { border-radius: 60px }\`, so the corner stayed pinned at 20px through the whole morph. Looked correct on screen because the scale animation masked the flat corner. Rule going forward: anything the stylesheet animates (border-radius, opacity, transform, etc.) must NOT also be set inline on the same element — inline styles always win CSS specificity battles. Fixed in PR #541 (design/voice-pill-motion, author Isaac Zhang).

<!-- lore:019f5fb8-4ecb-7535-9e81-b486cc791fa7 -->
* **Interactive rebase on shared branch: .lore.md merge conflicts — always take current version**: Trap: when rebasing \`feat/\*\` branches to drop commits, \`.lore.md\` frequently causes merge conflicts because it is updated by the memory system across sessions. Fix: always resolve \`.lore.md\` conflicts by taking the current branch version (\`git checkout --theirs .lore.md\`) and continuing the rebase. The \`.lore.md\` file is managed externally and should never be manually merged — the current branch's version is always authoritative.

<!-- lore:019f7b20-dc28-792c-a773-1606d91d4b9d -->
* **iOS 18+ keyboard extension: openURL selector hack fails — use SwiftUI Link**: On iOS 18+, \`UIApplication.shared.open(\_:options:completionHandler:)\` and \`perform(sel\_registerName("openURL:"))\` both fail silently when called from a keyboard extension. The only Apple-sanctioned mechanism is a SwiftUI \`Link(destination: url)\` hosted via \`UIHostingController\`. This also applies to \`openURL:\` triggered from the \`MicLink\` SwiftUI view used to launch dictation; use \`Link\` instead of gesture-based \`openURL\` calls.

<!-- lore:019f7aff-e631-7a11-92aa-ef2d4fb0e3d1 -->
* **iOS keyboard extension AVAudioSession: .record category fails — must use .playAndRecord**: Trap: \`AVAudioSession.setCategory(.record, mode: .measurement, options: \[.duckOthers])\` looks correct for mic-only capture and works in the main app. In a keyboard extension process, this combination causes \`setActive(true)\` to throw OSStatus \`561015905\` (\`cannotStartPlaying\`). Fix: use \`.playAndRecord\` + \`.default\` mode + \`\[.defaultToSpeaker, .allowBluetooth]\` options — this is the only category that reliably activates in the extension sandbox. Also guard \`inputNode\` format after activation: if \`sampleRate == 0 || channelCount == 0\`, throw before starting \`AVAudioEngine\`. Use a typed \`CaptureError\` enum (cases: \`session category\`, \`session activate\`, \`input format 0Hz/0ch\`, \`engine start\`) with \`NSLog\` to surface the exact OSStatus — a generic \`catch\` hides the real failure step.

<!-- lore:019f7e80-c7b6-74f9-ba3b-b086b57943e0 -->
* **iOS keyboard extension: .touchUpInside causes dropped keystrokes — use .touchDown for char keys**: On iOS, character keys in the keyboard must use \`.touchDown\` (not \`.touchUpInside\`) to avoid dropped keystrokes during fast typing. Delete uses \`.touchDown\` with 0.4s delay → 0.1s repeat → 0.5s word repeat. Also replace \`UIButton(type: .system)\` with a custom \`KeyButton\` subclass to eliminate system fade/tint animations that add lag.

<!-- lore:019f7bf9-db28-715e-81f1-146c3d40c026 -->
* **iOS keyboard extension: hasDictationKey=true suppresses system dictation button — must be set in init AND viewWillAppear**: Trap: setting \`hasDictationKey = true\` only in \`viewDidLoad\` looks sufficient but does NOT work — the system dictation button reappears. Fix: set it in \`init(nibName:bundle:)\`, \`required init?(coder:)\`, \`viewDidLoad\`, AND \`viewWillAppear(\_:)\`. Confirmed by StackOverflow accepted answer (score +8) and OP aditional note. Counter-intuitively, the property name is \`hasDictationKey = true\` (not false) — it tells iOS the extension provides its own dictation, suppressing the system button. Apps Wispr Flow and Typeless both use this approach. No Info.plist change needed — pure Swift on \`UIInputViewController\`.

<!-- lore:019f7c04-6286-72b6-89bd-cf5ffafea2d0 -->
* **iOS keyboard extension: textDidChange must NOT call applyColors() — causes per-keystroke lag**: Trap: \`textDidChange\` calling \`applyColors()\` looks correct for keeping key colors in sync with text state. But \`applyColors()\` rebuilds the SwiftUI mic host (\`micHost.rootView = MicLink(...)\`) AND recursively re-colors the entire key tree on every keystroke — causing severe input lag. Fix: \`textDidChange\` should call only \`applyAutoCap()\` (lightweight autocap state update). Full recoloring belongs in \`traitCollectionDidChange\` and \`viewDidLoad\` only — one-time events, not per-keystroke. Secondary lag factor (deferred): per-key drop shadow without \`shadowPath\` causes an offscreen render pass per button; fix requires UIButton subclass \`layoutSubviews\` override.

<!-- lore:019fc26e-0631-7860-92cf-121750580e39 -->
* **iOS KeyboardViewController.swift statusLabel truncates streamed text — wrap in fixed-height UIScrollView**: In apps/mobile/ios-keyboard/KeyboardViewController.swift, the streaming transcript renders into a UILabel \`statusLabel\` (fed by App Group state \`DictationBridge.partialTranscript\` via 0.3s polling). Setting \`numberOfLines = 2\` looks like a safe cap but the default \`.byTruncatingTail\` lineBreakMode silently ellipsizes long streamed text instead of scrolling. Fix: set \`numberOfLines = 0\` and wrap the label in a \`UIScrollView\` (\`statusScroll\`), with a \`scrollStatusToBottom()\` call after each update to keep the newest text visible. Gotcha within the fix: pinning the label to \`statusScroll.contentLayoutGuide\` does NOT give the scroll view a frame height — \`contentLayoutGuide\` only defines \`contentSize\`. Using \`heightAnchor.constraint(lessThanOrEqualToConstant: 30)\` alone leaves the scroll view's frame height ambiguous/underconstrained. Must use \`equalToConstant: 30\` (fixed height) instead; short text stays top-aligned via the \`overflow > 0\` guard resetting \`contentOffset\` to \`.zero\`. Note: transcript UI is pure UIKit (UILabel/UIScrollView); only the mic button uses SwiftUI via UIHostingController.

<!-- lore:019fc273-3a65-7fb3-8c54-fa3d4f0b3128 -->
* **KeyboardViewController statusScroll height ambiguity (contentLayoutGuide)**: iOS keyboard extension's streaming transcript label (\`statusLabel\`, apps/mobile/ios-keyboard/KeyboardViewController.swift) truncated to 2 lines with '…' because \`numberOfLines = 2\` used default \`.byTruncatingTail\`. Fixed by wrapping in \`statusScroll\` (UIScrollView): \`numberOfLines = 0\`, label pinned to \`statusScroll.contentLayoutGuide\` with width locked to \`frameLayoutGuide.widthAnchor\`, auto-scroll via \`scrollStatusToBottom()\`. Gotcha: pinning \`statusScroll.heightAnchor\` with only \`lessThanOrEqualToConstant\` looks correct (content should self-size) but a UIScrollView's frame height is NOT derived from its \`contentLayoutGuide\` — that guide only defines \`contentSize\`, not frame height. With just an upper bound and no other height constraint, the scroll view's height is ambiguous/underconstrained. Fix: use \`equalToConstant: 30\` (fixed ~2-line height) instead.

<!-- lore:019fd03f-b23f-705e-8579-caa336ec7ef5 -->
* **LazyMotion strict: m.\* components need to render inside a LazyMotion provider tree**: In apps/electron's vendored beUI components, preview-rail.tsx uses \`m.\*\` from motion/react but doesn't wrap itself in its own \`\<LazyMotion strict>\` provider — it only works because its sole consumer chain (message-scroller.tsx → remix-chat.tsx) happens to render inside remix-chat.tsx's \`\<LazyMotion features={domMax} strict>\`. It looks safe because it currently renders fine, but if any future consumer renders PreviewRail (or another m.\*-using vendored component) outside that provider tree, \`strict\` mode will throw at runtime. When reusing these vendored components elsewhere, verify/document the LazyMotion provider dependency first.

<!-- lore:019f0348-3334-7e1e-9748-87f2d7bbec74 -->
* **loadPlugins() JSDoc omitted built-ins — builtins are always prepended first**: Trap: \`loadPlugins()\` JSDoc in \`packages/sdk/src/loader.ts\` (lines 121-123) described load order as 'entries → local files → flatten presets → setup → sort by enforce', which looks complete. Fix: built-in plugins (passed via \`LoadPluginsOptions.builtin\`) are prepended BEFORE all other plugins, then the enforce-sort runs. The JSDoc was updated to include this. Built-ins bypass npm import/factory flow but \`setup()\` is still called. Built-ins are never filtered by disabled lists.

<!-- lore:019fcc78-8112-7ce2-b4b5-a8a77a38e104 -->
* **Local main stale vs contributor fork remote inflates git diff**: Trap: when reviewing a PR whose remote origin is actually a contributor's fork (e.g. tigerisaac/freestyle for freestyle-voice/freestyle), \`git diff main..HEAD\` after \`gh pr checkout \<PR>\` can show wildly inflated results (40 files/+888/-2104) because local \`main\` is stale relative to upstream — looks like the PR is huge but isn't. Fix: trust the PR's own file list/diff from the GitHub API (\`gh pr diff\` or the PR page) over a local \`git diff main..HEAD\`, or fetch/rebase local main from upstream before diffing. Confirmed actual scope for PR #541 was 5 files (+715/-122), matching GitHub's reported diff.

<!-- lore:019fb9ab-5db7-7f4e-bc1c-4caefeb7f127 -->
* **macOS CGEvent tap suppresses compound hotkeys — key swallowing and dictation activation**: On macOS, Freestyle registers a CGEvent tap (native key listener) that suppresses the configured compound hotkey so it doesn't leak into foreground apps. KeyCastr will show only the modifier (e.g., \`⌥\`) when the hotkey is pressed — this is expected suppression, not a bug. Dictation may fail to activate if: (1) Accessibility permission is missing or was revoked (check System Settings > Privacy & Security > Accessibility), (2) the app fell back to \`globalShortcut\` toggle mode (no key-up events), or (3) the native listener registered but the NSEvent monitor didn't (Sequoia changes). Troubleshooting: check Logs → Reveal Logs for \`macos-key-listener\` errors; verify Accessibility is enabled; try the default Globe/Fn hotkey; ensure full quit (Cmd+Q) and re-add Freestyle to Accessibility if permissions were reset.

<!-- lore:019f2449-2f8d-7095-ae2f-f75b1151f5cb -->
* **macOS glass/vibrancy removal: transparent:true at line 473 is for voice pill — do NOT remove it**: Trap: commit \`9191962\` added \`transparent: true\` to \`createSettingsWindow()\` as part of the glass effect. When reverting glass, it's tempting to remove ALL \`transparent: true\` occurrences. Fix: \`transparent: true\` at ~line 473 in \`createAppWindow()\` (voice pill window) is pre-existing and intentional — the pill window requires transparency. Only remove the darwin-gated block (\`backgroundColor: '#00000000'\`, \`transparent: true\`, \`vibrancy: 'under-window'\`, \`visualEffectState: 'active'\`) from \`createSettingsWindow()\` (~line 577). \`body { background: transparent }\` in \`globals.css\` base layer is also pre-existing — leave it.

<!-- lore:019f5b5e-925f-7ba2-b475-045bf614400a -->
* **max models.dev cache TTL from 10 min to 6 hours**: Increased \`models.dev\` API response cache TTL from 10 minutes to 6 hours in \`apps/server/src/routes/models.ts:170\`. Reduces external API calls while keeping model lists reasonably fresh. Cache key is the full response; STT models from models.dev remain limited to Groq whisper models (others are excluded because we have no \`TranscriptionProvider\` implementation for those platforms). The hardcoded \`BUILTIN\_VOICE\_MODELS\` list remains the source of truth for STT providers not tracked by models.dev (Deepgram, ElevenLabs, OpenAI transcription).

<!-- lore:019f432c-9ca9-7349-ad8d-70e29be4f2c5 -->
* **Memory notes about code removals may be stale — always verify with git log before trusting**: Trap: A long-term memory entry recording a prior removal (e.g. \`showLocal\`, \`hasLocalVoice\`, On-device row) looks authoritative and suggests the change was already merged. Fix: always verify with \`git log --all --grep\` or \`gh pr list\` before trusting a memory note about a removal. In this case, memory note \`\[019f40e3]\` recorded On-device row removal but \`git log\` confirmed it was never committed or merged on any branch — the row remained live on \`main\`. Memory notes can reflect planned or in-progress work that was abandoned.

<!-- lore:019fbc59-606f-746d-b545-e3dbcccb4d97 -->
* **mergeCloudVocabularyTerms is one-way additive — cloud deletes never propagate down**: \`mergeCloudVocabularyTerms()\` (apps/server/src/lib/vocabulary.ts) inserts cloud vocabulary terms not already present locally (case-insensitive dedup) and NEVER deletes local terms — it looks like a full sync because it's wired into \`pullCloudPreferences()\` on every launch/sign-in/industry-change, but it's intentionally one-directional (cloud→local, additive only). Known limitation, explicitly out of scope for PR #520: cloud-side deletes don't propagate down, and local vocabulary edits don't push up. Full two-way sync is a deliberate follow-up, not an oversight. Batch writes use explicit \`BEGIN\`/\`COMMIT\` because \`node:sqlite\` has no \`.transaction()\` helper.

<!-- lore:019fb9f9-a33f-78b2-87ae-f77282c323c9 -->
* **Mobile /v1/usage vs desktop /usage endpoint mismatch — unverified**: Mobile's \`fetchCloudUsage()\` (apps/mobile/src/lib/cloud/usage.ts:23) calls \`${cloudUrl()}/v1/usage\`, while desktop's cloud usage calls call \`/usage\` (apps/server/src/lib/freestyle-cloud.ts:518, 923). This looks like it could just be a documented mobile-only endpoint, but it may actually be a bug (wrong path) or the cloud service may alias both paths — unverified as of Aug 2026. Before building a shared cloud-fetch helper or unifying usage endpoints, confirm with the Freestyle Cloud service which path is canonical; do not assume either app's path is correct without checking the cloud service's route table.

<!-- lore:019fb9f9-a35c-7801-9c15-d01157126988 -->
* **Mobile app AGENTS.md: Expo v57 API surface changed — must consult versioned docs before coding**: apps/mobile/AGENTS.md states "Expo HAS CHANGED" and requires reading versioned docs at https://docs.expo.dev/versions/v57.0.0/ before writing any Expo-related code. This exists because Expo 57 (used here, alongside RN 0.86.0, React 19.2.3) has API differences from older Expo versions that general training knowledge may not reflect — code that looks correct based on older Expo patterns can be wrong or deprecated. Always check the pinned v57 docs before adding/modifying Expo APIs in apps/mobile.

<!-- lore:019fc2ee-7537-7d93-a111-027eac5d1675 -->
* **Mobile fetchCloudVocabularyTerms: undefined ≠ empty array**: In apps/mobile/src/lib/cloud/preferences.ts, \`fetchCloudVocabularyTerms()\` returning \`undefined\` means 'nothing synced yet' and callers MUST leave the local vocabulary list untouched — first launch or signed-out state must never wipe local terms. It's tempting to treat undefined the same as an empty result, but an explicit empty array \`\[]\` is a distinct signal meaning the user deleted all terms on another device, and that deletion must mirror down locally. Conflating the two (e.g. treating undefined as '\[], so clear local list') would wipe a fresh install's local vocabulary on first sync attempt.

<!-- lore:019f2988-db44-7956-a26e-847c2cca07c5 -->
* **Nested circle-in-circle icon artifact: CheckCircle2 inside a rounded-full span**: Trap: placing \`CheckCircle2\` (a circle-with-check glyph) inside a \`rounded-full\` span with a filled background creates a circle-inside-a-circle that looks muddy — it seems like a natural way to make a check indicator. Fix: use a bare \`Check\` icon (\`strokeWidth={3}\`) inside the filled \`rounded-full\` span instead. \`CheckCircle2\` is only appropriate as a standalone icon, not nested inside another circular container. Observed and fixed in \`tone.tsx\` cleanup and subset selector cards. \`CheckCircle2\` was fully removed from the import when the Cleanup tab was redesigned (Fix 6).

<!-- lore:019fb9b6-aadd-7c76-a8b3-0f245812043d -->
* **Network proxy/CA settings duplicated in Network tab**: The Network tab in Settings shows duplicate rows for \`network\_proxy\_url\` and \`network\_ca\_cert\_path\` because both the proxy/CA section and the legacy endpoint list each render their own GETs. The legacy endpoints (\`/api/settings/network\_proxy\_url\`, \`/api/settings/network\_ca\_cert\_path\`) were kept for backward compatibility and also queried by \`config.freestyle.json\` polling, causing 4–6 requests on page open.
