Consolidate by removing the legacy GET routes or deduplicating via a shared cache key; prefer the explicit proxy/CA section as the single source of truth.

<!-- lore:019faf89-ec1d-7c1f-93dc-504d3a933b0a -->
* **New /api/\* route prefixes must be added to TIMEOUT\_PREFIXES in apps/server index.ts**: Trap: adding a new top-level route group (e.g. \`/api/org\`) to the router looks complete once it's mounted and typechecks — but the 30s request-timeout middleware only applies to prefixes explicitly listed in \`TIMEOUT\_PREFIXES\` in \`apps/server/src/index.ts\`. Sibling groups like \`/api/usage\` are in the list; a newly added group can be silently missing, leaving those routes with no timeout guard. Fix: always add the new prefix to \`TIMEOUT\_PREFIXES\` alongside adding the route mount. Caught during PR review for the org endpoints (\`/api/org\` was missing).

<!-- lore:019f64f6-14dd-7c13-b11d-e9034080f164 -->
* **Onboarding model setup: Open model source button + i18n**: Onboarding uses the same \`ModelSetupPanel\` (updated in PR #388) that renders an "Open model source" button when \`model.state?.errorSourceUrl\` is present. The button opens the HF URL via \`window.api.openExternal()\` and is wired into the onboarding flow. i18n key \`onboarding.modelSetup.openModelSource\` added and translated into de/es/fr/it/ja/pt locales. The same error-source URL is propagated from \`download-guard.ts\` → \`whisper/models.ts\`/\`mlx-asr/models.ts\` → renderer state → \`ModelSetupPanel\`.

<!-- lore:019fafcb-7605-71bb-bce5-b42352cf647a -->
* **Org switcher menu: use optimistic mutation, not preventDefault, to keep dropdown responsive**: Trap: when a DropdownMenuItem onSelect triggers an async mutation (e.g. switching active org), Radix closes the menu before the mutation resolves — looks like the click did nothing. The fix that seems obvious is calling e.preventDefault() in onSelect to block the close until the mutation fires, but this leaves the menu open awkwardly, and disabling the active item to block re-clicks looks broken. Fix: let the menu close immediately (no preventDefault); use React Query's onMutate to optimistically write the new active org into the cache (from the already-fetched org list) for instant UI feedback; onError rolls back to the onMutate snapshot; onSettled always invalidates the query to sync with server truth. Keep the active item clickable but no-op instead of disabled. Pattern used in useSetActiveOrganization (apps/electron/src/renderer/src/lib/use-profile.ts).

<!-- lore:019fcbf0-d16c-740c-8d4f-86493d5586ff -->
* **permission-flow.test.ts: closeApp renamed to closePermissionApp (SIGKILL, avoids CI wedge)**: apps/electron/tests/permission-flow.test.ts's teardown helper was renamed \`closeApp\`→\`closePermissionApp\` in main (PR #516): \`app.close()\`'s native cleanup intermittently wedges macOS CI, producing two 60s Playwright timeouts. The fixture now uses \`childProcess.kill("SIGKILL")\` with a 5s race timeout instead, since this fixture only validates startup permissions, not production shutdown. Branches predating this rename keep the old \`closeApp\` (uses \`app.close()\` + 10s SIGKILL fallback) — merging both survives as duplicate definitions. Always standardize on \`closePermissionApp\` and delete the old \`closeApp\`.

<!-- lore:019fcbf1-d942-7f28-b77c-221f82ffbf47 -->
* **permission-flow.test.ts: use closePermissionApp, never app.close(), for teardown**: apps/electron/tests/permission-flow.test.ts's teardown helper was renamed \`closeApp\` → \`closePermissionApp\` in main (PR #516), switching from \`app.close()\` (10s SIGKILL fallback) to direct \`childProcess.kill("SIGKILL")\` (5s timeout). Reason (source comment): 'This fixture validates startup permissions, not production shutdown. Avoid app.close(), which runs native cleanup that intermittently wedges macOS CI and leaves Playwright waiting through two 60-second timeouts.' When merging older branches that still reference \`closeApp\`/\`app.close()\`, standardize all call sites on \`closePermissionApp\` and delete the duplicate old definition — don't keep both. Electron Playwright e2e tests run with \`workers: 1\` (serial only) per playwright.config.ts.

<!-- lore:019fc267-36a1-794b-9c67-a1ef1f92b6f5 -->
* **permission-flow.test.ts:183 flaky launch hang on macOS CI**: Trap: Playwright test "startup does not warn when Accessibility permission is granted" (apps/electron/tests/permission-flow.test.ts:183) intermittently hits \`Test timeout of 60000ms exceeded\` during app launch on macOS CI, plus a \`Worker teardown timeout\`, looking like a real startup regression. It is NOT — the same commit's PR run passed this exact test, and 10/11 other tests in the file pass on the same main run, confirming a flaky Electron launch hang rather than a code issue. Fix: re-run the job (\`gh run rerun --failed\`); consider adding Playwright \`retries: process.env.CI ? 2 : 0\` to absorb launch-hang flakiness (proposed, not yet implemented as of Aug 2026).

<!-- lore:019fcc47-e965-7ab2-8967-b8430c42aa40 -->
* **Pill CSS animation gotchas: data-show mount timing + inline style override**: In apps/electron/src/renderer/src/pages/app.tsx pill-card/pill-surface animations: (a) CSS enter transitions don't fire if the subtree mounts with data-show="true" already set on first paint — hold the attribute at its initial (hidden) value for one committed frame, then flip it, so the browser has a starting frame to transition from. (b) Inline styles silently outrank animated CSS rules on the same property — inline \`borderRadius: 20\` pinned the corner at 20px through a \`.pill-card\[data-show="false"] { border-radius: 60px }\` scale animation; the bug was masked because the scale animation visually distracted from the frozen corner. Fix: never set inline styles for any CSS property a stylesheet rule also animates.

<!-- lore:019fcbdf-37a6-7955-9c45-639840927671 -->
* **Pill elapsed-time readout showed garbage timestamp on first recording**: In app.tsx's voice pill, the elapsed-time readout was gated on \`recording || initializing\`, but \`startTimeRef\` is only set once the mic actually opens — on the first press of a fresh process it was still 0, so \`Date.now() - 0\` produced a huge value and the pill showed a stray full-timestamp readout (e.g. "29763131:43") instead of "0:00". Looked correct because the gating condition seemed to cover startup. Fixed in PR #541: gate on \`recording\` alone plus a non-zero start time, and reset the ref between sessions so staleness doesn't carry over.

<!-- lore:019fcc74-3e72-7cb9-80ea-1d11376a551d -->
* **Pill elapsed-time readout: startTimeRef=0 defaults instantly clear 60s threshold**: In apps/electron/src/renderer/src/pages/app.tsx, the pill's elapsed-time readout was gated on \`recording || initializing\`, but \`startTimeRef\` is only set once the mic actually opens. On the very first press of a fresh process, \`startTimeRef\` was still 0, so \`Date.now() - 0\` instantly exceeded the 60s threshold, showing a stray huge readout (e.g. "29763131:43") at the pill's right edge on every first startup. Fix (PR #541): gate on \`recording\` alone AND a non-zero start time, and reset the ref between sessions to avoid staleness. Any new timer/elapsed-time UI in this file must guard against a zero/unset ref, not just the recording-state flag.

<!-- lore:019fcc6d-261f-75f0-877e-59e8223ec4ba -->
* **Pill entrance animation: CSS transition doesn't fire when data-show mounts already true**: When React mounts a subtree with \`data-show="true"\` already set (as in the voice pill's entrance), CSS transitions never fire — there's no prior painted state to transition from, so the enter animation silently no-ops even though the transition rule exists. Fix used in apps/electron renderer pill code: hold the show-state at false for one committed frame (e.g. via rAF/setTimeout(0)) so the browser paints the initial state first, then flip to true on the next frame so the transition actually runs. Applies to any enter/mount animation gated by a boolean prop/attribute rather than a class toggle after mount.

<!-- lore:019fcbdf-37bc-7558-8ed4-5e7ff1dad3ba -->
* **Pill preview harness: ws@8 required, explicit Chromium executablePath needed**: The voice-pill preview/test harness (companion to specs/voice-pill-motion.md) needs: (1) \`ws\` resolved from \`.pnpm\` pinned to \`ws@8\` — \`ws@7\` lacks the \`WebSocketServer\` export the harness uses; (2) an explicit \`executablePath\` for Chromium, since only \`chromium\_headless\_shell-\*\` exists in this environment, not the default Chromium binary. For genuine silence testing (the fake-audio-device emits a tone, not silence, so flatline detection never fires against it), generate real silence via \`ffmpeg -f lavfi -i anullsrc=r=48000:cl=mono -t 12 silence.wav\` and pass \`--use-file-for-fake-audio-capture=\<path>%noloop\` to Chromium.

<!-- lore:019f6ac9-caaa-7d98-af4d-4312b9431d96 -->
* **Plugin dev-link slug mismatch: hardcoded ROUTE\_BASE breaks settings page for dev packages**: Trap: plugin middleware and UI both hardcode the production slug (e.g. \`freestyle-voice-plugin-emoji\`) in \`ROUTE\_BASE\`/\`ROUTE\` constants. When the plugin is dev-linked as \`@freestyle-voice/plugin-emoji-dev\`, \`pluginSlug()\` derives \`freestyle-voice-plugin-emoji-dev\` — the hardcoded route never matches, causing 404 on the settings page. Looks correct because the production slug is right for published installs. Fix: derive \`routeBase\` dynamically in \`setup()\` from \`ctx.name\` (the installed package name at runtime). In the UI, extract the slug from the page's own URL (\`/api/plugins/\<slug>/ui/...\`) via a \`getRoute()\` helper instead of a hardcoded constant. This makes both middleware and UI work under any slug automatically.

<!-- lore:019f6a1a-df45-7ebc-850c-1e3947744834 -->
* **PluginPage blank screen: entry undefined renders empty div with no error — stale nav route cause**: Trap: navigating to \`/plugins/\<slug>/\<pageId>\` after a plugin is unlinked/uninstalled looks like it should show an error or loading state. \`PluginPage\` at \`plugin-page.tsx:62-68\` early-returns from \`useLayoutEffect\` if \`!slug || !pageId || !entry\`, rendering only \`\<div ref={placeholderRef} className="flex-1" />\` — blank screen with no error boundary, no loading indicator. Root causes: (1) stale nav item routes to a slug no longer in the plugin list; (2) \`PluginViewManager\` caches \`WebContentsView\` for unlinked dev plugin (\`view-manager.ts:97\`) and \`--unlink\` never calls \`invalidatePluginView()\`. Fix: add error/empty state to \`PluginPage\` when \`entry\` is undefined, and ensure unlink/uninstall always calls \`invalidatePluginView()\` and removes stale nav items.

<!-- lore:019efa02-1b7f-7631-a7a2-c2cbc71c686f -->
* **pluginSlug() test assertion must be updated manually after scope rename**: Error logging for server 500s: added local error logs so 500s are visible in console/log (previously only sent to telemetry). Added formatError() to index.ts to inline error+stack into Winston message (Winston printf only prints message arg). Added log.error() with full stack in transcribe.ts catch blocks (cloud combined mode and main transcribe path) and log.warn() in usage.ts for transient cloud 502s. Global onError in index.ts now logs \`METHOD /path -> status: \<error+stack>\` for all 500s before calling captureException. This uncovers the real cause of transcribe 500s (e.g., fetch failures) and usage 502s.

<!-- lore:019faf89-ec21-793e-b13a-d6a94ff42a61 -->
* **POST /api/client-error must never receive transcript or clipboard text**: The \`/client-error\` endpoint in apps/server (window.onerror, unhandled rejections, React error boundary reports) always persists reports to the local log file for diagnostics; PostHog reporting is gated by telemetry opt-out inside \`captureException\`. Only \`message\`, \`stack\`, \`source\`, and \`context\` fields are accepted by the schema — callers must never include transcript or clipboard text in any of these fields, since crash reports are more likely to be inspected/shared than regular logs. Same rationale as never logging transcript text elsewhere (\[\[019f5b5e-925f-7ba2-b475-045bf614400a]]).

<!-- lore:019f3cb4-43cc-7f1c-b96c-3a326a2b81ee -->
* **postProcessWithFreestyleCloud: customPrompt ?? null sends explicit null — cloud schema rejects with 400**: Trap: \`customPrompt: opts.customPrompt ?? null\` looks safe — null means 'no custom prompt.' But \`postProcessV2BodySchema\` uses \`cleanupCustomPromptSchema.optional()\` (\`z.string().optional()\`), which short-circuits only on \`undefined\`, not \`null\`. JSON.stringify preserves \`null\` as a key, so cloud receives \`customPrompt: null\` → Zod rejects → HTTP 400 → cleanup silently no-ops and every dictation logs a Sentry error. Fix: use \`customPrompt: opts.customPrompt || undefined\` — \`JSON.stringify\` drops \`undefined\` keys entirely, so cloud sees no field and \`.optional()\` accepts it. Contrast: \`appContext\` correctly uses \`.nullish()\` (accepts null); \`customPrompt\` does not. Mode 1 (streaming, raw) and Mode 3 (combined, multipart) are unaffected — only Mode 2 (local voice + cloud cleanup) is broken by this.

<!-- lore:019f3cab-90cb-7928-b0c6-d8c0b587cc45 -->
* **PR #411 restoration audit: knip.jsonc and dashboard.tsx had stale residuals**: Trap: After restoring all 17 files touched by PR #411, two files had stale residuals that looked intentional. (1) \`knip.jsonc\`: #411 added a block ignoring 5 tone files with comment 'The Tone feature is temporarily disabled' — this block must be removed once tone is restored, not kept. (2) \`dashboard.tsx\`: \`/settings/formats\` redirect was changed from \`\<Navigate to="/settings/tone" replace />\` to \`\<Navigate to="/settings" replace />\` — since \`TonePage\` is imported and the tone route exists at line 109, the redirect target should be restored to \`/settings/tone\`. Fix: commit \`b513d26\` cleaned both. Audit method: diff branch against \`0a99d92~1\` (exact pre-#411 commit) across all 17 touched files.

<!-- lore:019f3be9-8af6-7af8-8be9-5ebdbf1fc1d4 -->
* **PR #411 schema migration v14 comment is stale — restoration is safe but comment misleads**: Trap: schema migration v14 comment reads 'Freestyle Cloud cleanup is no longer a standalone LLM option — the v3 cloud path always cleans, keyed on the voice provider alone.' This looks authoritative and suggests restoring cloud cleanup as a separate LLM option would conflict with the migration. Fix: the migration only ran once (guard \`currentVersion < 14\`) and deleted existing \`freestyle-cloud\` LLM \`model\_configs\` rows; if a freestyle-cloud LLM was the default, also sets \`llm\_cleanup\` to \`'false'\` via upsert. Re-adding \`FREESTYLE\_CLOUD\_CLEANUP\_MODEL\_ID\` to \`BUILTIN\_LLM\_MODELS\` at runtime is safe — \`BUILTIN\_LLM\_MODELS\` is a static list, not a DB table. Migration and runtime restoration operate at different layers. \`cloud-cleanup-migration.test.ts\` (120 lines, 2 tests) confirms migration behavior and does NOT conflict with the restored runtime path.

<!-- lore:019fbc9a-9e60-7553-beed-937530dd1bcb -->
* **preferences-sync FIELD\_MAP can't serialize an array for a future \`languages\` field**: apps/server/src/lib/preferences-sync.ts FIELD\_MAP entry \`{settingKey: "language", cloudField: "language", kind: "string"}\` looks trivial to extend to a plural \`languages\` array, but \`FieldMap.kind\` only supports \`"string" | "json"\` and \`cloudField\` is typed against \`keyof MemberPreferencesInput\` (packages/validations/src/member-preferences.ts), which currently only declares a \`language: z.string().max(10).nullish()\` field. Adding a \`languages\` cloudField won't typecheck until member-preferences.ts schema is updated first, and \`kind: "json"\` would need to be used (JSON.stringify serialization) since there's no array-specific kind. Also: sync errors here are always swallowed by design ("a failed sync never disrupts the local write") — don't add throwing behavior when touching this file.

<!-- lore:019fbc97-18e5-7275-b8af-3f01f39a2b36 -->
* **preferences-sync.ts FIELD\_MAP has no array kind — blocks future multi-language sync**: \`apps/server/src/lib/preferences-sync.ts\` FIELD\_MAP entry for language is \`{settingKey: "language", cloudField: "language", kind: "string"}\`. The \`kind\` union only supports \`"string" | "json"\` — there is no array-serialization kind. Also \`cloudField\` is typed against \`keyof MemberPreferencesInput\`, which currently only exposes a singular \`language\` field in packages/validations/src/member-preferences.ts. A future multi-language refactor introducing a \`languages\` array setting would need: (1) a new \`kind: "array"\` (or reuse \`json\`) in FIELD\_MAP, (2) a \`languages\` field added to \`member-preferences.ts\` schema before \`cloudField\` can reference it, or pushSettingToCloud/pullCloudPreferences will not typecheck.

<!-- lore:019fcc0d-4576-7d4a-b314-e645ef03d11e -->
* **pull\_request\_target + checkout@v6 blocks fork PR checkout (Craft reusable workflows)**: actions/checkout@v6 refuses to check out fork PR code in \`pull\_request\_target\` workflows (pwn-request protection) unless \`allow-unsafe-pr-checkout: true\` is set on the checkout step. Freestyle's \`.github/workflows/changelog-preview.yml\` calls the reusable \`getsentry/craft/.github/workflows/changelog-preview.yml@v2\` with \`secrets: inherit\` — the checkout step lives inside Craft's workflow, so this repo cannot set that flag directly (would require an upstream Craft change). Fix: add \`if: github.event.pull\_request.head.repo.full\_name == github.repository\` to the job so it's skipped (not failed) for external-fork PRs — changelog preview is a maintainer convenience, not required for merge. Same root cause likely affects other \`pull\_request\_target\` + reusable-workflow jobs (e.g. any future Craft-based automation) — check for forks before assuming CI is broken.

<!-- lore:019fcc79-bbc6-7a17-a390-2ff89bf555ca -->
* **pull\_request\_target workflows fail checkout on fork PRs (actions/checkout@v6)**: Workflows triggered by \`pull\_request\_target\` (e.g. .github/workflows/changelog-preview.yml calling getsentry/craft's reusable changelog-preview.yml@v2) fail on fork PRs with 'Refusing to check out fork pull request code from a pull\_request\_target workflow' — actions/checkout@v6 added this pwn-request protection since pull\_request\_target runs with the base repo's GITHUB\_TOKEN/secrets against attacker-controlled fork code. Looks fixable via \`allow-unsafe-pr-checkout: true\`, but that flag lives inside Craft's reusable workflow, not settable from this repo — would need an upstream Craft change. Fix used: add \`if: github.event.pull\_request.head.repo.full\_name == github.repository\` to the job so it's skipped (not failed) for external forks; changelog preview is a maintainer convenience, not required for fork contributors to merge.

<!-- lore:019faf89-ec11-7e46-a0bb-0581be88b6ab -->
* **Radix DropdownMenu submenu item + async mutation: preventDefault breaks close, optimistic update is the real fix**: Trap: switching orgs via a Radix DropdownMenu submenu item failed because Radix closes the menu (and unmounts the item) before an onSelect-triggered async mutation fires — looks like it needs \`e.preventDefault()\` in onSelect to keep the menu open until the mutation completes. That workaround stops the menu from ever auto-dismissing, which feels broken. Fix: remove \`preventDefault()\` (let the menu close immediately) and instead give the mutation hook (\`useSetActiveOrganization\`) an optimistic-update pattern: \`onMutate\` snapshots and swaps the cached active org immediately, \`onError\` rolls back to the snapshot, \`onSettled\` always invalidates the query to sync with server truth. The already-active item should be clickable-but-no-op (just closes the menu), not \`disabled\` (which looks broken/unclickable).

<!-- lore:019f5b23-acfc-7aa2-8f2a-f78f9b0455b3 -->
* **Raw fetch for /api/transcribe WAV POST is a legitimate exception — hc client cannot send binary Blob**: Trap: \`app.tsx\` has two raw \`fetch\` calls — both look like candidates to migrate to the typed \`hc\` client. Fix: \`/api/transcribe\` (WAV POST) MUST stay as raw \`fetch\` — it sends a binary \`Blob\` with \`Content-Type: audio/wav\`, and the \`hc\` client only accepts \`{ json }\`, \`{ form }\`, or \`{ query }\` bodies. \`/api/transcribe/pre-warm\` has no such constraint and SHOULD use the typed client: \`void getClient().api.transcribe\['pre-warm'].$post().catch(() => {})\`. Always check whether a raw fetch sends binary data before migrating it to \`hc\`.

<!-- lore:019f40ba-f284-79ae-8878-6d0386b4a5fd -->
* **react-day-picker today dot clipped when date is selected — hide with after:!hidden on selected**: Trap: \`react-day-picker\` renders a \`bg-primary\` pseudo-element dot (\`after:bottom-1\`) on the today cell to mark the current day. When today is also selected, the button fills with \`bg-primary\` and the dot sits on the digit's bottom, making the number look clipped. Fix: add \`after:!hidden\` to the \`selected\` className — hides the dot only when the day is selected, preserving it on unselected today cells. The \`!important\` flag is required to override the \`today\` modifier styles.

<!-- lore:019f40a9-8d15-7668-80c4-e0aab03175c7 -->
* **react-day-picker v10 Chevron renders black in dark mode — add fill-current to className**: Trap: \`react-day-picker@10.0.1\` \`Chevron.js\` renders an SVG \`\<polygon>\` with no explicit \`fill\` attribute, so it defaults to black regardless of theme. Looks correct in light mode. Fix: pass \`fill-current\` in the Chevron's \`className\` prop so the polygon inherits \`currentColor\` from the surrounding text color. Applied in \`FilterPanel\`'s \`\<DayPicker>\` component props.

<!-- lore:019f1672-dce1-747e-8b78-ee1da7f0cb31 -->
* **releaseStream() after every session forces getUserMedia round-trip — init delay for cloud providers**: Trap: \`releaseStream()\` is called after every recording session (in \`cancelRecording\`, \`app.tsx:891\`), stopping all mic tracks so the OS mic indicator turns off. This looks correct for privacy, but forces a fresh \`getUserMedia()\` round-trip on every hotkey press — the dominant init delay for cloud providers (no local Whisper). Fix: user confirmed preference to keep this behavior (release stream after each dictation). The delay is mitigated by running \`prepareSystemAudio\` concurrently with \`acquireStream()\` rather than sequentially. \`recorder.ts:acquireStream()\` reuses existing stream if tracks are live — but since \`releaseStream()\` nulls the stream, it always falls through to \`getUserMedia\`.

<!-- lore:019fcea0-efd9-76b6-b11b-fa25aee041b4 -->
* **Remix hotkey can supersede dictation but no inverse path exists — stuck-in-remix bug**: Trap: handleRemixHotkeyDown() (main/index.ts:3884) implements remix:supersede — if hotkeyPressed (dictation) is true when remix fires, it cancels dictation and sends 'remix:supersede' to hand the shared macOS home key to remix. This looks like a complete conflict-resolution mechanism, but there is no inverse: no 'dictation supersedes remix' path exists at the native-listener level. If a remix chat session is left open (e.g. ended via endRemix({hide:false}), :1935, which deliberately skips main's hidePill()), mainWindow.setFocusable(false) never runs (only guaranteed inside hidePill, :1444) and remixPressed/route-keys may linger — so shared key presses keep resolving through the remix listener/focus state, and dictation appears permanently broken. Fix direction: add a symmetric supersede path so a dictation key-down can force-close an active remix session and restore focusable(false)/remixPressed=false, not just the reverse.

<!-- lore:019fd059-3316-77da-9cd5-13db1f411ab2 -->
* **remix web\_search/image\_search fail on BYOK/local — unconditional prompt advertises cloud-only tools**: REMIX\_AGENT\_PROMPT (apps/server/src/lib/editor/remix-prompts.ts) unconditionally instructs the model to call web\_search/image\_search for facts, but these are server-side/cloud-only tools. runRemixAgentLocally() (remix-agent.ts) only registers remixClientTools(); the renderer dispatcher executeTool() (remix-chat.tsx) has no case for web\_search/image\_search, so BYOK/local runs get {ok:false, reason:"unknown tool: web\_search"} (confirmed in logs). Looks safe because the system prompt intentionally mirrors cloud byte-for-byte, so tool names seem fine to reference unconditionally — but tool \*availability\* isn't gated per path, only \*registration\* is. The model then burns an agent step (of REMIX\_MAX\_STEPS=16) on guaranteed failure and recovers from memory, ironically violating the prompt's own 'never edit from memory of a stale snapshot' rule. Proposed fix (not yet implemented): add \`hasWebSearch: boolean\` to buildRemixAgentSystem(), gate the two web-search prompt sections on it, pass false from runRemixAgentLocally, true from the cloud route.

<!-- lore:019fd060-98f6-779e-a1f6-6bcc906869f5 -->
* **REMIX\_MAX\_STEPS (16) vs specs/remix.md stepCountIs(8) — spec doc may be stale**: apps/server/src/lib/remix-agent.ts defines \`REMIX\_MAX\_STEPS = 16\` with rationale ("a canvas-app edit costs 6-8 calls before verification; a run that hasn't converged in 16 is lost"), but specs/remix.md's own description of lib/remix-agent.ts states \`stopWhen: stepCountIs(8)\`. This discrepancy was observed but not reconciled — do not trust specs/remix.md's step-count claim over the actual \`REMIX\_MAX\_STEPS\` constant in code when reasoning about agent loop budget; verify against the live constant.

<!-- lore:019fcea2-0010-71a8-bcc8-97ce0b138470 -->
* **remix:supersede is one-directional — dictation can't force-close remix, suspected cause of stuck-remix bug**: main/index.ts's remix:supersede (~line 3901) lets a remix keypress cancel in-progress dictation, but there's no inverse: a dictation press while remix chat is active doesn't force-close remix at the main-process listener level. app.tsx's onHotkeyDown (line 2395) self-heals by calling endRemix({hide:false}) — but hide:false skips window.api.hidePill(), and main's hidePill() (main/index.ts:1426) is the ONLY place that resets remixPressed=false, clears remix route keys, and calls mainWindow.setFocusable(false). Since opening remix chat sets setFocusable(true) (main/index.ts:3277-3286; remix-chat.tsx:103-106), if remix chat exits via endRemix({hide:false}) instead of the full hidePill flow, the pill window can stay focusable/remixPressed stuck true, causing subsequent shared-key presses to keep resolving through the remix listener instead of dictation. Looks like a harmless UI-preserving optimization (keep pill visible during handoff) but silently skips critical state reset. Not yet fixed — flagged as most likely root cause.

<!-- lore:019fcea2-001e-74bd-878f-1ab95f4e8794 -->
* **remixPressed has no stuck-watchdog, unlike dictation's HOTKEY\_STUCK\_TIMEOUT\_MS**: main/index.ts has a 5-minute watchdog (HOTKEY\_STUCK\_TIMEOUT\_MS, armHotkeyStuckWatchdog/clearHotkeyStuckWatchdog, ~line 4059-4078) that force-resets hotkeyPressed=false and calls sendHotkeyUp() if the dictation key-up event is ever missed. No equivalent watchdog exists for remixPressed — if a key-up is dropped for the remix hotkey (e.g. OS swallows it, or app loses focus mid-press), remixPressed can stay true indefinitely with no self-healing mechanism, unlike dictation. Any fix for the stuck-remix bug should likely add a matching watchdog for symmetry, or explain why remix doesn't need one.

<!-- lore:019fb1fd-8e0b-7d94-a0f0-ad5522231f31 -->
* **Remote cloud cleanup path previously skipped beforeCleanup hook**: The remote Freestyle Cloud cleanup call (used in local-voice + cloud-cleanup mode) previously never ran the \`beforeCleanup\` plugin hook at all, unlike the local-model cleanup path which does. Fix direction: short-circuit/guard the remote cloud cleanup call the same way as the local-model path so plugins can still intercept via \`beforeCleanup\` before cloud cleanup mode processes the transcript. This mirrors the two-layer empty-transcript protection pattern \[\[019f6bda-dcea-7da5-8d68-7063d9a45d4a]] — cloud cleanup is a single remote call and must be explicitly gated client-side since it can't be interrupted mid-flight.

<!-- lore:019fb989-9c94-702d-917c-6e57c0e80785 -->
* **resolvePluginAsset guards against empty plugin.dir traversal**: In \`apps/electron/src/main/plugins/manifest.ts\`, \`resolvePluginAsset\` falls back to \`process.cwd()\` when \`plugin.dir\` is empty (e.g. for a "missing" plugin). This could allow a plugin page to request assets outside the plugin directory and potentially read files from the app root. Fix: reject the request when \`!plugin.dir\` (return 404). Commit \`2d6930f\`.

<!-- lore:019fb22f-6381-7269-b36d-eb5fc59b53e8 -->
* **Schema migrations must guard for tables not yet created in pre-version test fixtures**: Trap: migration v17 in schema.ts unconditionally copied rows via \`INSERT ... SELECT ... FROM sessions\` when rebuilding the table — looks safe since \`sessions\` was created in v9 and the app has been on v16+ for a while. But migration tests (tone-migration.test.ts, cloud-cleanup-migration.test.ts) simulate a pre-v9 database to test earlier migrations in isolation, where \`sessions\` never existed yet, causing \`Error: no such table: sessions\`. Fix: check \`sqlite\_master\` for table existence before referencing a table assumed to exist from an earlier migration version. Any migration that reads/copies from a table created in a prior \`if (currentVersion < N)\` block must be defensive against fixtures that stop before that version.

<!-- lore:019fb234-5091-71d1-8669-60ed642c2c1d -->
* **schema.ts migrations must guard for tables absent in earlier schema versions**: Trap: a migration that copies rows from an old table into a rebuilt one (e.g. v17 sessions rebuild: \`INSERT OR IGNORE INTO sessions\_new SELECT ... FROM sessions\`) looks safe since the table 'always exists' in current DBs. But migration tests simulate pre-vN schema state — e.g. cloud-cleanup-migration.test.ts and tone-migration.test.ts simulate a pre-v9 DB where \`sessions\` never existed, causing \`no such table: sessions\` inside applyMigrations. Fix: check \`sqlite\_master\` for the source table's existence before running any INSERT-FROM copy step in a new migration. Always run the full server test suite (\`pnpm --filter @freestyle-voice/server test\`) after any schema.ts migration — older-version test fixtures are what catch this, not manual testing against a live DB.

<!-- lore:019fb1fd-8e02-7f8f-b96e-965ff3014282 -->
* **schema.ts v17 sessions migration assumes legacy sessions table exists**: Trap: the v17 migration (schema.ts) that rebuilds \`sessions\` with a host-keyed primary key copies rows via \`INSERT OR IGNORE INTO sessions\_new SELECT ... FROM sessions\` unconditionally — looks safe since the \`sessions\` table has existed since schema v9. But migration test fixtures (tone-migration.test.ts, cloud-cleanup-migration.test.ts) simulate pre-v9 databases to test unrelated migrations, and applyMigrations() runs all versions sequentially from 0 — hitting v17 before the \`sessions\` table was ever created, throwing \`no such table: sessions\`. Fix: guard the copy step to check (e.g. via \`sqlite\_master\`) that the legacy \`sessions\` table exists before running the INSERT SELECT. Any future migration touching a table introduced in a later version must guard for DBs that predate that table's creation.

<!-- lore:019fb237-237e-70b3-828e-e5a43c6a79f3 -->
* **schema.ts v17 sessions migration fails on pre-v9 DBs lacking sessions table**: Trap: the v17 migration (schema.ts, rebuilding \`sessions\` keyed by host — see \[\[019fb22f-6368-791f-af20-2696050f94a6]]) runs \`INSERT OR IGNORE INTO sessions\_new SELECT ... FROM sessions\` unconditionally on every DB being migrated, which looks safe since \`sessions\` has existed since schema v9. But migration test fixtures simulating pre-v9 DBs (no \`sessions\` table yet) hit \`Error: no such table: sessions\` because all \`if (currentVersion < N)\` blocks run sequentially in one transaction regardless of the DB's true starting version. Fix: guard the copy step by checking \`sqlite\_master\` for the legacy \`sessions\` table's existence before running the \`INSERT ... FROM sessions\` copy. Confirmed via failures in cloud-cleanup-migration.test.ts and tone-migration.test.ts, fixed and re-verified (44/44 test files passing).

<!-- lore:019f6a56-d8d1-78e8-a3c1-bd42df82fd87 -->
* **SegmentedControl default size (h-8) is shorter than Input h-9 — use size="lg" to match**: Trap: placing a \`SegmentedControl\` next to an \`Input\` with \`className='h-9'\` in a \`flex items-center\` row looks aligned because they're vertically centered, but the visual heights differ — SegmentedControl inner toggles are \`h-8\` (32px, \`size='default'\` in \`toggle.tsx:16\`) while Input is 36px, and the outer wrapper adds \`border p-\[3px]\` making it ~38px. Fix: pass \`size='lg'\` to \`SegmentedControl\` so inner \`ToggleGroupItem\` uses \`h-9\` (36px), matching the Input height. Size variants in \`toggle.tsx\`: \`default\`=\`h-8\`, \`sm\`=\`h-7\`, \`lg\`=\`h-9\`.

<!-- lore:019f1337-b901-7bc1-afba-c5594112d007 -->
* **SelectContent position='item-aligned' breaks dark theme bg-popover on Windows**: Trap: \`SelectContent\` in \`apps/electron/src/renderer/src/components/ui/select.tsx:60\` defaulted to \`position='item-aligned'\` (non-upstream shadcn). In item-aligned mode, Radix wraps the listbox in extra positioning elements that don't carry \`bg-popover\`, so the dark theme list background doesn't render — list appears light while text stays dark-colored. Fix: change default to \`position='popper'\` (upstream shadcn default) — content renders directly in the styled \`Content\` element so \`bg-popover\` covers the full surface. Introduced in commit \`cb6ba80\` (#298 UI unification refactor), not a deliberate UX choice. All \`\<SelectContent>\` in \`settings.tsx\` and \`language-selector.tsx\` inherit this default.

<!-- lore:019f69de-4c86-7884-8556-449d62eca7e6 -->
* **Server connectivity test must probe authenticated endpoint — not just /api/health**: Trap: testing server connectivity with only \`/api/health\` looks sufficient because it confirms the server is reachable. But \`/api/health\` is always exempt from bearer-token auth, so a wrong token or a missing token on a token-protected server returns 200 — the test passes falsely. Fix: always probe an authenticated endpoint after the health check. Original \`testServer()\` flow in \`settings.tsx\`: (1) validate URL via \`serverUrlSchema\`, (2) \`checkServerHealth(base, 5000)\` → unreachable if false, (3) \`checkServerAuth(base, token, 5000)\` → unauthorized if false, (4) set \`'ok'\`. Both steps are required to catch wrong-token AND no-token cases.

<!-- lore:019ee7ed-0eb9-76ec-b7e7-b2c64eea40f5 -->
* **Server: add pre-warm endpoint and lazy ASR startup**: Added \`POST /api/transcribe/pre-warm\` endpoint that lazily starts whisper.cpp or MLX ASR server in the background. Both \`autoStartWhisperServer\` and \`autoStartMlxAsrServer\` removed; prewarm logic consolidated in the endpoint. Electron fires \`POST /api/transcribe/pre-warm\` on record start (while user speaks) so model load happens in parallel with recording. On submit, \`ensureServerRunning\`/\`ensureMlxServerRunning\` remains as fallback. Quit behavior unchanged — servers terminated via \`process.once('exit')\` handlers. No app restart required for server URL/token changes.

<!-- lore:019f6b63-4cb8-7885-9368-9600cfcfc93c -->
* **Server: remove autoStartMlxAsrServer dead function**: Removed exported but unused \`autoStartMlxAsrServer\` from \`apps/server/src/routes/mlx-asr.ts\`. Also removed its unused \`createAppLogger\` and \`log\` references. MLX server remains lazy-only; prewarm handled by the consolidated endpoint.

<!-- lore:019f6be5-ccf9-7839-94c0-4ba8ecc3693e -->
* **Server: remove autoStartWhisperServer boot call**: Removed \`autoStartWhisperServer()\` from \`setImmediate\` at app boot in \`apps/electron/src/main/index.ts\`. Whisper server now starts lazily via \`POST /api/transcribe/pre-warm\` fired on record start, or via \`ensureServerRunning\` at submission. Keeps \`reconcileUnsupportedMlxVoiceDefault()\`. Quit behavior unchanged — servers terminated via \`process.once('exit')\` handlers.

<!-- lore:019fb9e6-a60d-7087-96b6-46fabd92de07 -->
* **sessions table host-scoping fix (schema v17) — was a singleton row**: Historical bug: the \`sessions\` table was a singleton row (CHECK(id=1)) storing one cloud auth token regardless of host, so \`getSession()\`/\`setSession()\` ignored a \`host\` column and clobbered dev/prod tokens when switching FREESTYLE\_CLOUD\_URL. It looked fine because there was conceptually 'one' active cloud session. Fixed in schema v17: \`sessions\` rebuilt with \`host TEXT PRIMARY KEY\`; queries in apps/server/src/lib/sessions.ts (getSession/clearSession/touchSessionExpiry/setSession) now scope by \`host = freestyleCloudUrl()\`. Never assume a single global cloud session when touching this code.

<!-- lore:019f6552-8df0-7a8d-b215-6753b7b57dca -->
* **Settings toggle state lost on navigation — must patch SETTINGS\_QUERY\_KEY cache after PUT**: Trap: toggling a setting via PUT \`/api/settings/:key\` updates the server but not the React Query cache. When the user navigates away and back, \`useQuery(settingsQueryOptions())\` re-reads the stale cache and resets the toggle to its old value — looks like the PUT failed. Fix: after the PUT, call \`queryClient.setQueryData\<Record\<string, string>>(SETTINGS\_QUERY\_KEY, (prev) => ({ ...prev, \[key]: value }))\` to patch the cache in-place. \`useQueryClient()\` must be called in the same component that owns the toggle handler. The \`NetworkPanel\` and \`ExperimentalPanel\` (streaming audio toggle) both use this pattern as canonical references.

<!-- lore:019fcbf3-30dd-704c-9450-032f587d0db1 -->
* **startTimeRef=0 causes bogus elapsed-time readout on first pill press**: Trap: gating an elapsed-time UI readout on \`recording || initializing\` looks correct since both states mean 'timer should show', but on the very first press of a fresh process, \`startTimeRef\` hasn't been set yet (only set once mic is actually open) and defaults to 0 — \`Date.now() - 0\` instantly exceeds any threshold, producing a stray huge readout (e.g. "29763131:43") at the pill edge. Fix (apps/electron/src/renderer/src/pages/app.tsx): gate the readout on \`recording\` alone AND a non-zero start time, and reset the ref between sessions to prevent staleness carrying into the next recording.

<!-- lore:019f6b63-4cb3-767d-95ed-4879d2389e2b -->
* **stream.ts combined cloud path: empty-transcript guard required before applyFinalRewrites**: Trap: the Freestyle Cloud streaming combined path (cleanup enabled) had no empty guard before \`applyFinalRewrites\`, so an empty cloud response still ran hooks, captured telemetry, saved a blank history row, and sent \`final: ""\` — pasting nothing. The post-process-off branch already guarded at line 389, making the omission non-obvious. Fix: after computing \`cloudText = rawText?.trim() || ""\`, guard \`if (!cloudText) { ws.send(JSON.stringify({ type: 'final', text: '' })); return; }\` before entering the combined-mode block. Mirrors the same fix applied to \`transcribe.ts\`. Both routes must guard empty transcripts before any hook/history/telemetry code runs.

<!-- lore:019fcc31-5c2c-715e-9f78-a6e1ae1635e1 -->
* **streamer.ts onPartial removed by main, needed by remix card live transcript**: main branch removed onPartial/onCleaned from StreamerCallbacks because its own dictation pipeline stopped using partials — looks like safe dead-code removal. But remix-prototype's getRemixStreamer (app.tsx) still needs onPartial to render the live transcript in the Remix card UI, so merging main breaks it with TS2353. Fix: restore onPartial as OPTIONAL (\`onPartial?:\`), not required as in the pre-fork base, so main's existing streamer instantiations that omit it still typecheck. onCleaned was not restored — nothing in remix's streamer calls needs it.

<!-- lore:019fcbf1-d948-7f5b-993d-d4ef4eb157d5 -->
* **StreamerCallbacks.onPartial: keep optional when restoring, BarMode 'connecting' renamed 'listening'**: apps/electron/src/renderer/src/lib/streamer.ts's \`StreamerCallbacks.onPartial\` (live partial-transcript callback) was removed by main because its dictation pipeline stopped using partials, but other features (e.g. remix-prototype's live remix-chat transcript) still need it. When restoring, declare it OPTIONAL (\`onPartial?:\`) not required — main's existing streamer instantiations omit it and would fail typecheck if required. \`onCleaned\` was intentionally not restored (no current consumer). Also: main renamed \`BarMode\` value \`"connecting"\` → \`"listening"\` — any code calling \`startBarAnimation("connecting")\` must switch to \`"listening"\`, which renders a flat/idle bar row.

<!-- lore:019f6566-b1c0-7b86-bc12-ce1eef41337e -->
* **Streaming audio: gate stream route on freestyle-cloud flag**: The stream route (\`apps/server/src/routes/stream.ts\`) now checks \`getFlag('streaming\_audio')\` and returns 403 if the flag is not set. This ensures the streaming WebSocket path is only available when the experimental flag is enabled, preventing BYOK providers from being used with the streaming endpoint. The renderer's \`\_streamingAudioEnabled\` flag controls whether the UI opens the WebSocket; if the flag is false, the streamer falls back to the batch WAV POST path.

<!-- lore:019f65b0-dce6-749f-b8de-29b0b276037d -->
* **Streaming commit path: pendingCount never decremented — badge inflates monotonically**: Trap: \`setPendingCount((c) => c + 1)\` is called on the streaming commit path in \`app.tsx\` (line 893), but the \`transcribePromise\` resolve/reject paths never call \`setPendingCount((c) => Math.max(0, c - 1))\`. Looks correct because \`hidePill()\` resets the badge at line 664 — but for multiple streaming commits before pill hides, the count inflates and never returns to 0. Fix: always decrement \`pendingCount\` in the \`.finally()\` of \`transcribePromise\` on the streaming path, mirroring the batch path's decrement pattern.

<!-- lore:019fc2d6-cd13-766c-bdf9-6f36645ce544 -->
* **Streaming DO translate guard evaluates post-fallback languages, not raw payload**: In the Freestyle Cloud Durable Object (stt-session.ts), \`this.translate = !!msg.translate && this.languages?.length === 1\` is checked against \`this.languages\` AFTER merging payload ?? stored-prefs fallback, not the raw request. This means clients can send \`translate: true\` while omitting \`languages\` (relying on synced member\_preferences) and it still works IF the stored languages array resolves to exactly one entry. Two traps: (1) the Hinglish alias "hi-en" expands via normalizeLanguages to \`\["hi","en"]\` (length 2), silently disabling translate even though it looks like a single language; (2) if the D1 stored-prefs read fails, it fails open to null, making \`this.languages\` undefined and disabling translate with no error surfaced. Any future translate-related change must trace stored-language resolution, not just the request payload.

<!-- lore:019fd046-4e74-7cc8-8228-8e562e32288e -->
* **Streaming routes must be excluded from TIMEOUT\_PREFIXES or get killed at 30s**: Any route implementing a long-running streaming response (transcribe, post-process, and the remix agent route \`apps/server/src/routes/remix/agent.ts\`) must NOT be added to \`TIMEOUT\_PREFIXES\` in routes/index.ts — that middleware imposes a 30s cutoff which kills in-flight streams before the agent loop or model finishes. Looks like a reasonable default timeout to apply uniformly, but \`.lore.md\` records this exact trap having previously hit \`/api/org\`. When mounting a new streaming route, check it's excluded from TIMEOUT\_PREFIXES the same way transcribe/post-process are.

<!-- lore:019fc2ee-7529-7ca1-8195-05fb3dbef138 -->
* **Streaming translate flag: DO guards on resolved languages, not raw payload**: In the cloud streaming Durable Object (stt-session.ts), \`this.translate = !!msg.translate && this.languages?.length === 1\`, where \`this.languages\` is the POST-FALLBACK resolved list (\`payloadLanguages ?? storedLanguages\`), not the raw \`start\` message. So desktop/mobile can send \`translate: true\` and omit \`languages\` entirely (relying on synced member\_preferences) and translate still works — as long as the stored language list resolves to exactly one entry. Gotcha: if the stored language is the Hinglish alias \`hi-en\`, \`normalizeLanguages\` expands it to \`\["hi","en"]\` (length 2), silently disabling translate; also fails open (disables translate) if the D1 stored-prefs read errors. When trimming synced fields from a streaming start message, send \`translate\` unconditionally from the local setting — don't gate it client-side on local \`languages.length===1\`, since the real guard is evaluated cloud-side against resolved languages.

<!-- lore:019fc2de-0075-730b-ae87-8deab287936e -->
* **Streaming translate flag: no runtime validation, guard runs against post-fallback languages**: Cloud DO's \`start\` message field \`translate?: boolean\` (streaming/types.ts ClientMessage) has NO Zod/runtime validation — it's JSON.parse'd and cast straight through, so any truthy value flows. The translate guard \`this.translate = !!msg.translate && this.languages?.length === 1\` (stt-session.ts) is evaluated against \`this.languages\` AFTER the payload??stored fallback, not the raw payload — so desktop/mobile can send \`translate: true\` while omitting \`languages\` entirely (relying on synced member\_preferences), and translate still works IF the stored languages array resolves to exactly 1 entry. Caveats: a stored Hinglish alias like 'hi-en' expands to \`\['hi','en']\` (length 2, disables translate); if the D1 prefs read fails, it fails open to null languages (also disables translate). This let desktop drop the local \`languages?.length === 1\` gate when languages is no longer sent inline.

<!-- lore:019fb9cb-9ebe-79dd-b665-f297c5470ced -->
* **titleBarStyle hiddenInset macOS invisible native drag region**: On macOS, \`titleBarStyle: 'hiddenInset'\` creates an invisible native drag region at the top of the window (~28px tall) that overlays the topbar and swallows pointer events. CSS \`WebkitAppRegion: 'drag'\` on the page wrapper does NOT override this native region. Fix: change to \`titleBarStyle: 'hidden'\` and rely solely on CSS drag regions. The \`DragSpacer\` component (28px wide with \`mr-\[72px]\`) becomes the only drag area, leaving top-right buttons fully clickable. Traffic lights remain via \`trafficLightPosition\`.

<!-- lore:019f3c03-ef17-7966-8d77-0fa963aaeb98 -->
* **tone.tsx cloudVoiceActive banner short-circuit wrong under three-mode — use llmCleanup/hasCleanupModel instead**: Trap: \`cloudVoiceActive\` (voice provider === freestyle-cloud) was used to suppress cleanup banners in \`tone.tsx\`, which looks correct because 'cloud voice always post-processes.' Under three-mode, cloud voice active no longer implies cleanup is running — user may have cloud voice with a local LLM for cleanup. Fix: remove \`cloudVoiceActive\` short-circuit from banner block entirely; let generic \`llmCleanup\`/\`hasCleanupModel\` logic handle all cases including cloud. The \`cloudVoiceActive\` variable was removed from \`tone.tsx\` state entirely after this fix.

<!-- lore:019fc26f-9a3e-759e-bc7f-594c2d179bd6 -->
* **UIScrollView height ambiguous with lessThanOrEqualToConstant + contentLayoutGuide**: In iOS UIKit, pinning a subview to a UIScrollView's \`contentLayoutGuide\` only defines \`contentSize\`, not the scroll view's own frame height. Constraining the scroll view's height with \`lessThanOrEqualToConstant\` looks correct (it 'caps' height) but leaves the frame height underconstrained/ambiguous since there's no other frame-defining constraint. Fix: use \`constraint(equalToConstant:)\` for a definite frame height. Short content stays top-aligned; overflow triggers scroll-to-bottom logic. Found in apps/mobile/ios-keyboard/KeyboardViewController.swift's \`statusScroll\` (PR #533).

<!-- lore:019f1672-dce4-74ab-a45a-6aa4cce2fada -->
* **Vertical/dockless monitor: bottomOverlap=14 clips pill at screen edge**: Trap: \`bottomOverlap = 14\` in \`getAppWindowPosition()\` (index.ts line 378) pushes bottom-positioned pill 14px past \`workArea\` bottom. On primary display with a dock, \`workArea.bottom < physical bottom\` so the overlap is hidden behind the dock — looks correct. On secondary/vertical monitors with no dock, \`workArea.bottom == physical bottom\` → pill is clipped ~6px below screen edge. Fix: compute \`bottomInset = display.bounds.y + display.bounds.height - (display.workArea.y + display.workArea.height)\`; only apply \`bottomOverlap\` when \`bottomInset < 14\` (i.e., dock is present and large enough to absorb it).

<!-- lore:019f6a56-d8cc-7a1e-a431-4cd1b20f9f11 -->
* **Vite dev server: disable dep optimizer for electron renderer**: Workaround for \`Could not resolve "zod/v4/core"\` and \`Could not resolve "react"\` in electron renderer Vite dev server: set \`optimizeDeps?.esbuildOptions?.target = 'es2020'\` and ensure \`optimizeDeps?.include\` lists all problematic deps explicitly, or disable esbuild optimization by adding \`optimizeDeps?.disableEsbuild = true\` in \`apps/electron/electron.vite.config.ts\`. Disabling esbuild avoids the broken resolution path while Vite resolves correct entries. This is a temporary mitigation until pnpm/hookform/resolvers alignment is fixed upstream.

<!-- lore:019fc2d6-cd0c-70cb-b520-c5ea349e583f -->
* **Vocabulary note text not synced — dropped from cloud transcribe/stream paths**: Cloud-synced vocabulary (member\_preferences.vocabulary) only stores \`terms: string\[]\`, never the note-derived \`text\` field used as ASR bias context (desktop's CloudVocabularyBias.text, Soniox \`context.text\`). After the Aug 2026 refactor to stop sending saved vocabulary inline to Freestyle Cloud, users relying on vocabulary notes lose that bias context on cloud transcribe/stream requests — terms still bias recognition via the synced row, but note text does not. This looks like a lossless simplification (vocabulary is 'already synced') but silently drops a feature. User explicitly accepted this regression ("Drop vocabulary too") pending future note-sync support. Per-request plugin overrides of vocabulary (via beforeTranscribe hook bias) are unaffected — those still forward inline since they can't come from the synced row.

<!-- lore:019fc2c4-8a15-777c-974a-142c4d1cd15a -->
* **Vocabulary note-derived text is never synced to cloud member\_preferences**: Cloud vocabulary sync (pushVocabularyToCloud) only pushes the \`terms\` array to member\_preferences.vocabulary — the note-derived \`text\` field (built by getCloudVocabularyBias(), used as ASR/cleanup bias context) is never synced. After trimming vocabulary from cloud transcribe/streaming requests to rely on synced member\_preferences, cloud loses note-text bias entirely on those paths for users relying on vocabulary notes. This is an accepted regression per explicit user decision ('drop vocabulary too', Aug 2 2026) rather than keeping vocabulary sent inline. To restore note-text bias, either sync \`text\` to cloud too, or keep vocabulary forwarded inline on cloud paths.

<!-- lore:019fcc62-d600-730e-99e4-339ee53ca8cb -->
* **Voice pill animation bugs (app.tsx): enter-transition, borderRadius, elapsed timer**: Three bugs fixed in PR #541 (apps/electron/src/renderer/src/pages/app.tsx, voice pill): (1) Enter animation never fired because React mounted the pill subtree with data-show="true" already set — CSS transitions keyed on that attribute don't fire without a post-mount change. Fix: hold state at false for one committed frame, then flip true. (2) Handover corner stuck at 20px: inline style borderRadius:20 on pill-card silently overrode the animated CSS rule \`.pill-card\[data-show="false"]{border-radius:60px}\` — inline styles always beat stylesheet rules regardless of animation; masked because scale animation drew the eye elsewhere. Fix: never set inline style for a property a stylesheet also animates. (3) Elapsed-timer garbage: gated on \`recording || initializing\`, but startTimeRef stays 0 until mic actually opens, so Date.now()-0 showed a huge stray number on first startup. Fix: gate on \`recording\` alone + non-zero start-time check; reset ref between sessions.

<!-- lore:019fcbf9-8b3d-7211-926c-5f68b070a66c -->
* **Voice pill arrival animation never ran — data-show=true set before first paint**: Trap: React mounted the pill subtree with \`data-show="true"\` already set on the initial render, so the CSS enter transition never fires — CSS transitions don't trigger on first paint since there's no prior state to transition from. Looks correct because the DOM ends up in the right final state either way. Fix (PR #541, apps/electron/src/renderer/src/pages/app.tsx): hold \`data-show="false"\` for one committed frame, then flip to true so the transition has a starting state to animate from. Bars then stagger right-to-left via an index param in the BAR\_X\_POSITIONS.map callback.

<!-- lore:019fcc57-e852-73e2-9968-9bcb20c109e3 -->
* **Voice pill arrival transition no-ops if data-show mounts already true**: In apps/electron/src/renderer/src/pages/app.tsx, the pill's arrival/enter CSS transition never fired because React mounted the subtree with \`data-show="true"\` already set — since the DOM's first-painted state matches the transition's target, there's no state change for the browser to animate, so it looks broken but is really a no-op. Fix: hold the initial/false state for one committed frame, then flip the attribute on a subsequent frame so the transition has an actual state change to animate. Applies to any CSS transition intended to run 'on mount' — initial and target states must differ across at least two renders, not be set identically at mount.

<!-- lore:019fcc21-d18a-7b61-8641-f4b7246ff9a3 -->
* **Voice pill arrival: CSS transition doesn't fire on synchronous mount**: In apps/electron/src/renderer/src/pages/app.tsx, the voice pill's enter transition existed but never played: React mounted the subtree with data-show='true' already set, so the CSS transition had no prior state to animate from (transitions need a state \*change\*, not just a value present at first paint). Fix (PR #541): hold data-show='false' for one committed frame, then flip to true, giving CSS a real transition to run. General rule: any enter animation gated by a data-attribute/class set synchronously during initial render needs a delayed one-frame flip, not a mount-time value.

<!-- lore:019fcbdf-3776-7b48-a4f4-cd8773099e99 -->
* **Voice pill arrival: CSS transition never fires when data-show set at mount**: In apps/electron/src/renderer/src/pages/app.tsx's voice pill, React mounted the subtree with data-show="true" already set, so the CSS transition bound to that attribute never fired on first paint — transitions require a state change, not just an initial value, even though the CSS rule itself was correct. Fixed in PR #541: hold data-show="false" for one committed React frame, then flip to true, forcing a genuine transition; bars then stagger right-to-left via an index param on BAR\_X\_POSITIONS.map. Applies to any enter-animation in this pill gated by a data-attribute.

<!-- lore:019fcc12-2403-7fd6-b74c-d2bf0b5ca2ed -->
* **Voice pill CSS enter transition doesn't fire on first paint**: In apps/electron/src/renderer/src/pages/app.tsx, the pill's arrival CSS transition never fired because React mounted the subtree with \`data-show="true"\` already set — CSS transitions require a property \*change\*, not an initial value, so nothing triggers on first paint. Looks correct because the enter-transition CSS rule exists and 'should' just work. Fix (PR #541): mount with \`data-show="false"\`, hold for one committed frame, then flip to \`true\` so the transition has an actual before/after to animate across; bars then stagger right-to-left. Any future enter-animation in app.tsx/pill components must force this initial-false-then-flip sequence — never set the 'shown' attribute synchronously at mount.

<!-- lore:019fcc04-6df9-7d04-9e2b-79dbc58533ea -->
* **Voice pill CSS enter transition needs held frame before data-show flip**: In the electron voice pill (apps/electron/.../pages/app.tsx), the CSS enter transition never fired because React mounted the subtree with \`data-show="true"\` already set on first paint — CSS transitions require a prior committed frame with the initial state to detect a change. Looks correct because the element renders fine at rest, but the animation silently skips. Fix (PR #541): hold \`data-show="false"\` for one committed frame, then flip to \`true\` on the next frame so the transition fires; bars stagger right-to-left via an index param added to the BAR\_X\_POSITIONS.map callback.

<!-- lore:019fcc41-9449-7acb-943e-b593e7b2d660 -->
* **Voice pill CSS enter transition needs hold-then-release**: apps/electron's \`.pill-surface\` starts at \`opacity: 0; scale(0)\` and relies on a CSS transition to enter. If React mounts the subtree with \`data-show="true"\` already set on the same commit, the browser never observes a state change — it just paints the final state, so the transition never fires. Looks correct because the DOM ends up right, but no animation plays. Fix (PR #541, apps/electron/src/renderer/src/pages/app.tsx): hold \`data-show="false"\` for one committed frame after mount, then flip to \`true\` on a later frame so the browser has an actual transition to animate from. Bars stagger right-to-left via an index param added to the \`BAR\_X\_POSITIONS.map\` callback.

<!-- lore:019fcc09-57f8-7fe0-b5ca-47f463e77a6a -->
* **Voice pill CSS transitions don't fire when React mounts with data-show already true**: In apps/electron/src/renderer/src/pages/app.tsx, mounting the pill subtree with \`data-show="true"\` already set prevents \`\[data-show="true"]\` CSS transitions from firing — browsers treat the mounted value as the initial style, not a transition target. This looks correct because the state naturally reflects 'shown' on mount, but the entrance animation silently never runs. Fix: hold \`data-show="false"\` for one committed React frame, then flip to \`true\`, giving the transition a defined start state. Same principle applies to \`.pill-surface\` opacity/scale entrance and the border-radius morph — first-paint state must differ from the target state for CSS transitions to trigger.

<!-- lore:019fcc10-4945-7457-9d3b-b58c9439e562 -->
* **Voice pill CSS/inline-style traps in app.tsx (arrival, morph, timer)**: Three gotchas found reviewing PR #541 (voice pill motion, apps/electron/src/renderer/src/pages/app.tsx): (1) CSS enter-transitions don't fire on first paint if React mounts the subtree with \`data-show="true"\` already set — there's no state change to trigger the transition. Fix: hold for one committed frame then flip the attribute. (2) Inline style (e.g. \`borderRadius: 20\`) silently outranks an animated CSS rule (\`.pill-card\[data-show="false"] { border-radius: 60px }\`) — corner stays pinned through the whole morph, masked by the concurrent scale animation so it looks fine on screen. Rule: never set inline what the stylesheet animates. (3) Elapsed-time readout was gated on \`recording || initializing\`, but \`startTimeRef\` is only set once mic is actually open — on first press it was still 0, so \`Date.now()-0\` showed a huge stray timestamp. Fixed by gating on \`recording\` alone plus a non-zero start time, and resetting the ref between sessions.

<!-- lore:019fcbea-6293-7e9f-9487-8f648762ae13 -->
* **Voice pill elapsed-time readout: stale startTimeRef bug**: apps/electron pill's elapsed-time readout was gated on recording||initializing, but startTimeRef is only set once the mic actually opens. On first press of a fresh process startTimeRef was still 0, so Date.now()-0 instantly exceeded the 60s threshold and showed a garbage timestamp (e.g. '29763131:43') at the pill's right edge. Fixed in PR #541: gate on recording alone plus a non-zero start time, and reset the ref between sessions to prevent staleness.

<!-- lore:019fcbe5-7d6b-7480-b1af-95e87ef10499 -->
* **Voice pill enter transition never fires on React first mount**: In apps/electron/src/renderer/src/pages/app.tsx, the pill's CSS enter transition looked correctly wired but never played: React mounted the subtree with \`data-show="true"\` already set on the very first render, so the CSS transition had no prior state to animate from (CSS transitions don't fire on first paint, only on subsequent attribute changes). Fix: hold the show-state false for one committed frame, then flip it true on a later frame so the browser actually observes a state change to transition. Bars then stagger right-to-left. Documented in specs/voice-pill-motion.md (PR #541).

<!-- lore:019fcc44-1074-7f22-b35c-e1ac0b837a72 -->
* **Voice pill enter transition: data-show mount-state trap**: In apps/electron/src/renderer/src/pages/app.tsx, the voice pill's enter transition never ran because React mounted the subtree with data-show="true" already set — CSS transitions don't fire on first paint, only on subsequent state changes. It looks correct because the animated CSS rule exists and the DOM eventually reaches the right state. Fix (PR #541): hold data-show="false" for one committed frame, then flip to true so the transition actually fires; bars stagger right-to-left via an index passed into BAR\_X\_POSITIONS.map.

<!-- lore:019fcbea-624c-7ae6-9b36-69f52a6be11e -->
* **Voice pill: CSS transition + inline-style traps (specs/voice-pill-motion.md)**: apps/electron renderer pill (app.tsx) hit two CSS traps fixed in PR #541: (1) React mounted the subtree with data-show="true" already set, so the enter transition never fired — browsers skip a transition when start state equals end state on first paint. Fix: hold data-show="false" for one committed frame then flip to true. (2) An inline style borderRadius:20 always outranks the animated CSS rule .pill-card\[data-show="false"]{border-radius:60px} regardless of specificity — looked fine because a simultaneous scale animation masked the pinned corner. Rule: never set inline a property the stylesheet animates.

<!-- lore:019f7af5-5db5-703f-a31c-a6b82cd1cfef -->
* **Windows and Linux are NOT affected by the macOS Fn/Globe desync bug**: Windows uses \`GetAsyncKeyState\` polling which self-heals modifier state; Linux uses uinput with balanced press/release events and no modifier-state accumulation. Neither platform is affected by the macOS-specific desync. No changes required for Windows or Linux key-listener code.

<!-- lore:019fb972-64c5-7bee-b803-27ad5123879c -->
* **ws optional deps bufferutil utf-8-validate require() wrapped in try/catch**: The \`ws\` package uses optional dependencies \`bufferutil\` and \`utf-8-validate\` with runtime \`try/catch\`. When Vite bundles \`ws\`, those \`require()\` calls become static ESM imports that throw at module evaluation before the \`try/catch\` can catch them. Marking them as external preserves the runtime \`require()\` inside the \`try/catch\`, allowing graceful fallback to ws's pure-JavaScript implementation.

### Housekeeping

<!-- lore:019fb9bb-5993-7122-97bc-f5bf2a5dc1c8 -->
* **chore: update lore knowledge after PR #451 merge**: PR #451 (branch \`perf/electron-startup-cost\`) added a \`.lore.md\` commit to avoid checkout-blocking. Updated the knowledge entry to reflect that \`.lore.md\` must be committed first before branch switches and that PRs #451 and #453 have no file overlap and can be reviewed independently.

### Knowledge

<!-- lore:019fb991-3a76-7e02-abfd-a38326c543a4 -->
* **PostHog: attach app version + environment to all events**: Added \`process.env.FREESTYLE\_APP\_VERSION = app.getVersion()\` in \`apps/electron/src/main/index.ts\` and registered global super properties (\`app\_version\`, \`environment\`) with \`isServer: false\` in \`getClient()\` in \`apps/server/src/lib/posthog.ts\`. Removed redundant inline \`environment\` from \`capture()\` and \`captureException()\`. All PostHog events (manual, explicit, and autocaptured exceptions) now carry \`app\_version\` and \`environment\`, enabling filtering by release in PostHog. No breaking changes; event-level props still override super properties.

### Optimization

<!-- lore:019fb9b8-93d6-7629-a489-c8bcb84ab5d5 -->
* **buildAsrBiasPrompt optimize vocabulary dedup and budget packing**: Optimize \`buildAsrBiasPrompt\` in \`packages/stt/src/asr-bias.ts\`: - Deduplicate terms case-insensitively before sorting; preserve first-seen order for equal-priority terms. - Sort by priority (custom > recent > vocabulary) then by length DESC, then by insertion order ASC for stable tie-break. - Pack terms greedily within the PROMPT\_CHAR\_BUDGET (900), respecting the "Terms: " prefix, and stop when the next term would exceed the budget. - Ensure the output always ends with a period and never exceeds PROMPT\_CHAR\_BUDGET — use \`.slice(0, PROMPT\_CHAR\_BUDGET)\` after concatenation. - Keep the existing deepgram/elevenlabs caps as they are; no other provider-specific limits needed. - Add a small unit test for dedup + budget edge cases (overlapping terms, mixed priorities, exact budget boundary).

### Pattern

<!-- lore:019fbc97-18ef-7f60-abb5-4cdb5e39c76f -->
* **'auto' language option is always pinned first via pinnedFirst param**: In \`packages/validations/src/cloud-config.ts\`, \`resolveLanguageOptions()\`/list-ordering logic takes a \`pinnedFirst: readonly string\[] = \["auto"]\` param — pinned codes are always kept at the front of the suggested-language order regardless of the cloud's \`suggestedLanguages\` ordering, so \`"auto"\` is always surfaced first in any language picker (settings.tsx LanguageCombobox, onboarding.tsx LanguageStep pills). Onboarding's \`LanguageStep\` independently reimplements the 'auto gets its own pill' rule (excludes 'auto' from the first-12 pill slice and renders it as a separate always-visible pill) rather than relying on pinnedFirst ordering alone — keep both mechanisms in sync if refactoring the picker.

<!-- lore:019f0ebe-4531-783a-afe9-70a20376c163 -->
* **Add authClientErrorStatus helper for consistent error status extraction**: Added \`authClientErrorStatus(error)\` helper in \`apps/server/src/lib/freestyle-cloud.ts\` to centralize extracting the numeric status from better-auth errors. Used by \`fetchCloudUser\` to identify 401 and by \`cloudJson\` for consistent error classification. Confidence: 0.9

<!-- lore:019faf4f-7e23-7d2b-aa74-b0f83a0af4b7 -->
* **Add new cloud-proxied auth operation: schema→cloud-lib→route→hook→UI**: Steps: 1. Add Zod schema to packages/validations/src/auth.ts (e.g. unlinkAccountSchema) — shared validation contract between client and server. 2. Add a function to apps/server/src/lib/freestyle-cloud.ts that POSTs/fetches the cloud endpoint using the server-side session token (e.g. unlinkCloudAccount()). 3. Add a route in apps/server/src/routes/auth.ts that validates the body against the schema, calls the lib function, and never returns raw tokens. 4. Add a mutation hook in apps/electron/src/renderer/src/lib/use-profile.ts (or equivalent) that calls the route via Hono RPC and invalidates the relevant query cache on success. 5. Wire UI in the renderer, guarding against invalid states (e.g. disable a 'Disconnect' button with tooltip when it's the user's only sign-in method to avoid lockout). Verify: - \[ ] typecheck passes across all touched packages. - \[ ] no token field appears in any HTTP response body.

<!-- lore:019fbca6-2b5f-7216-9e72-10ae91b80598 -->
* **apps/mobile targets Expo v57 — check versioned docs before writing code**: apps/mobile/AGENTS.md warns "Expo HAS CHANGED": before writing any mobile code, read versioned docs at https://docs.expo.dev/versions/v57.0.0/ rather than relying on general/cached Expo knowledge, since APIs and patterns differ across Expo versions. Project convention: apps/mobile targets Expo v57 specifically.

<!-- lore:019faf89-ec19-76d8-bf88-bd67769c7fb5 -->
* **Auth/org zod schemas live in packages/validations/src/auth.ts, never inline in route files**: Convention: request-body validation schemas for auth/org/billing routes must be defined in \`packages/validations/src/auth.ts\` and exported (e.g. \`unlinkAccountSchema\`, \`linkSocialSchema\`, \`SetActiveOrgInput\`), not inline as \`z.string()\` etc. directly in \`apps/server/src/routes/\*.ts\`. Caught during PR review: \`setActiveOrgSchema\` was initially defined inline in \`org.ts\` — moved to the validations package to match the existing pattern so schemas are shared/reusable and consistently located.

<!-- lore:019fd03f-b239-7ea7-a449-4ac029e8e9dc -->
* **beUI vendored components under components/agents + components/motion (shadcn registry)**: Freestyle vendors MIT-licensed beUI agent components (shadcn registry entry \`@beui\` → https://beui.dev/r/{name}.json in apps/electron/components.json) directly into components/agents/\* and components/motion/\*, same convention as existing components/ui/\*, rather than depending on an npm package. Vendored: agent-activity, message-scroller, prompt-input, streaming-response, thinking-shimmer. Deliberately NOT vendored: tool-result and agent-code, because they pull in shiki for syntax highlighting and Remix's tool-result disclosure only needs JSON display, not source rendering. Introduces \`motion\` (motion/react) as a dependency — first use of Motion in apps/electron — gated by \`LazyMotion features={domMax} strict\`, which requires \`m.\*\` (not \`motion.\*\`) usage throughout consumers.

<!-- lore:019fc267-a2ba-7cf7-b769-2f60fe871be9 -->
* **CI diagnosis: compare PR-run vs main-push job conclusions for same SHA**: Steps: 1. When a Build & Test run fails on \`main\` right after a merge, check the CI run for the same commit SHA on its PR branch — query job conclusions via \`gh run view/list\` for both runs. 2. If the identical commit's jobs passed on the PR run but fail on the push-to-main run, classify as flaky/infra failure (runner resource exhaustion, transient hang), not a code regression — since no code changed between the two runs. 3. Re-run only the failed jobs (\`gh run rerun \<run-id> --failed\`) rather than proposing code changes. Gotchas: - A failure that looks code-related (timeout, generic build error with no stack trace) can still be pure CI flakiness if the same SHA passed elsewhere — don't jump to a code fix. Verify: - \[ ] Job conclusions for the SHA on PR run == passing for the jobs that failed on main. - \[ ] Re-run of failed-only jobs succeeds without any code change.

<!-- lore:019fc26f-0acc-7b53-9726-e5c40e4d2e37 -->
* **CI triage: diff PR-run vs merge-run job conclusions to rule out regression**: Steps: 1. When a Build & Test run fails on \`main\` (push trigger) but the same commit's PR run succeeded, fetch job conclusions for the PR run via \`gh run view \<pr\_run\_id> --json jobs\` and compare per-job conclusion to the failing main run. 2. If the identical commit passed the same jobs on the PR, classify as flaky/infra rather than a code regression — do not start editing code. 3. Re-run only the failed jobs with \`gh run rerun \<run\_id> --failed\` instead of proposing a fix. 4. Only propose hardening changes (e.g. Playwright \`retries\`, freeing CI disk space) after confirming flakiness, and let the user decide whether to implement them. Gotchas: - A generic native-build error (no stack trace) after a long silent hang is often CI runner resource exhaustion, not a code bug — don't chase a root cause in application code. Verify: - \[ ] \`gh run rerun --failed\` job conclusions become success. - \[ ] No code changes were made for confirmed-flaky failures.

<!-- lore:019fc267-3682-7483-a9bf-7899db558eb3 -->
* **CI: diagnose main-only failures via PR-run job comparison**: Steps: 1. Get job conclusions for the failing push-triggered run on main (\`gh run view \<run-id> --json jobs\`). 2. Find the same commit's PR run (pre-merge) and get its job conclusions for the same job names. 3. If all failing jobs passed on the PR run for the identical commit SHA, classify as flaky/infra rather than a code regression — the code is unchanged between PR and push runs. 4. Re-run only the failed jobs via \`gh run rerun \<run-id> --failed\` instead of pushing a code change. Gotchas: - A failure appearing only after merge looks like a merge-order or environment regression, but since main's run is the exact same commit SHA as the PR run, a PR-run pass proves the code isn't at fault — the delta is runner/environment noise. Verify: - \[ ] Failing job names match between PR run and main run. - \[ ] Job conclusions for those names were "success" on the PR run. - \[ ] Rerun of failed jobs transitions to success without code changes.

<!-- lore:019fbca6-2b53-776c-a63a-fc137f02d3f4 -->
* **cloud-config.ts language helpers: resolveLanguageOptions/filterLanguageOptions/orderBySuggestedLanguages**: packages/validations/src/cloud-config.ts exports shared language-list helpers used by both mobile and desktop. \`orderBySuggestedLanguages(options, suggested, getCode, pinnedFirst=\["auto"])\` puts pinned codes at front, then cloud-suggested languages, then remaining options in stable original order. \`resolveLanguageOptions(suggested, localFallback, pinnedFirst)\` uses cloud's ~66-language Soniox set (pre-sorted English→region→alphabetical) as source of truth when present, else falls back to the bundled local list — "auto" is always resolved from the local list since the cloud set has no auto entry. \`filterLanguageOptions(options, query, pinnedFirst)\` does case-insensitive label/code substring filtering but always keeps pinned entries visible regardless of match, so the default option is never hidden by search.

<!-- lore:019fb9ae-e087-7496-9dc2-38581d6ec87d -->
* **Cloud-sync API endpoints (cloud repo)**: Endpoints (cloud service): GET /v1/settings/sync — returns merged config + timestamps; POST /v1/settings/sync — accepts partial patch with client timestamps and merges server-wins merge semantics; GET /v1/settings/schema — returns the allowlist schema and per-key metadata. Use better-auth session binding; require authenticated bearer token; return 409 with merge details when conflicts detected. Validate with Zod schemas defined in packages/validations/src/cloud-sync.ts. Background sync triggered by desktop via authenticated HTTP call; no streaming or long-polling.

<!-- lore:019eda79-a6cf-7977-ab47-e17f20c8f6fd -->
* **Custom prompt save: explicit Save button pattern (not debounced autosave)**: The cleanup custom prompt uses an explicit Save button instead of debounced autosave. \`use-models.ts\`: \`setCleanupCustomPrompt\` only updates local state; \`saveCleanupCustomPrompt()\` does the \`PUT /api/settings/cleanup\_custom\_prompt\` and toggles \`savingCustomPrompt\`. \`customPromptDirty\` is derived (current text ≠ last saved baseline). \`cleanup-intensity.tsx\`: Save button (\`variant="ink" size="sm"\`) shown only in Custom mode. Three states: saving (Loader2 spinner), dirty (enabled), clean (disabled). Rationale: debounced autosave had an unmount-flush gap — navigating away within 500ms silently dropped the last edit because \`clearTimeout\` on unmount cancelled the pending PUT without flushing it.

<!-- lore:019fb9ae-e090-7ee5-b48d-1ed21945d6fe -->
* **Desktop cloud-sync trigger and background task**: Desktop: on login (auth-context), fetch last-synced timestamp; if older than check interval (e.g. 6 hours) or force flag, trigger background sync (non-blocking). Expose a manual 'Sync now' button in Settings > Cloud Sync that calls the cloud endpoint and updates last-synced timestamp on success. Use exponential backoff for transient network errors; surface non-transient errors in UI but never block the main thread. Store last-synced timestamp in settings.json (not SQLite) so it's available before server is reachable.

<!-- lore:019fb98d-f58c-7bc3-9b0f-72327d425b32 -->
* **devPlugins option for local plugin testing**: Add a \`devPlugins\` option to the server plugin loader (apps/server/src/lib/plugins/loader.ts) that, in dev mode, automatically discovers and loads workspace plugins from the repo's \`plugins/\` directory. These are separate from the user's \`plugins\`/\`disabled\_plugins\` settings — they're always loaded in dev, never in production builds. Implementation: detect dev mode via \`FREESTYLE\_ENV !== 'production'\` or \`is.dev\`, resolve repo-relative \`plugins/\`, scan for directories with \`package.json\` containing a \`freestyle\` field, and import them the same way \`resolveLocalPackage\` does. No env var or config changes required.

<!-- lore:019fcbfb-ee84-7346-a796-50fd134d98d8 -->
* **Direct push to main bypasses branch protection when explicitly requested**: freestyle-voice/freestyle's \`main\` has branch protection (required 'CI Status' check + 'changes must be made through a PR'), but Aditya Mathur has admin rights to push directly and bypass both when he explicitly asks for it (e.g. small CI workflow fixes). This is intentional escape-hatch usage, not a bug — only do this when the user explicitly requests committing/pushing straight to main; otherwise follow the normal PR flow per \[\[019f4763-ee22-7190-aa87-ecd1909810f9]] (never commit unless instructed).

<!-- lore:019fb9cb-9ec9-7831-a795-462b45c675be -->
* **DragSpacer component for topbar button protection**: New \`DragSpacer\` component (\`apps/electron/src/renderer/src/components/drag-spacer.tsx\`) with \`WebkitAppRegion: 'drag'\` and \`h-7 shrink-0 mr-\[72px]\`. The right margin keeps the top-right action buttons outside the drag region. Each page should use this as the sole drag region and remove outer wrapper \`drag\` + inner content \`no-drag\`.

<!-- lore:019fb9c8-fa3e-737e-be4a-cbca9a0b2dc6 -->
* **Electron plugin bridge and IPC integration**: Tests that plugin UI pages can call window.freestyle.api.\* and receive correct responses, and that plugin middleware can communicate with the host via IPC. Verifies pluginApiGuard allows only /api/plugins/\* and /api/health from plugin origins. Located in apps/electron/tests/plugin-bridge.test.ts.

<!-- lore:019fb9c8-fa36-71db-8ecf-e1942631d285 -->
* **Electron plugin E2E: sidebar nav, page load, enable/disable UI**: Playwright E2E tests in apps/electron/tests/plugins.test.ts: plugin list renders, sidebar nav items appear, plugin detail page loads, enable/disable toggle updates UI and disables/enables middleware, plugin pages navigate correctly, and plugin bridge errors are handled gracefully.

<!-- lore:019fbc93-bba3-76a1-a8e9-a155264ec744 -->
