<!-- lore:019fb9c5-c81d-7878-9915-8a31fb0e2545 -->
* **Restore Soniox model to CLOUD\_VOICE\_PROVIDERS list**: User preference: Soniox (\`soniox/stt-rt-v4\`) should be present in \`CLOUD\_VOICE\_PROVIDERS\` in \`apps/electron/src/renderer/src/lib/models.ts\` alongside other cloud voice providers. This keeps the model catalog consistent even when streaming is disabled — batch transcription still works with Soniox via the v2 API. Add back the Soniox entry with providerId \`soniox\`, modelId \`soniox/stt-rt-v4\`, family \`soniox\`, type \`voice\`.

<!-- lore:019f1672-dce3-782d-8f9d-6a9ffe93ab8a -->
* **Restore system audio volume before playing stop tone (restore-then-tone ordering)**: User explicitly confirmed: when audio ducking is active, system volume must be restored before \`playTone('stop')\` fires. Ordering: \`await restoreSystemAudioSafely()\` → \`playTone('stop')\`. Rationale: tone plays at full volume (audible), not at \`DUCKED\_VOLUME=0.15\`. This is the mirror of the start-tone fix (play start tone before ducking begins).

<!-- lore:019fb9e6-a5dd-70b5-a99e-f93fa529fbc5 -->
* **Reuse existing wrapper before duplicating env-read+fallback logic**: Before adding code that reads an env var with a default fallback (e.g. \`process.env.FREESTYLE\_CLOUD\_URL\`), always search for an existing wrapper function that already provides that behavior (e.g. \`freestyleCloudUrl()\` in apps/server/src/lib/freestyle-cloud.ts:236) and reuse it rather than duplicating read+fallback logic inline. This directive has recurred across 20+ sessions — treat any new inline env-read+default as a signal to search first.

<!-- lore:019f7bed-7f6d-72f1-b6c1-2229db1543d2 -->
* **Review code before committing**: Behavioral pattern detected across 8 sessions (action: requested-review). The user consistently demonstrates this behavior.

<!-- lore:019faf89-ec28-7918-9956-aa46c7f4dfd8 -->
* **Run apps/electron typecheck after every change during a session**: User runs \`tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.web.json --composite false\` (the \`typecheck:web\` script for \`@freestyle-voice/electron\`) after nearly every discrete change within a session — not just at the end — including after small UI tweaks, hook additions, and bug fixes. Treat typecheck as a required checkpoint between edits, not a one-time final gate. Observed 6+ times in one session (profile redesign, org endpoints, org UI, dropdown fixes, layout restoration).

<!-- lore:019fb9a9-d8c0-7c6c-b739-35b91aa3023b -->
* **saveSetting res.ok guard**: Added \`if (!res.ok) throw new Error(...)\` in \`saveSetting\` in \`tone.tsx\`. This ensures the existing \`.catch\` handlers fire on server 400 responses (e.g. validation errors), preventing silent failures when the server rejects a setting update.

<!-- lore:019fb9ce-d89a-7e32-b6cc-f84853439277 -->
* **Search bar height match SegmentedControl**: In plugins.tsx, change the search input wrapper height from \`h-9\` to \`h-10\` so the input visually aligns with the SegmentedControl (which uses \`h-9\`). This prevents vertical mismatch in the filter bar.

<!-- lore:019fb1fd-8e05-7b6f-8822-6a5b732e5e96 -->
* **Search for existing env-var wrapper before reimplementing default-fallback logic**: When an env var has a default-value fallback (e.g. \`FREESTYLE\_CLOUD\_URL\` falling back to \`DEFAULT\_CLOUD\_URL\`), always search for and reuse the existing wrapper function rather than re-reading \`process.env.X\` directly with new default logic. User directive (stated explicitly): "that env has a default value too so check for a wrapper function which we might already have to use here." Example: \`freestyleCloudUrl()\` in \`apps/server/src/lib/freestyle-cloud.ts\` is the canonical resolver — reuse it instead of duplicating \`process.env.FREESTYLE\_CLOUD\_URL ?? DEFAULT\_CLOUD\_URL\` logic elsewhere.

<!-- lore:019f6be5-ccfc-7ff3-9404-0108dcb33c2d -->
* **Server URL/token UI must appear in Network tab, after enterprise proxy/CA section**: User directive: the server URL and token configuration UI must be placed in the 'Network' tab of settings, after the enterprise proxy/CA section (after the \`envNote\` div). Implemented as \`ServerConnectionCard\` inserted into \`NetworkPanel\`'s \`SettingsPanel\` after the enterprise section.

<!-- lore:019f433a-5963-736b-8c79-5d1fc55fbd39 -->
* **Settings architecture: three-layer persistence (SQLite, settings.json, .env.local)**: The app uses three distinct persistence layers: (A) SQLite \`settings\` table for server-persisted settings (selectedDevice, hotkey, language, outputMode, soundEnabled, historyRetention, etc.) — written via \`PUT /api/settings/:key\`; (B) \`settings.json\` in Electron main for machine-specific local settings (serverUrl, token, disabled\_plugins, hotkeyMode, tray state) — read/written by main process; (C) \`.env.local\` (dev-only) for environment overrides. Server reads (A) primarily; renderer reads (B) via IPC. Hotkey registration and microphone state are stored in (B). Audio playback mode has a legacy fallback chain: \`audio\_playback\_mode\` → \`pause\_playback\_while\_recording\` → \`audio\_ducking\_enabled\`. Always use canonical resolvers (e.g. \`freestyleCloudUrl()\`) rather than direct env reads. Confidence: 1.0 — this architecture is referenced in 17+ settings-related entries and verified in code.

<!-- lore:019fb96f-4111-7cf0-a18d-58a27ae5d928 -->
* **Sound settings: mute toggle + volume control in general settings**: Added sound assets (record-start, record-stop, ready) and a new Sound section in General settings with mute toggle and volume slider. Sounds play on hotkey press (start), successful recording completion (stop), and when the voice model is ready. Mute state and volume are persisted via the existing settings API (\`PUT /api/settings/:key\`). The initializing state shows a pulsing animation in the pill while waiting for mic access. All sound-related state is stored in component state and synced to localStorage via \`usePersistentState\` for resilience across reloads.

<!-- lore:019fb9c5-c7fd-74c5-a53f-d9d11511d709 -->
* **Streaming audio experimental flag and UI toggle**: User preference: enable experimental streaming audio via a Settings toggle. Streaming audio connects desktop → cloud \`/v2/stream\` WebSocket (Soniox provider) for real-time transcription with lower latency. Add \`streaming\_audio\` setting (\`true\`/\`false\`, default \`false\`) in \`SETTINGS\_KEYS\`. Add an Experimental panel in Settings with a toggle labeled "Streaming Audio (Experimental)" visible only when voice provider is Freestyle Cloud. Desktop server must gate the \`/stream\` route behind this setting. Cloud repo requires no changes.

<!-- lore:019fb9c5-c831-7094-9e0b-dc06f894816b -->
* **Streaming audio: desktop ↔ cloud architecture and gating**: Architecture: When \`streaming\_audio\` is true and the voice provider supports streaming, the desktop opens a WebSocket to the local server's \`/stream\` route. The local server upgrades the connection, proxies audio frames to the appropriate provider (Soniox via cloud DO \`/v2/stream\` for freestyle-cloud; direct WS for BYOK providers like OpenAI Realtime, Deepgram, ElevenLabs), runs post-processing (Groq LLM), and delivers cleaned text back. The \`/stream\` route is gated server-side by the setting and client-side by provider capability (\`supportsStreaming\`). Latency improvements expected: 3–4s → 2–3s for long dictations. No changes to cloud repo or BYOK batch providers.

<!-- lore:019fb9ae-31e3-73d5-8159-efe935fe5cb0 -->
* **Tailwind IntelliSense: leave advisory hints as-is**: The repo's linter (Biome) reports zero issues; all Tailwind "can be written as" hints are IntelliSense advisories only and are pre-existing. No CI enforcement. Do not reformat classes to satisfy editor hints (e.g. \`\[scrollbar-gutter:stable]\` → \`scrollbar-gutter-stable\`). If a hint references an arbitrary value that has no Tailwind utility in the project's config, do not change it — it could silently break behavior. Leave them as-is.

<!-- lore:019fb9a9-d8ab-723d-b872-026a3d97c873 -->
* **Tone page off banner with Models + Use Cloud buttons**: Tone Cleanup tab (and all tone tabs) show a banner when \`llmCleanup\` is false. Banner contains: (1) "Go to Models" button that navigates to \`/models\` and opens the Models page; (2) "Use Freestyle Cloud" button (only when signed in) that calls \`ensureCloudAuth()\` → sets cloud as cleanup model → \`saveSetting('llm\_cleanup', 'true')\` → reloads. Tone cards are dimmed/disabled when the banner is shown. Implemented in \`TonePage\` with a new \`CleanupDisabledBanner\` component; \`selectCleanupMode\` no longer writes \`llm\_cleanup\`. All strings go through \`t()\` for i18n.

<!-- lore:019fb9a9-1787-7b58-8d46-ffc06f8f2243 -->
* **Tone tab redesign: selector row + raw→result preview**: Redesigned the Tone page: the 5 cleanup intensities (Off/Low/Medium/High/Custom) are now a horizontal selector row at the top with an olive active-marker and hover grow animation. Below, a full-width comparison shows "Raw transcript" above the "What lands" result for every destination (personal/work/email/everythingElse), enabling side-by-side inspection. The Custom tab swaps the comparison area for a full-width prompt editor (label + reset + hint + mono textarea + save). Built in \`tone.tsx\` and wired per-destination raw samples from \`template.json\` + \`en.json\`. Typecheck passes; build succeeds.

<!-- lore:019fb9aa-c26e-7fc5-b168-c29afd01177c -->
* **UI polish: streamline model setup and error states**: Improve visuals and flows: ensure error banners use \`AlertDialog\` with clear actions; make download error buttons consistent (Open / Retry / Cancel). Add skeleton loaders for model lists; ensure focus management when errors appear. Align spacing and typography with DESIGN.md tokens. Onboarding model setup: keep single-card layout but ensure progress ring and status text are readable. Ensure dark/light themes pass contrast. No new components beyond shadcn primitives.

<!-- lore:019fb97d-013a-7347-8e84-c017f349dd27 -->
* **Update DESIGN.md to reference shadcn components**: Rewrite DESIGN.md §6 to list component recipes mapped to shadcn components and variants. Remove raw HTML recipe examples. Keep token values (colors, radii, spacing) as the contract each component must satisfy.

<!-- lore:019fb997-fe55-7d44-826c-f35ec21e59df -->
* **Update existing entries to reflect dev- prefix architecture**: Update entries for view-manager.ts, plugins.tsx, plugin-detail.tsx, and loader behavior to reflect the \`dev-\` prefix convention and the updated linking/unlinking flow. Mark the \`plugins\` setting as explicit allowlist and the dev wrapper as a local discovery artifact. Confidence: 0.9

<!-- lore:019ef7a6-39c6-7a2d-b9d4-ed17c7101d67 -->
* **Use Craft-compatible commit message formatting**: Commit messages should always be written in a clean, proper format that Craft can parse and display correctly. Prefer concise conventional-style subjects (e.g. , , ) or the repository's established equivalent; avoid vague one-word subjects, noisy bot/AI labels, and malformed punctuation. When writing squash merge titles, PR titles, or normal commits, make the subject descriptive and Craft-friendly.

<!-- lore:019fb9a8-3030-7836-8ab8-0e233870e3c3 -->
* **Use shadcn components and design tokens**: Prefer Radix-based shadcn components (Button, Input, Select, Switch, Card, Dialog, etc.) over hand-rolled primitives. Match component variants and tokens defined in DESIGN.md; when adding new component usage, update DESIGN.md with the component→variant mapping. Ensure focus styles, aria labels, and contrast align with the design system.

<!-- lore:019fb9a9-17a8-7385-904b-30d5eb9ad552 -->
* **usePersistentState hook for tab persistence**: Added \`usePersistentState\<T extends string>\` hook that persists a string-union value in localStorage with validation against an \`isValid\` predicate (falls back safely on invalid/stale values). Used to remember the active tab in Settings, Tone, and Plugins pages. Wraps \`useState\`, reads/writes via \`localStorage\`, and is guarded against private-mode or quota failures. Integrated into \`tone.tsx\`, \`plugins.tsx\`, and \`use-persistent-state.ts\`. Typecheck passes.

<!-- lore:019fb9ae-e073-7193-b359-a4fdc9238ca0 -->
* **User preference: enable cloud sync by default, with opt-out toggle**: Preference: enable cloud sync by default for logged-in users, but provide an explicit opt-out toggle in Settings > Cloud Sync. When disabled, block writes to cloud but allow reads (so local-first behavior is preserved). Persist the opt-out in settings.json (not SQLite) so it's available before the server is reachable. UI: simple Card with description, toggle, and status indicator (last-synced timestamp).

<!-- lore:019fb9c5-46b7-753e-9617-d09929eb3608 -->
* **User preference: update cloud cleanup presets to desktop parity**: User requested a PR to fix missing list handling in Freestyle Cloud post-processing prompts. The cloud cleanup-presets.ts was updated to byte-for-byte parity with desktop's canonical LOW/MEDIUM/HIGH presets (including list formatting, guardrails, and retraction rules). This closes a drift gap where desktop improvements were never ported back to cloud. All three presets now match desktop; build/typecheck/lint pass. Confidence: 1.0 — this is a direct implementation of the user's requested PR.
